Scope: all 199 screenshots in this batch have a source mapping. Repeated questions may share a group. S001–S199 are organizational IDs, not test item numbers or guaranteed upload order.

答案边界：本手册是根据题干独立推导的教学解析，不是核验过的官方答案册。标为待核实的题不提供确定答案；请查看该题说明。

Answer status: solutions are independently inferred from the supplied material, not verified official answer keys. Unconfirmed cards explain the remaining ambiguity rather than assert certainty.

错因边界：截图通常未显示你的错误选项，因此这里总结常见、可能错因，不认定你本人一定犯了这些错误。知识点出现次数也不等于你的错误频率。

Error attribution: the material generally does not show your chosen wrong answer. Traps are possible patterns, not diagnoses of your personal reasoning or error frequency.

Sources: uploaded questions are concisely paraphrased and traced to filenames. Literary attributions follow the supplied images. No College Board test, module, or item ID is invented. Added practice is original; raw screenshots, OCR transcripts, and local paths are not published.

01

语境词汇Words in context

27 题组 / question groups
知识点总则 / Core principles

先在空格写出中文或英文预测词，再核对选项。追踪转折、否定、程度和观点归属；用完整动作场景判断多义词。‘利用’不一定带贬义，‘通行’也不一定是‘文章段落’。

Predict a meaning before checking choices. Track contrast, negatives, degree, and whose claim is reported. Let the complete scene determine a word's sense rather than its most familiar dictionary meaning.

常见错因 / Common traps

只认识词的常见义；忽略not、but或被否定的claims；把补救动作理解成加剧问题。

Default dictionary meanings; missed negation or contrast; confusing a reported claim with endorsement; reversing a corrective action.

S001独立推导 / Inferred

单细胞生物与基本行为Single cells and basic behavior

知识点：语境反义 · 否定范围
Skills: Contrast in context · Scope of negation
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

草履虫类原生生物会在策略失效时换策略，因此不只具有哪类行为？

A protozoan changes strategies when one fails. Its behavior is not limited to what level?

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
B — rudimentary
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

not limited to 表示超越某一层次；sophisticated 与 rudimentary（基本、初级）构成对照。不是说这些行为没有优势，也不是讨论攻击性。

Not limited to signals behavior beyond a baseline. Sophisticated contrasts with rudimentary, meaning basic or elementary, not with advantageous or aggressive.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

只看一个生物学名词猜词，忽略 not 和 sophisticated 的关系。

Guessing from the scientific topic instead of tracking the negative and the contrast.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

The device solves complex problems; it is not limited to ___ tasks. 填 basic 还是 beneficial？

The device solves complex problems; it is not limited to ___ tasks. Choose basic or beneficial.

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

basic。复杂与基本形成层级对照；beneficial 意为有益，不是复杂的反义。

Basic. Complexity contrasts with simplicity, not with usefulness.

练习出处 / Practice source: 本手册原创，基于 S001 的考点；不是官方原题。 / Created for this guide from the linked skill; not an official question.

S002独立推导 / Inferred

总体遵守与局部例外General adherence with an exception

知识点：转折预测 · 整体与局部
Skills: Predicting from contrast · General patterns and exceptions
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

铸造行会大体沿用传统，但不严格遵循每一个细节，例如改用电机。

A casting guild generally follows tradition but replaces one traditional tool with a motor.

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
B — adhere to
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

后半句 but they don't strictly observe every tradition 限制的是遵守的程度，暗示前半句总体仍遵守。adhere to = follow；experiment with 和 improve on 不是必然结论。

But they don't strictly observe every tradition limits the extent of adherence. Adhere to means follow; experimenting or improving is not the central claim.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

见到 but 就认为前半句必须是彻底拒绝传统。

Treating but as a reversal of every part of the preceding claim.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

The team generally ___ the protocol, although it adapts one step. 填 adheres to / rejects。

The team generally ___ the protocol, although it adapts one step. Choose adheres to or rejects.

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

adheres to：通常遵守，但有一个例外。

Adheres to: broad compliance allows a specific exception.

S013独立推导 / Inferred

法律的执行Implementing laws

知识点：动作释义 · 语境搭配
Skills: Meaning inferred from actions · Collocation in context
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

议会制定法律，行政官通过政策和服务落实法律。

A council makes laws; executive officials put them into effect through policies and services.

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
A — implement
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

分工线索是制定与执行。implement = put into effect。后面的by引出执行的具体方式，而不是重新立法或仅评价法律。

The roles contrast lawmaking with implementation. The by-phrase describes how officials put existing laws into effect.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

忽略by后的动作，把所有政府行为一概理解为制定。

Ignoring the by-phrase and treating all governmental action as lawmaking.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

The council approved the rule; staff will ___ it by updating procedures. 填implement / invent。

The council approved the rule; staff will ___ it by updating procedures. Choose implement or invent.

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

implement：规则已批准，现在将其落实。

Implement: the approved rule is now put into practice.

练习出处 / Practice source: 本手册原创，基于 S013 的考点；不是官方原题。 / Created for this guide from the linked skill; not an official question.
原题出处 / Uploaded source: 用户提供截图 S013 · Reading and Writing: Question 4。正式试卷、模块与官方题库ID未核实。 / Test, module, and official item IDs are unverified.
S014独立推导 / Inferred

黑天鹅家庭的灵活性Flexibility in swan family structures

知识点：比喻义 · 例子归纳
Skills: Figurative meaning · Generalizing from examples
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

黑天鹅存在代养及不同配对方式，显示家庭结构的什么特征？

Fostering and different parental pairings illustrate what property of family structure?

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
B — elasticity
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

这些例子共同指向可变、可调整的结构。elasticity在此是灵活性，不是物理拉伸；代养并不证明双方互惠，不能选reciprocity。

The examples establish adaptable arrangements. Elasticity is figurative flexibility; fostering alone does not establish reciprocal exchange.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

看到照顾幼鸟就凭情感选affection，未概括结构变化。

Choosing affection because care is involved, instead of describing structural flexibility.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

A work schedule allows shifts to be exchanged and hours rearranged. Which fits: elasticity or hostility？

A schedule allows shifts and hours to be rearranged. Choose elasticity or hostility.

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

elasticity：可调整的灵活性。

Elasticity: adaptability rather than hostility.

练习出处 / Practice source: 本手册原创，基于 S014 的考点；不是官方原题。 / Created for this guide from the linked skill; not an official question.

S016独立推导 / Inferred

壁画：表面朴素，意义复杂A mural: modest appearance, complex meaning

知识点：转折 · 表象与实质
Skills: Contrast · Appearance versus substance
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

咖啡馆里三人喝咖啡的壁画听起来并不宏大，但实际上有复杂的社会意义。

A coffee-shop scene sounds modest, but the mural actually explores complex social themes.

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
A — unassuming
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

空格概括表面印象，but之后揭示真正复杂性。unassuming意为不张扬、看似朴素。pretentious则是故作高深，与前面的‘不显得雄心勃勃’不符。

The blank describes the initial modest impression. Unassuming fits; pretentious suggests affected importance, contradicting the stated lack of apparent ambition.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

把后半句的complex直接填进前半句，忽视表象与实质的反差。

Filling the blank from the later complexity while ignoring the contrast.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

The ___ shop looks plain, but its collection is extraordinary. 填unassuming / extravagant。

The ___ shop looks plain, but its collection is extraordinary. Choose unassuming or extravagant.

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

unassuming：外观朴素并不排除内容出众。

Unassuming fits the plain exterior and the contrasting exceptional collection.

练习出处 / Practice source: 本手册原创，基于 S016 的考点；不是官方原题。 / Created for this guide from the linked skill; not an official question.

S025独立推导 / Inferred

研究失衡：纠正而非加剧Redressing an imbalance

知识点：问题与解决 · 词义方向
Skills: Problem and solution · Semantic direction
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

研究长期偏重年轻人，如今纳入老年人，是在如何处理失衡？

Research overemphasized younger people; including seniors now does what to the imbalance?

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
B — Redressing
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

扩大样本是在纠正既有偏差，redress = remedy/correct。exacerbate是加剧，epitomize是成为典型，preclude是事先排除；都不符合补救动作。

Broader inclusion remedies the bias: redress. Exacerbate worsens, epitomize exemplifies, and preclude prevents; none describes this corrective action.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

只因负面问题出现在句中，就选负面方向动词。

Choosing a negative-direction verb merely because a problem is mentioned.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

Adding rural schools to an urban-only study helps ___ the imbalance. 填redress / exacerbate。

Adding rural schools to an urban-only study helps ___ the imbalance. Choose redress or exacerbate.

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

redress：补充被忽略群体，纠正偏差。

Redress: including the omitted group corrects the imbalance.

练习出处 / Practice source: 本手册原创，基于 S025 的考点；不是官方原题。 / Created for this guide from the linked skill; not an official question.
原题出处 / Uploaded source: 用户提供截图 S025 · Reading and Writing: Question 3。正式试卷、模块与官方题库ID未核实。 / Test, module, and official item IDs are unverified.
S028独立推导 / Inferred

exploited的中性义The neutral sense of exploited

知识点：一词多义 · 语义色彩
Skills: Polysemy · Connotation
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

研究者利用金属磁场在受压时改变的性质来检测地下管道。

Researchers use stress-related magnetic changes to inspect underground pipes.

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
D — exploited
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

exploited在科学语境可意为utilized，利用已有性质，并非剥削。团队实际测量和利用现象，不是仅提出假说或重新定义。

In scientific contexts, exploited can neutrally mean utilized. The team uses the property, rather than merely hypothesizing or redefining it.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

固定套用‘剥削’这一负面义，排除合适答案。

Rejecting the word because of its negative sense in other contexts.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

Engineers exploited sunlight to heat water. exploited在此是‘利用’还是‘虐待’？

Engineers exploited sunlight to heat water. Does exploited mean utilized or mistreated?

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

利用；宾语和动作决定中性色彩。

Utilized; the object and action establish a neutral sense.

练习出处 / Practice source: 本手册原创，基于 S028 的考点；不是官方原题。 / Created for this guide from the linked skill; not an official question.

S029 · S043独立推导 / Inferred

Ibn Rushd：跟踪被否定的说法Ibn Rushd: tracking an undermined claim

知识点：让步结构 · 观点归属
Skills: Concessive structure · Attribution of claims
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

新研究削弱某种旧说，但作品传播地远离当时思想中心仍无可争议。

Recent research weakens an older claim, though geographic distance from the intellectual center is indisputable.

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
D — inconsequential to
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

距离远可解释旧说为何认为影响有限，因此空格为‘对其他哲学家不重要/影响甚微’。注意作者说该说法被削弱，并非作者自己断言毫无影响。

Distance is relevant to an older claim of limited influence: inconsequential to. The text reports that claim as undermined, not as the author's settled conclusion.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

把claims中的他人观点，误读成作者最终立场。

Mistaking a reported, weakened claim for the author's own position.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

Research challenges claims that the book was irrelevant. 作者是否认为它无关紧要？

Research challenges claims that the book was irrelevant. Does the author endorse irrelevance?

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

不；被挑战的是‘无关紧要’这个说法。

No. Irrelevance is the claim being challenged.

练习出处 / Practice source: 本手册原创，基于 S029 · S043 的考点；不是官方原题。 / Created for this guide from the linked skill; not an official question.

S030独立推导 / Inferred

样本偏差的改善Ameliorating sampling bias

知识点：问题与解决 · 抽样代表性
Skills: Problem and solution · Sample representativeness
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

研究只使用本校学生，却推广到全人类；招募多背景受试者旨在改善问题。

Studies generalize from local students; recruiting diverse participants aims to improve this problem.

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
B — ameliorate
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

ameliorate = make better。招募更多样本背景是补救策略；sanction为批准，rationalize为辩解，postulate为假定，均不是改善。多样化本身也不保证完全代表总体。

Ameliorate means improve. Diverse recruitment is a remedy, not approval, justification, or postulation; it still does not automatically guarantee full representativeness.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

只记词的大概熟悉感，未按问题—解决方向筛选。

Choosing a familiar word without checking the problem–solution direction.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

The city repaired leaks to ___ the water shortage. 填ameliorate / rationalize。

The city repaired leaks to ___ the water shortage. Choose ameliorate or rationalize.

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

ameliorate：修漏可缓解问题，而非为问题找理由。

Ameliorate: repair mitigates the problem rather than rationalizing it.

练习出处 / Practice source: 本手册原创，基于 S030 的考点；不是官方原题。 / Created for this guide from the linked skill; not an official question.

S040 · S042独立推导 / Inferred

disputing：阻挡通行，而非争论Disputing: resisting passage, not arguing

知识点：一词多义 · 动作场景 · 宾语线索
Skills: Polysemy · Physical action in context · Object-based clues
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

Giovanni's Room中，出租车穿过满是行人、手推车与篮子的街道，众人disputing our passage。

In Giovanni's Room, a taxi moves through a street crowded with people, carts, and baskets that are disputing its passage.

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
D — Providing resistance to
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

这里passage指通行，不是文章段落；disputing表示对前进形成阻力。场景是物理拥挤，不是双方用语言争辩。可将短语理解为‘处处挡着我们的去路’。

Passage means movement through the street, not a text excerpt. Disputing means physically resisting that movement; the crowd is obstructing, not verbally debating.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

固定把dispute译为争论，又把passage认成文章，导致整个场景错位。

Applying dictionary defaults for dispute and passage instead of the physical scene.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

Fallen branches disputed our progress along the path. branches如何影响progress？

Fallen branches disputed our progress along the path. What are the branches doing?

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

原创句中表示树枝阻碍前进，不是树枝在争论。

In this original sentence, they obstruct progress rather than argue.

练习出处 / Practice source: 本手册原创，基于 S040 · S042 的考点；不是官方原题。 / Created for this guide from the linked skill; not an official question.

S058独立推导 / Inferred

manifest in：抽象观点的可见体现Manifest in: visible evidence of a stance

知识点：抽象与具体 · 搭配
Skills: Abstract claims and concrete evidence · Collocations
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

Morris反工业化的态度体现在手工材料与中世纪式装饰中。

Morris's opposition to industrialization is visible in handmade materials and medieval-style ornament.

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
B — manifest in
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

manifest in = evident in / expressed in。冒号后的细节是态度如何显现的证据，不是被材料审查或因材料而复杂化。

Manifest in means evident or expressed in. The handmade details demonstrate the stance rather than scrutinize or complicate it.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

认识manifest的别的词性，却未判断is后需要的短语含义。

Recalling another use of manifest without checking the phrase after is.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

Her commitment to reuse is ___ her recycled sculptures. 填manifest in / insensible to。

Her commitment to reuse is ___ her recycled sculptures. Choose manifest in or insensible to.

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

manifest in：再生材料让这一理念具体可见。

Manifest in: recycled materials visibly express that commitment.

S061独立推导 / Inferred

历史记忆：tentative而非permanentHistorical memory: tentative versus permanent

知识点：比较结构 · 语境反义
Skills: Comparative constructions · Contrast in context
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

Pennington贡献很大，但其历史记忆地位可能不如将被长久铭记的Kwolek稳固。

Pennington made major contributions, but her place in historical memory may be less secure than Kwolek's lasting recognition.

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
B — tentative
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

后句will long be remembered构成比较锚点；前句more tentative表示更不稳固、更未确定。permanent和prominent方向相反，warranted则讨论是否应得，不是记忆稳固程度。

Will long be remembered anchors the contrast. More tentative means less secure or established, not more permanent, prominent, or deserved.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

把贡献大小直接等同于公众记忆是否牢固。

Equating substantial contributions with guaranteed lasting recognition.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

Her rival's reputation is secure; hers remains ___. 填tentative / permanent。

Her rival's reputation is secure; hers remains ___. Choose tentative or permanent.

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

tentative：与secure形成对照。

Tentative contrasts with secure.

S079独立推导 / Inferred

伽马暴：偏离典型值A gamma-ray burst: an unusual duration

知识点：典型与异常 · 数量语境
Skills: Typical versus unusual cases · Numerical context
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

这次中子星合并相关暴发持续200秒，而同类通常少于2秒。

A neutron-merger burst lasts 200 seconds, whereas such bursts usually last under 2 seconds.

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
D — an oddity
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

200秒相对于典型时长极不寻常，所以oddity（异乎寻常之事）最准确。incident只说发生了一件事，未表达偏离常态；reprieve是缓解或暂缓。

Oddity captures the departure from the stated norm. Incident merely names an event; reprieve means temporary relief or postponement.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

只选语法可放入空格的词，未表达数字形成的异常关系。

Accepting a grammatically possible word that misses the numerical contrast.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

通常花期3天，一株持续30天。称它an oddity还是a routine event？

Flowers usually last 3 days; one lasts 30. Is it an oddity or a routine event?

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

an oddity，显著偏离通常时长。

An oddity: it departs markedly from the usual duration.

S082独立推导 / Inferred

Franklin：受欢迎带来的尊重Franklin: esteem and popularity

知识点：近义衔接 · 正面社会评价
Skills: Synonym-based cohesion · Positive social regard
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

Franklin在法国很受欢迎，这种社会评价有助于争取支持。

Franklin's popularity in France helped him win support.

B — esteem
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

esteem指尊重、推崇，与popular形成衔接。thoughtfulness和sincerity是性格品质，文本没直接建立；controversy也不等于受欢迎。

Esteem means respect or high regard and follows from the popularity context. Thoughtfulness and sincerity add unstated traits; controversy is not equivalent to popularity.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

凭名人印象补出性格，而不找上下文近义关系。

Importing presumed personality traits instead of using textual linkage.

Esteem links to the positive regard expressed by admired.

S097独立推导 / Inferred

Harjo：拒绝旧刻板呈现Harjo: repudiating an old portrayal

知识点：后文释义 · 观点动词
Skills: Definitions supplied later · Verbs of stance
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

导演用当代衣着与俚语描写原住民青少年，反对电视总把原住民放在遥远过去。

Contemporary clothes and slang reject television's tendency to portray Native people only in the distant past.

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
A — repudiates
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

this rejection直接解释空格，repudiates意为拒绝、否定。recants通常指撤回自己先前的主张，而非反对行业普遍做法。

This rejection defines the blank: repudiates. Recants typically withdraws one's own prior position, not a broader industry's practice.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

只看两个词都有否定色彩，忽视否定对象是否自己的旧观点。

Treating all rejection-related words as interchangeable regardless of what is rejected.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

She rejects an industry's stereotype, not her own earlier statement. 用repudiates还是recants？

She rejects an industry's stereotype, not her own earlier statement. Choose repudiates or recants.

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

repudiates；recants需要撤回原先自己持有的立场。

Repudiates; recants would suggest retracting her own earlier position.

S199独立推导 / Inferred

surmised：被数据推翻的推测Surmised: an assumption overturned by data

知识点：近义复现 · 证据状态
Skills: Synonymous restatement · Evidence status
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

历史学家曾认为大家庭人均食品支出较低，后来的细查数据推翻这个假设。

Historians assumed larger households spent less on food per person; closer data analysis disproved the supposition.

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
A — surmised
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

they assumed与this supposition都提示空格是推测、假定，surmised正好对应。questioned会变成他们质疑该观点，逆转了原有立场；contrived暗示刻意编造，文中没有动机证据。

Assumed and supposition identify a conjecture: surmised. Questioned reverses their stated position; contrived adds unsupported deliberate fabrication.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

因为该观点最后被推翻，就误认为最初历史学家也在质疑它。

Letting the later refutation overwrite the historians' earlier belief.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

They ___ that the route was shorter, but later measurements disproved this assumption. 填surmised / demonstrated。

They ___ that the route was shorter, but later measurements disproved this assumption. Choose surmised or demonstrated.

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

surmised：前面只是推测，并非已被证实。

Surmised: the initial position was a conjecture, not a demonstration.

S101独立推导 / Inferred

休眠基因与语境释义Dormant genes and contextual definitions

知识点：冒号后的释义线索 · 存在但不活跃
Skills: Definition clues after a colon · Presence versus inactivity
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

人类可能拥有某些冬眠基因，但这些基因几乎不影响身体过程；选最准确的形容词。

Choose the adjective for hibernation genes that may exist in humans yet have essentially no bodily effect.

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
D — dormant
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

独立推导：冒号后说明基因“存在但基本不起作用”，对应 dormant（休眠的、未激活的）。lacking 表示不存在，直接矛盾；decisive 和 variable 都不能表达未激活。

Independent derivation: The definition after the colon—present but essentially inactive—matches dormant. Lacking contradicts presence; decisive and variable do not express inactivity.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

常见错因是把“不发挥作用”误当“不存在”；本题明确区分存在状态和活跃程度。

A common trap is equating inactivity with absence; the sentence explicitly distinguishes them.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

填词：该病毒仍存在于体内，但多年未活跃，因此处于____状态。

Fill in: The virus remains in the body but has been inactive for years; it is ____.

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

dormant：仍然存在但不活跃。

Dormant: still present but inactive.

S102独立推导 / Inferred

帝国边缘位置Peripheral locations

知识点：因果语境选词 · 空间关系
Skills: Word choice from causal context · Spatial relationships
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

Xoconochco 距阿兹特克首都数百公里，货物需长途陆运；其位置应如何描述？

Xoconochco lay hundreds of kilometers from the Aztec capital, requiring long overland transport. Describe its location.

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
D — peripheral
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

独立推导：远离首都、运输漫长说明位于帝国边缘，即 peripheral。unobtrusive 是不引人注意，concealed 是被隐藏，approximate 是大概的，均非距离关系。

Independent derivation: Distance from the capital and lengthy transport indicate a peripheral, or outlying, location. The other choices mean inconspicuous, hidden, or approximate, not geographically marginal.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

常见错因是凭偏僻联想到“隐蔽”，而忽视题干强调空间距离。

A common trap is associating remoteness with concealment instead of following the geographic evidence.

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

peripheral：位于外围，不表示被隐藏。

Peripheral: situated on the outskirts, not necessarily hidden.

S127独立推导 / Inferred

来源与命题真假的逻辑区分Separating a claim's source from its truth

知识点：释义对应 · 逻辑相关性
Skills: Matching contextual definitions · Logical relevance
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

严格逻辑上，主张的来源对其真假没有直接影响；填形容词。

From a strictly logical standpoint, a claim's source has no direct bearing on whether it is true. Supply the adjective.

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
B — irrelevant
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

独立推导：冒号后的 no direct bearing 是 irrelevant（不相关）的释义。indistinct 指模糊，indisputable 指无可争辩，implicit 指含蓄，都不是逻辑无关。

Independent derivation: No direct bearing defines irrelevant. Indistinct means unclear, indisputable means beyond dispute, and implicit means unstated; none expresses logical irrelevance.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

常见错因是根据对来源的不信任判断内容真假，而非理解句中限定的严格逻辑关系。

A common trap is importing distrust of the source instead of following the stated logical relationship.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

填词：演讲者的鞋码对算术证明是否正确没有影响，因此是____信息。

Fill in: The speaker's shoe size has no bearing on whether an arithmetic proof is valid; it is ____ information.

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

irrelevant。信息与待判断问题无关。

Irrelevant. The information does not bear on the issue being evaluated.

S136独立推导 / Inferred

同时开花与同步Simultaneous flowering and synchronization

知识点：同义线索 · 行为时间一致
Skills: Synonym clues · Alignment in the timing of actions
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

菟丝子吸收宿主开花前产生的蛋白质，从而与宿主同时开花；填名词。

Dodder uses a host protein to flower at the same time as its host. Name this coordination.

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
A — synchronization
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

独立推导：at the same time 对应 synchronization（同步）。hibernation 是冬眠；prediction 是预测；moderation 是适度控制，均不表示两者时间一致。

Independent derivation: At the same time defines synchronization. Hibernation, prediction, and moderation do not mean coordinating the timing of two events.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

常见错因是把植物利用提前信号误认为重点在预测；题目要命名实际同时开花。

A common trap is focusing on advance signals and choosing prediction rather than naming the coordinated timing.

Fill in: Adjusting two clocks to show the same time is called ____.

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

synchronization。核心是时间一致。

Synchronization. The defining feature is aligned timing.

S144独立推导 / Inferred

科学悖论中的阻碍关系An inhibiting relationship within a paradox

知识点：让步逻辑 · 反义方向
Skills: Concessive logic · Contrasting semantic directions
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

虽然水对生命反应至关重要，但早期地球的水可能怎样影响蛋白质等生物分子的形成，才构成悖论？

Although water is essential for life, its presence on early Earth may have done what to biomolecule formation, creating a paradox?

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
B — hampered
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

独立推导：although 与 paradox 要求后半句呈现相反作用：水反而妨碍形成，hampered 最准确。reflected、imitated、exploited 均不表达阻碍。

Independent derivation: Although and paradox require a contrasting effect: water hindered formation, matching hampered. Reflected, imitated, and exploited do not express inhibition.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

常见错因是凭“水有利于生命”的常识选正向词，而忽视悖论信号。

A common trap is following the familiar beneficial role of water instead of the explicit paradox.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

填词：虽然道路旨在促进运输，长期施工却____了交通。

Fill in: Although the road was intended to improve transport, prolonged construction ____ traffic.

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

hampered（阻碍）。让步结构要求实际结果与目标相反。

Hampered. The concessive structure contrasts the intended benefit with actual obstruction.

S150独立推导 / Inferred

大量广布与 ubiquitous 词族Abundance, distribution, and ubiquity

知识点：数量与分布语境 · 精确词义
Skills: Contextual clues about quantity and distribution · Precise word meanings
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

许多北非遗址发现大量贝壳珠；其广泛出现被归因于象征行为传播和贝壳易获得。填名词。

Numerous North African sites contain many shell beads; their widespread presence is attributed to spreading symbolic behavior and readily available shells. Choose the noun.

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
B — ubiquity
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

独立推导：many sites、plethora、widespread availability 共同指向 ubiquity（无处不在、广泛存在）。heterogeneity 指多样性，expediency 指权宜性，fecundity 指繁殖力，都不是遗物的分布广度。

Independent derivation: Many sites, plentiful beads, and widespread shell availability point to ubiquity. Heterogeneity concerns diversity, expediency convenience, and fecundity reproductive productivity rather than artifacts' widespread presence.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

常见错因是把“数量多、分布广”误当“类型多样”或生物繁殖多。

A common trap is confusing widespread abundance with diversity or reproductive capacity.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

填词：从学校到车站，各处都能看到二维码；它们的____改变了支付方式。

Fill in: QR codes appear everywhere, from schools to stations; their ____ has changed payment habits.

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

ubiquity，强调普遍可见，不是类型多样。

Ubiquity, emphasizing widespread presence rather than variety.

S164独立推导 / Inferred

制冷剂回收与排放对立Recovering refrigerant rather than releasing it

知识点：同位解释线索 · rather than 对比
Skills: Restatement clues · Contrast signaled by rather than
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

维修时必须从制冷系统收集R-22而不放空，以便后续处置或再生。填最精确动词。

During repairs, technicians must collect R-22 from cooling systems instead of venting it, allowing disposal or reclamation later. Choose the verb.

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
D — recover
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

独立推导：careful collection process 与 rather than venting 指向 recover（回收）。release 恰好是禁止的释放；measure 只是测量，shield 是保护，均非收集回收。截图标示答案不作为官方证明。

Independent derivation: Careful collection and rather than venting identify recover. Release reverses the requirement; measure and shield do not describe collection. Any displayed key is not treated as official verification.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

常见错因是把从系统中移出理解成直接排放，忽略必须收集而不是放空。

A common trap is equating removal from a system with release into the atmosphere.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

填词：实验后，技术员____溶剂以便循环使用，而不是倒掉。

Fill in: After the experiment, technicians ____ the solvent for reuse rather than discard it.

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

recover。表示收回可继续处理或再用的物质。

Recover. It means collecting material so it can be processed or reused.

S175独立推导 / Inferred

consult 的非人宾语词义Consult with an inanimate object

知识点：语境词义 · 动词与宾语搭配
Skills: Meaning in context · Verb-object combinations
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

Peter Pan 查看船上海图后估算抵达日期；consulting 在四个选项中最接近什么？

Peter Pan estimates an arrival date after consulting the ship's chart. Which option best captures consulting?

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
D — Considering
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

独立推导：宾语是海图，consulting 意为查看、参考其信息；给定选项中 Considering 最接近。Advising 是向别人提建议，Thwarting 是阻挠，Persuading 是说服，都不适用于查看海图。

Independent derivation: With a chart as its object, consulting means examining or referring to its information; Considering is the closest supplied option. Advising, thwarting, and persuading do not describe reading a chart.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

常见错因是只记 consult 的“咨询某人”，未依据物体宾语调整为“查阅”。

A common trap is knowing only consult a person and missing the reference-source sense.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

“Before defining the word, she consulted a dictionary.”这里 consulted 是什么含义？

Before defining the word, she consulted a dictionary. What does consulted mean here?

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

查阅字典、参考其中信息，不是给字典建议。

Looked up or referred to information in the dictionary, not gave it advice.

练习出处 / Practice source: 本手册原创，基于 S175 的考点；不是官方原题。 / Created for this guide from the linked skill; not an official question.

S181独立推导 / Inferred

count 的认为义Count meaning deem

知识点：熟词生义 · 上下文替换
Skills: Less familiar senses of common words · Testing substitutions in context
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

Henry James 说威尼斯不如其他地方那样容易生活，其中 as you count living in other places 的 count 接近何义？

Henry James contrasts living in Venice with living elsewhere, using as you count living in other places. What does count mean?

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
D — Deem
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

独立推导：这里讨论你如何理解、看待其他地方的生活方式，count 接近 deem（认为、视为）。Enumerate 是列举数数；Signify 是表示；Establish 是建立，都无法替换这一判断义。

Independent derivation: Count here concerns what one regards or considers living elsewhere to be, so Deem is closest. Enumerate means list or count numerically, Signify mean, and Establish create or confirm.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

常见错因是见 count 就选数数义，而未试着用选项替换原词检查整句意思。

A common trap is defaulting to numerical counting without testing the replacement in context.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

“I count her among my closest friends.”此处 count 是“数出”还是“视为、列入”？

I count her among my closest friends. Does count mean enumerate or regard/include here?

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

视为、列入：把她看作最亲近朋友之一。

Regard/include: the speaker considers her one of their closest friends.

练习出处 / Practice source: 本手册原创，基于 S181 的考点；不是官方原题。 / Created for this guide from the linked skill; not an official question.
原题出处 / Uploaded source: 用户提供截图 S181 · The following text is adapted from a 1909 republication of Henry James' s 1882 travel essay。正式试卷、模块与官方题库ID未核实。 / Test, module, and official item IDs are unverified.
S184独立推导 / Inferred

颜料使用由耐久性驱动Durability as a motivation for pigment use

知识点：因果词义 · as much by...as by...
Skills: Causal word meanings · The as much by...as by... construction
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

Vermeer 大量使用昂贵群青，原因既可能是独特色泽，也可能是化学稳定、不易降解。填动词。

Vermeer's extensive use of expensive ultramarine may reflect its chemical durability as much as its exclusivity and vivid color. Choose the verb.

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
B — motivated
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

独立推导：后文不易降解是选择该颜料的动因，was likely motivated by 最合适。constrained 是限制，preempted 是预先阻止，expedited 是加速，均不表达采用原因。

Independent derivation: Resistance to degradation is a reason for choosing the pigment, so motivated by is appropriate. Constrained, preempted, and expedited mean limited, forestalled, and accelerated, not driven by a reason.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

常见错因是被“昂贵”吸引，认为一定受成本限制，而题干解释的是为什么愿意使用。

A common trap is focusing on expense and assuming constraint, although the sentence explains why the pigment was chosen.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

填词：她选择耐磨地板，这一决定主要受到其长寿命的____。用 motivated 构成英文句。

Complete using motivated: Her decision to choose durable flooring was ____ by its long lifespan.

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

motivated。长寿命是驱动决定的原因。

Motivated. Long lifespan is the reason driving the decision.

练习出处 / Practice source: 本手册原创，基于 S184 的考点；不是官方原题。 / Created for this guide from the linked skill; not an official question.

S185独立推导 / Inferred

早期迹象与 incipientEarly signs and incipient

知识点：时间阶段线索 · 同义复现
Skills: Clues about developmental stages · Repeated meaning through synonyms
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

叶绿素荧光遥感能发现植物压力的最初迹象，尚未体现在常规植被指数中。此时压力处于什么阶段？

Fluorescence sensors detect the first signs of plant stress before conventional vegetation indices reflect it. Describe the stress at that stage.

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
D — incipient
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

独立推导：first signs、not yet reflected 指刚开始的阶段，incipient。ambivalent 是态度矛盾，profuse 是大量的，attenuated 是减弱的；“尚早”不等于“原有压力已减弱”。

Independent derivation: First signs and not yet reflected point to incipient, meaning beginning to develop. Ambivalent concerns mixed attitudes, profuse abundance, and attenuated weakening; early development does not imply prior stress has diminished.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

常见错因是把早期不明显误读成减弱，混淆阶段与变化方向。

A common trap is confusing early, not-yet-obvious stress with stress that has become weaker.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

填词：裂缝刚刚形成，肉眼几乎看不见，传感器已检测到这种____损伤。

Fill in: The crack has only just begun and is barely visible, but sensors detect this ____ damage.

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

incipient（初现的），强调发展初期。

Incipient, emphasizing the earliest developmental stage.

练习出处 / Practice source: 本手册原创，基于 S185 的考点；不是官方原题。 / Created for this guide from the linked skill; not an official question.
原题出处 / Uploaded source: 用户提供截图 S185 · MetOpA and other data sets of plants' solar-induced chlorophyll fluorescence (a。正式试卷、模块与官方题库ID未核实。 / Test, module, and official item IDs are unverified.
02

主旨与句段功能Purpose and structure

20 题组 / question groups
知识点总则 / Core principles

主旨覆盖全文的研究对象、核心动作与总体发现。功能题则问一句话‘为什么放在这里’：它是在展开、让步、解释原因、反驳，还是提供例子？按文本实际顺序概括，不能补写未发生的修改假说或精度比较。

A main idea covers the subject, central action, and overall finding. A function question asks why a sentence is there: elaboration, concession, cause, challenge, or illustration. Describe the actual sequence without adding unstated research steps.

常见错因 / Common traps

把细节当主旨；只翻译划线句；凭常识补出研究者动机或下一步。

Promoting a detail to the thesis; paraphrasing instead of explaining function; inventing motives or later steps.

S031独立推导 / Inferred

土壤研究：观察反对假说Soil study: an observation conflicts with a hypothesis

知识点：篇章结构 · 未提及不补写
Skills: Text structure · Avoiding unstated additions
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

深层土壤是否仍受土地利用影响尚不清楚；研究预测有差异，但30厘米以下各类土壤水平相近。

Researchers ask whether land-use effects persist at depth, predict differences, but find similarly low levels below 30 cm.

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
B
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

结构是未解问题→假说→冲突观察。A多写了‘修正假说’，文中并未说明；C多写竞争假说；D把反证变成验证。

The sequence is question, hypothesis, conflicting observation. Revision is not stated; neither are competing hypotheses or validation.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

根据通常科学流程，擅自补上文中没有的研究步骤。

Adding an unstated step because it often occurs in scientific research.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

研究预测肥料让根变长，实测无变化；文本到此结束。能概括为‘研究者随后修改理论’吗？

A study predicts longer roots after fertilizer but finds no change; the text ends. Can its structure include a later theory revision?

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

不能；只可写结果不支持预测，修改步骤未给出。

No. The finding conflicts with the prediction; revision is unstated.

练习出处 / Practice source: 本手册原创，基于 S031 的考点；不是官方原题。 / Created for this guide from the linked skill; not an official question.

S032 · S047独立推导 / Inferred

Israel Potter：性格与情节功能Israel Potter: a trait's role in the plot

知识点：句段功能 · 性格与结果
Skills: Rhetorical function · Traits and plot outcomes
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

Israel本想用工钱获得土地，却被雇主违约；温和、耐心的性格帮助解释他为何接受挫折。

Israel loses promised land; his patience and mildness help explain his acquiescence.

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
B
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

插入性格描写不是孤立赞美，而是解释务农抱负为何容易受挫。B准确描述其情节作用；没有证据说他体力不足，也不在解释冒险勇气。

The characterization helps explain why his farming ambition is thwarted. It is not evidence of physical incapacity or an explanation of daring behavior.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

复述性格含义，而没有说明该描写在前后情节中的作用。

Paraphrasing a trait without explaining its role in the surrounding events.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

Mira rarely argued, so she accepted the unfair decision. ‘rarely argued’的功能是什么？

Mira rarely argued, so she accepted the unfair decision. What is the trait's function?

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

解释她为何接受不公决定，而不是评价决定公平。

It explains her acceptance, not the fairness of the decision.

练习出处 / Practice source: 本手册原创，基于 S032 · S047 的考点；不是官方原题。 / Created for this guide from the linked skill; not an official question.

S044独立推导 / Inferred

Johnson：概括后的展开Johnson: elaborating a general statement

知识点：句子功能 · 概括与细化
Skills: Sentence function · Elaboration of a general statement
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

前句说画风发生突然转变，后句说明从表现主义风景转向黑人肖像。

A general statement of a stylistic transformation is followed by the specific shift from landscapes to portraits.

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
A
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

划线句把抽象的transformation具体化，因此是elaborates。它不是反驳前句，也不是用旅行经历证明之后的全部影响来源。

The sentence specifies what the transformation involved, so it elaborates. It does not dispute the transformation or primarily document travel.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

只因句子提到过去与后来，就概括成一般传记叙述。

Calling it biography without identifying its explanatory relationship to the prior claim.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

Her methods changed. She replaced lectures with workshops. 第二句对第一句起什么作用？

Her methods changed. She replaced lectures with workshops. How does the second sentence function?

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

用具体变化展开第一句的概括。

It elaborates the general change with a specific example.

练习出处 / Practice source: 本手册原创，基于 S044 的考点；不是官方原题。 / Created for this guide from the linked skill; not an official question.

S046独立推导 / Inferred

洞穴照明：主旨不等于一个例子Cave lighting: main idea versus example

知识点：主旨 · 覆盖范围
Skills: Main idea · Coverage of the whole text
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

研究团队测试火把、脂肪灯与火堆，探索旧石器时代人如何在黑暗洞穴移动。

Researchers test torches, fat lamps, and fireplaces to understand movement through Paleolithic caves.

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
D
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

D概括研究行动与整体收获。脂肪灯不照亮地面只是说明不同光源用途的例子，不能据此断言某光源更常用或火堆绝不可少。

D covers the investigation and its overall insight. One lamp limitation illustrates differing functions; it does not establish frequency of use or the indispensability of fireplaces.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

把For instance后面一个鲜明细节升级为全文中心。

Promoting an illustrative detail into the entire main idea.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

文章比较三种储水法，举例说陶罐蒸发较慢。主旨应只写陶罐吗？

An article compares three water-storage methods and notes slower evaporation in clay pots. Should the main idea mention only clay pots?

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

不；应概括比较三种方法及所发现的性能差异。

No. It should cover the comparison and its broader findings.

练习出处 / Practice source: 本手册原创，基于 S046 的考点；不是官方原题。 / Created for this guide from the linked skill; not an official question.

S062独立推导 / Inferred

中世纪浴场：承认限制后补充Medieval baths: conceding a limitation

知识点：让步功能 · 城市与乡村
Skills: Concessive function · Urban versus rural access
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

文本反驳中世纪人不洗澡；承认公共浴场主要供城镇居民使用，然后补充其他普遍洗浴方式。

The text disputes a hygiene myth, concedes urban concentration of bathhouses, and notes other common bathing practices.

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
C
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

划线部分承认不是所有人都能使用公共浴场，为后面的替代洗浴方式作限定。它没有证明乡村更爱蒸汽浴，也没有评价旧研究的方法。

The clause concedes limited bathhouse access before adding other practices. It does not compare steam-bath popularity or criticize earlier research methods.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

把服务可及性的限制，误读成不同地区偏好的比较。

Converting an access limitation into a comparison of preferences.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

Although buses serve only the center, rural residents use shared taxis. 前半句功能是什么？

Although buses serve only the center, rural residents use shared taxis. What does the first clause do?

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

承认公交覆盖范围有限，然后补充其他交通方式。

It concedes limited bus coverage before introducing an alternative.

S063独立推导 / Inferred

Chicano运动：研究视角遮蔽多样性The Chicano movement: a narrowing scholarly lens

知识点：学术观点 · 句段功能
Skills: Scholarly perspectives · Rhetorical function
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

常见学术叙述使运动显得一致激进；社区层面的研究则呈现多种政治取向。

Conventional accounts make the movement appear uniformly radical; neighborhood-level research reveals varied orientations.

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
C
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

划线部分描述一种常见研究路径及其遮蔽多样性的后果。不能额外归因于学者自己的政治立场；也未说社区项目无效。

The portion identifies an approach that obscures ideological diversity. It does not attribute that approach to scholars' own politics or judge neighborhood projects ineffective.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

从‘研究偏重某方面’跳到对研究者动机的猜测。

Inferring researchers' motives from a selective research focus.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

报道只采访明星运动员，让团队显得人人富有。指出报道方法的后果。

Coverage interviews only star athletes, making a team appear uniformly wealthy. Describe the effect of the method.

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

选择性采访遮蔽了成员经济状况的差异，而非证明记者本人富有。

Selective coverage obscures economic diversity; it says nothing about reporters' wealth.

S065独立推导 / Inferred

自然资源与投资：反直觉主旨Resources and investment: an explained paradox

知识点：主旨 · 让步加解释
Skills: Main idea · Concession followed by explanation
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

资源开采需要投资，但依赖资源的经济反而吸引较少外资，因为周期波动制造投资者不喜欢的不确定性。

Extraction needs investment, yet resource-dependent economies receive less foreign investment because volatility creates uncertainty.

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
C
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

主旨需同时包含表面矛盾、观察到的负相关和解释机制。C完整保留三者；A错误声称外资无力承担初始投资，B则把外资减少改写为依赖减少。

C preserves the apparent paradox, the negative relationship, and the uncertainty mechanism. The text does not claim investors cannot fund extraction or equate lower receipts with lower dependence.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

漏掉but之后的核心解释，或把相关变量换成相近词。

Missing the explanatory turn or substituting a different variable.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

观光带来收入，但过度依赖观光增加收入波动，令投资减少。用一句话概括。

Tourism earns income, but overdependence increases volatility and reduces investment. Summarize.

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

虽观光可创收，过度依赖会因不确定性削弱投资吸引力。

Although tourism generates revenue, overdependence can deter investment through uncertainty.

练习出处 / Practice source: 本手册原创，基于 S065 的考点；不是官方原题。 / Created for this guide from the linked skill; not an official question.

S081独立推导 / Inferred

早期电视：说明广告商犹豫的原因Early television: why advertisers hesitated

知识点：短语功能 · 因果背景
Skills: Phrase function · Causal background
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

战时电视机生产停滞，受众规模不确定，帮助解释广告商为何犹豫。

Wartime production delays and an uncertain audience help explain advertisers' hesitation.

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
B
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

划线背景给出支持电视投资有风险的具体原因。它不是广播公司如何说服广告商的办法，也不是某类节目受欢迎的原因。

The phrase supplies a specific reason for hesitation. It does not describe persuasion tactics or explain a program type's popularity.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

把背景条件与后续应对措施混淆。

Confusing the cause of a problem with the response to it.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

Factories were closed, so investors delayed orders. ‘Factories were closed’说明原因还是解决办法？

Factories were closed, so investors delayed orders. Does the first clause state a cause or a solution?

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

原因：解释投资者延后订单。

A cause: it explains the delay in orders.

S083独立推导 / Inferred

翼足类：新发现缓和担忧Pteropods: findings qualify a concern

知识点：主要目的 · 科学结论限度
Skills: Main purpose · Limits of scientific conclusions
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

原认为酸化容易溶解薄壳，但研究发现保护层和修复内壁可降低伤害。

Acidification threatens delicate shells, but protective coatings and inner-wall repair can mitigate damage.

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
D
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

主要目的是呈现会使原担忧需要重新评估的发现，D最接近。注意may be unwarranted是有保留的，不应扩写成酸化对所有翼足类完全无害。B还倒置了保护层与碳酸钙的关系。

D best captures findings that qualify the concern. Its may language matters: the passage does not prove acidification universally harmless. B also reverses the protective relationship.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

把缓解机制升级成绝对安全，或反转谁保护谁。

Turning mitigation into universal safety or reversing the mechanism.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

A material reduces crack propagation. Can we conclude that it will never break?

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

不能；减少风险不等于消除一切风险。

No. Risk reduction is not elimination of all risk.

S091独立推导 / Inferred

新媒体：挑战替代论New media: challenging displacement predictions

Skills: The function of a challenge · Evidence in an argument

A

Recognizing the topic but not the direction of argument.

It uses coexistence to challenge total displacement.

S103独立推导 / Inferred

低成本对政策价值的支持Low cost as a justification

Skills: Sentence function · Cost-benefit reasoning
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

风险提示短信的行为影响小且短暂，但研究者仍认为值得尝试；划线部分指出短信成本几乎为零。问其作用。

Risk-disclosure messages had small, short-lived effects, yet researchers considered them worthwhile. What is the role of noting their negligible cost?

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
A — a reason not to dismiss inexpensive messaging
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

Independent derivation: Negligible cost explains why even limited benefits may justify messaging, matching A. C misstates the cost-benefit relationship; B and D introduce unsupported qualifications.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

常见错因是只看到效果小便判断毫无价值，或混淆低收益与高成本。

A common trap is treating small benefits as worthless without considering their cost.

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

说明即使收益小，零额外成本仍可使措施值得采用。

It explains why a modest benefit can still justify a measure with no additional cost.

S116独立推导 / Inferred

海浪的拟人化与不屈性Waves characterized as persistent

知识点：诗歌句段功能 · 拟人化 · 语气线索
Skills: Function of lines in a poem · Personification · Tone clues
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

Grimké《At Newport》写海浪不断撞岩、被击退却不认输。问相关诗句的作用。

In Grimké's At Newport, waves leap against rocks and refuse defeat despite being repulsed. What do these lines contribute?

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
B — a relentless and enduring force
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

独立推导：被反复击退但仍不屈服，将海浪写成持久而顽强的力量，选 B。全诗 delight、joyous 等词是喜悦语气，不支持恐惧或矛盾心理；也未对照思想与海浪。

Independent derivation: Repeated repulsion without surrender characterizes the waves as enduring and relentless, B. Delight and joyous indicate appreciation, not intimidation or ambivalence, and no contrast with the speaker's thoughts is established.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

常见错因是因 rocks、repulsed 等强烈词语而误判为恐怖场景，忽视整体赞赏语气。

A common trap is reading forceful words as threatening while ignoring the appreciative tone.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

The wind bends the reed repeatedly, yet it rises again each time. What quality is emphasized?

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

坚韧与持续抵抗；重复受挫和再次挺起构成依据。

Resilience and persistence, supported by repeated setbacks followed by recovery.

S139独立推导 / Inferred

贯穿诗段的夜之拟人Extended personification of night

知识点：整体结构 · 持续拟人
Skills: Overall structure · Extended personification
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

Bennett 诗中夜穿衣服、遮面纱，有头发与双手。问整体结构。

Bennett portrays Night wearing a garment and veil, with hair and hands. What describes the poem's overall structure?

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
C — an extended comparison of night to a person
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

独立推导：多个连续意象都把夜写成人，构成贯穿的拟人化比较，C。没有城乡交替、日出接续或季节变化的证据。

Independent derivation: The recurring clothing and bodily imagery develops Night as a person throughout, C. The poem does not alternate city and countryside, move to sunrise, or trace seasonal change.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

常见错因是从标题或自然词汇补出时间转换，而忽视贯穿的共同修辞结构。

A common trap is inventing a temporal progression from the title instead of tracking repeated figurative structure.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

Dawn opens the door, shakes starlight from her shoulders, and smiles at the valley. What sustained device is used?

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

拟人；开门、肩膀、微笑连续赋予黎明人的特征。

Personification: opening a door, having shoulders, and smiling repeatedly give Dawn human traits.

S151独立推导 / Inferred

限定语显示推测而非确定Qualifying language signals speculation

知识点：戏剧语言 · 情态限定 · 句段功能
Skills: Dramatic language · Modal qualifications · Function of a passage segment
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

《冬天的故事》中 Camillo 想回西西里；他说自己或可慰藉旧王的悲伤，随即表示也许只是自己妄想。划线话语有什么作用？

In The Winter's Tale, Camillo wishes to return to Sicily and says he might soothe his former king's sorrow, while qualifying that he may merely presume so. What is the function?

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
C — a possible benefit to Sicily's king is speculative
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

独立推导（已核划线）：might 和 or I o'erween to think so 承认慰藉效果仅是猜想，故 C。它不是向 Polixenes 保证，也未说这是主要动机；此前已有思乡、归葬故里的理由。

Independent derivation, underline checked: Might and the acknowledgment that he may presume too much mark the consoling benefit as speculative, C. This neither reassures Polixenes nor establishes the primary motive; Camillo has already expressed homesickness and a wish to die at home.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

常见错因是忽略自我修正语，把“可能有帮助”读成“确定且主要的离开理由”。

A common trap is ignoring the self-qualification and turning a possible benefit into a certain primary motive.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

“我的出席也许能让他安心——至少我是这样希望的。”破折号后的话起什么作用？

My presence might reassure him—at least, that is what I hope. What does the final phrase do?

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

限定前述效果的确定性，表明这是希望或推测，不是已证实事实。

It qualifies the certainty of the proposed effect, presenting it as a hope rather than an established fact.

S154独立推导 / Inferred

尖峰波研究的主旨The main idea of research on spike waves

知识点：科学文主旨 · 研究对象与结论 · 避免机制偷换
Skills: Main ideas in scientific passages · Research subjects and conclusions · Avoiding misattribution of mechanisms
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

行进波波高受波陡度导致的破碎限制；相向行进波形成的尖峰波可超过该限制，因为破碎还受射流稳定和空腔形状影响。概括主旨。

Traveling-wave heights are constrained by steepness-driven breaking; spike waves produced by opposing waves can exceed this limit because other factors govern their breaking. State the main idea.

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
D — intersecting traveling waves can produce waves exceeding expected height limits
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

独立推导：D 保留特定交汇方式、形成的新波及超出行进波性质预期的高度三点。A 把尖峰波机制转给行进波；B 错归因于同一临界陡度；C 说通常更高，超出可超过的证据。

Independent derivation: D captures the specific intersection, resulting wave, and height beyond traveling-wave expectations. A transfers spike-wave mechanisms to traveling waves; B reinstates the very steepness constraint surpassed; C overgeneralizes possibility into a tendency.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

常见错因是把两种波的机制混为一谈，或把 can exceed 升格成通常更高。

Common traps are transferring mechanisms between wave types and strengthening can exceed into usually higher.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

材料甲常在80°C变形；新结构在其他稳定因素作用下能超过此限制。主旨应强调什么？

Material A normally deforms at 80°C; a new structure can exceed this limit because other stabilizing factors matter. What should the main idea emphasize?

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

新结构可突破原有材料的温度限制，而非说原材料现在也有同样稳定机制。

The new structure can surpass the old thermal limit, not that the old material necessarily gains the same mechanism.

S168独立推导 / Inferred

熟悉参照物说明数值规模Using a familiar reference to convey numerical scale

知识点：数据修辞功能 · 比较参照 · 避免用途推断
Skills: Rhetorical functions of data · Comparative benchmarks · Avoiding unsupported inferences about use
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

一种有孔虫在瓦登海表层储存约1880吨磷酸盐，约等于德国年磷肥用量5%。划线比较有什么作用？

A foraminifer stores about 1,880 tons of phosphate in Wadden Sea surface sediment, compared with roughly 5% of Germany's yearly phosphorus fertilizer use. What does the comparison do?

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
D — a comparison illustrates phosphate-storage scale
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

独立推导（已核图）：用熟悉的人类使用规模帮助读者理解储量大小，D。它不主张实际供给德国肥料；百分比不是个体密度；末句说其他有孔虫储量相当，也不支持“异常高于同类”。

Independent derivation, image-checked: A human-use benchmark makes the storage magnitude comprehensible, D. It does not establish an actual fertilizer supply, organism density, or an exceptional effect compared with other foraminifera, which are described as comparable.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

常见错因是把数值比较误读成实际利用建议，或混淆总储量与单位体积密度。

Common traps are mistaking a scale comparison for a proposed use and confusing total storage with density.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

“水库储水量相当于全市十天用水量。”这句话一定是在建议供水十天吗？主要作用是什么？

The reservoir holds the equivalent of ten days of city water use. Does this necessarily recommend ten days of supply, and what is its main function?

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

不是；主要用熟悉的用水量说明储量规模。

No. It primarily uses familiar consumption to illustrate storage magnitude.

S176独立推导 / Inferred

假说之后呈现不一致结果A hypothesis followed by inconsistent findings

知识点：主旨功能 · 假说与结果区分
Skills: Main purpose · Distinguishing hypotheses from findings
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

有人推测兄弟姐妹互动会增强儿童幽默识别；研究发现无、一个、两个兄弟姐妹的儿童识别能力无差异。问文章目的。

Sibling interactions are proposed to improve humor recognition, but children with zero, one, or two siblings show no recognition differences. What is the text's purpose?

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
A — propose a relationship, then present inconsistent study findings
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

独立推导：文本先提出关系，再报告与预期不一致的结果，A。它未修订假说，也未先展示代表性受质疑的已观察模式，更不存在共识。结论只涉及该年龄和该测量，不是否定所有幽默能力影响。

Independent derivation: The passage proposes a relationship and reports findings inconsistent with it, A. It neither refines the hypothesis nor questions an established pattern or consensus. The result concerns this age group and measure, not every humor-related ability.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

常见错因是把合理推测当已证实模式，或将无差异结果说成提出了新版本理论。

A common trap is treating a proposed relationship as an established pattern or inventing a revised theory.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

先猜想养宠物提高耐心，再报告两组耐心测试无差异。文章结构是什么？

A text proposes that pet ownership improves patience, then reports no difference between groups on a patience test. What is its structure?

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

提出假说，随后给出未支持该假说的研究结果。

It presents a hypothesis followed by findings that do not support it.

练习出处 / Practice source: 本手册原创，基于 S176 的考点；不是官方原题。 / Created for this guide from the linked skill; not an official question.

S186独立推导 / Inferred

潜在方法挑战与未出现的结果A potential methodological challenge absent from the reported study

知识点：句子功能 · 一般风险与个案结果 · 转折信号
Skills: Sentence function · General risks versus specific study results · Contrast signals
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

水体中铀同位素分布有时不均，会影响采样研究；但两湖垂直和水平测量在各湖内部未发现显著差异。划线风险说明有何作用？

Uranium isotopes can be unevenly distributed within water bodies, compromising sampling; however, horizontal and vertical measurements found no significant within-lake variation in two lakes. What is the function of the risk statement?

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
B — potential heterogeneity can undermine research but was not observed here
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

独立推导：先给可能降低研究可靠性的挑战，再用 however 引出本研究未观察到该挑战，B。A 倒置因果：采样不足不造成分布不均；C 未有动机证据；D 错把湖内均匀说成两湖间比较消除了普遍风险。

Independent derivation: A general threat to robust sampling precedes a study in which it was not observed, B. A reverses causality, C adds an unstated research motive, and D mistakes within-lake uniformity for a cross-lake comparison disproving the general concern.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

常见错因是把“此研究未见”扩大为“普遍问题已被彻底消除”。

A common trap is turning absence in this study into universal elimination of the risk.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

“样本可能被污染。然而，本次检测未发现污染。”首句的功能是什么？

Samples may be contaminated. However, no contamination was detected in this experiment. What does the first sentence do?

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

提出潜在可靠性威胁，后句说明该威胁在本次实验未被观察到。

It introduces a potential reliability threat that the reported experiment did not observe.

练习出处 / Practice source: 本手册原创，基于 S186 的考点；不是官方原题。 / Created for this guide from the linked skill; not an official question.

S188独立推导 / Inferred

期刊同时改变文学地位与读写角色Periodicals changed literary status and reader-author roles

知识点：主旨双线概括 · 转折后重点 · 避免虚构学术转向
Skills: Summarizing a two-part main idea · Emphasis after a contrast · Avoiding invented shifts in scholarship
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

大众期刊有助提高通俗文学的学术地位，也通过读者投稿打破作者单向生产模式。概括主旨。

Mass-market periodicals helped legitimize popular literature and invited reader submissions, shifting authorship toward interactive production. Summarize the main idea.

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
A — mass-market periodicals elevated popular literature and redefined reader-author roles
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

独立推导：A 同时覆盖文学地位变化和读者成为意义创造者的角色变化。B 把动机说成吸引受众且过窄；C 虚构学术领域已转向；D 将积极贡献改成遮蔽差异，无依据。

Independent derivation: A captures both legitimization of popular literature and redefinition of readers as creators. B invents an engagement motive and narrows the claim; C invents a scholarly pivot; D introduces an unsupported obscuring effect.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

常见错因是只抓读者来信细节，或把作者补充的新视角说成整个学术领域已经改变。

A common trap is choosing one submission detail or turning a new perspective into a documented field-wide shift.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

文章说公园既改善空气，也通过公共活动增强邻里联系。主旨应包含什么？

A passage says parks improve air and strengthen neighborhood ties through public events. What should its main idea include?

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

同时包含环境改善和社会联系两方面，而不是只列某次活动。

Both environmental improvement and social connection, rather than one particular event.

练习出处 / Practice source: 本手册原创，基于 S188 的考点；不是官方原题。 / Created for this guide from the linked skill; not an official question.
原题出处 / Uploaded source: 用户提供截图 S188 · Mass-market nineteenth- and early twentieth-century US periodicals, such as Bradley, His。正式试卷、模块与官方题库ID未核实。 / Test, module, and official item IDs are unverified.
03

文本证据与研究逻辑Evidence and research logic

11 题组 / question groups
知识点总则 / Core principles

先把论点拆成必要组成部分：例如‘观众参与＋肢体喜剧’需要两面都能证明。研究题先画出预期机制，再选能直接支持这一机制、方向正确的发现。‘有关联’不等于‘足以支持’。

Break a claim into required components. Audience-mediated slapstick needs evidence of audience influence and physical comedy. For research claims, map the mechanism and check the direction of the proposed evidence.

常见错因 / Common traps

只匹配一个关键词；选择相关但不直接支持的发现；把相关性写成已证明的因果。

One-keyword matches; related but nondiagnostic findings; overstated causal claims.

S017独立推导 / Inferred

剧团：证据必须覆盖两个限定Theater evidence must cover both qualifiers

知识点：复合论点 · 直接文本证据
Skills: Compound claims · Direct textual evidence
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

论点是早期表演受‘观众参与调节的闹剧式肢体喜剧’影响。

The claim concerns audience-mediated slapstick in early performances.

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
A
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

A既有夸张肢体幽默，又有欢呼和嘘声促成即兴调整。单有喜剧、讽刺或简陋道具，不能同时证明audience-mediated与slapstick。

A includes exaggerated physical humor and audience reactions prompting improvisation. Humor, satire, or minimal props alone does not establish both components.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

看到comedy关键词就选，漏掉audience-mediated这一约束。

Matching comedy alone and overlooking the audience-mediated requirement.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

要证明‘学生反馈驱动的合作学习’，证据只有‘学生分组’够吗？还需什么？

To support feedback-driven collaborative learning, is grouping students enough? What else is needed?

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

不够；还应显示学生反馈导致学习或分组活动调整。

No. Feedback must be shown to influence the collaborative activity.

练习出处 / Practice source: 本手册原创，基于 S017 的考点；不是官方原题。 / Created for this guide from the linked skill; not an official question.

S036独立推导 / Inferred

Poetry：矛盾态度需要两面证据Poetry: evidence for ambivalence

知识点：双重态度 · 复合证据
Skills: Mixed attitudes · Evidence for multiple components
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

论点既有对诗歌的不满，也承认它的价值；要选同时包含两面的引文。

The claim combines displeasure with recognition of poetry's merits.

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
D
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

D将contempt与a place for the genuine放在一起，完整呈现ambivalence。只批评难懂或只赞其重要，都只证明一半。

D juxtaposes contempt with room for the genuine. Criticism alone or praise alone supports only half of ambivalence.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

把ambivalence当成完全否定，或满足于一面证据。

Reading ambivalence as pure dislike or accepting one-sided support.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

哪句表现复杂态度？甲‘我讨厌它的一切’；乙‘我嫌它冗长，却佩服其洞察’。

Which conveys ambivalence: 'I hate everything about it' or 'I find it long but admire its insight'?

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

乙，同时存在负面感受与正面认可。

The latter combines criticism and appreciation.

练习出处 / Practice source: 本手册原创，基于 S036 的考点；不是官方原题。 / Created for this guide from the linked skill; not an official question.

S049独立推导 / Inferred

TMAO：用机制证据支持假说TMAO: mechanistic support

知识点：支持假说 · 变量方向
Skills: Supporting a hypothesis · Direction of relationships
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

高压破坏氢键使水更易压缩；假说认为TMAO降低水的可压缩性。

Pressure destabilizes hydrogen bonds; TMAO is hypothesized to reduce water compressibility.

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
D
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

D显示高压下有TMAO时氢键更稳定，正好支持保护水结构的机制。TMAO自身形状不变并不说明水受保护；浓度与压缩速率正相关反而与假说方向相反。

More stable hydrogen bonds with TMAO directly supports the proposed mechanism. TMAO retaining its own shape does not establish water protection; increased compression with concentration points the wrong way.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

只看变量名称相同，没检查测量对象及正负方向。

Matching variable names without checking the measured outcome and direction.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

假说：添加剂减少金属腐蚀。哪项更支持：添加剂自身不变，还是金属损失更少？

Hypothesis: an additive reduces corrosion. Which supports it better: the additive stays unchanged, or the metal loses less mass?

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

金属损失更少，因为它直接测量需要保护的对象。

Less metal loss directly measures the proposed protection.

练习出处 / Practice source: 本手册原创，基于 S049 的考点；不是官方原题。 / Created for this guide from the linked skill; not an official question.
原题出处 / Uploaded source: 用户提供截图 S049 · Reading and Writing: Question 16。正式试卷、模块与官方题库ID未核实。 / Test, module, and official item IDs are unverified.
S069独立推导 / Inferred

NFM：间接效应链News finds me: an indirect pathway

知识点：中介机制 · 相关与因果
Skills: Mediating mechanisms · Association versus causation
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

被动等新闻可能降低知识与兴趣，而知识兴趣与投票概率正相关，形成间接路径。

Passive news reliance may reduce knowledge and interest, which are positively associated with voting.

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
B
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

B补齐NFM→知识/兴趣下降→投票可能性降低这条路径。要同时有前后两段关系；研究并未在此次数据中直接确认投票因果，因此应说支持机制而非证明因果。

B supplies both links: NFM to reduced knowledge/interest, and those factors to voting likelihood. This supports the proposed indirect pathway without proving causal voting effects in this study.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

只找到与投票有关的一段关系，就以为完整支持间接效应。

Finding only one link rather than a complete proposed pathway.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

A降低睡眠，睡眠改善记忆。要支持A间接损害记忆，需要连接哪两个关系？

A reduces sleep; sleep supports memory. Which links support an indirect adverse effect of A on memory?

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

A→睡眠减少，以及睡眠减少→记忆表现降低。

A to less sleep, and less sleep to worse memory.

练习出处 / Practice source: 本手册原创，基于 S069 的考点；不是官方原题。 / Created for this guide from the linked skill; not an official question.

S120独立推导 / Inferred

敬畏与助人行为的实验证据Experimental evidence for awe and helping

知识点：实验组与对照组 · 操作化指标 · 支持因果假说
Skills: Experimental and control groups · Operational measures · Supporting a causal hypothesis
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

研究者让一组看高树、另一组看普通建筑，再故意掉笔；哪项结果支持敬畏使人更利他？

Participants view awe-inspiring trees or an ordinary building; an experimenter then drops pens. Which finding supports awe increasing altruism?

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
A — tree viewers picked up more pens
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

独立推导：高树组比建筑组帮助捡起更多笔，同时对应敬畏操纵和助人结果，A 最直接。B 聚焦积极用词；C 方向相反；D 的注意差异不是更多利他行为。

Independent derivation: More pens retrieved by tree viewers links the awe manipulation to greater helping, making A most direct. B concerns positive wording, C reverses the prediction, and D measures noticing rather than altruistic assistance.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

常见错因是只看情绪描述，未把抽象利他转化为可观察的帮助行为。

A common trap is focusing on emotional descriptions rather than the behavioral measure of altruism.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

要支持“音乐提高合作”，一组听音乐，一组安静。最直接比较什么？

To test whether music improves cooperation, one group hears music and another stays in silence. What outcome should be compared?

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

两组在同一合作任务中的合作行为或贡献量；仅问是否喜欢音乐不够。

Cooperative behavior or contributions in the same task; liking the music alone is insufficient.

练习出处 / Practice source: 本手册原创，基于 S120 的考点；不是官方原题。 / Created for this guide from the linked skill; not an official question.

S122独立推导 / Inferred

削弱投票不改变态度的主张Weakening the claim that voting leaves attitudes unchanged

知识点：削弱题 · 组间对照 · 态度变化
Skills: Weakening a claim · Between-group comparisons · Changes in attitudes
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

传统观点认为投票不改变对候选人的态度。研究对比刚有资格投票者和略小、无资格者，两年后测态度。什么结果最削弱该观点？

The traditional claim says voting does not alter candidate attitudes. Researchers compare just-eligible voters with slightly younger ineligible peers two years after elections. Which finding most weakens the claim?

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
C — voters later had more polarized attitudes than ineligible peers
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

独立推导：C 中实际投票者态度比不可投票者更极化，与“投票无影响”不一致，最直接削弱。B 只报告未投票组变化；A、D 不显示与投票相关的组间差异。这里是削弱证据，不等于完全证明因果。

Independent derivation: Greater later polarization among actual voters than ineligible peers conflicts most directly with no effect, supporting C. B concerns only nonvoters; A and D do not isolate a voting-related difference. This weakens the claim without proving causation conclusively.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

常见错因是把任何态度变化都当投票效果，忽略是否比较投票与未投票。

A common trap is treating any attitude change as a voting effect without a relevant comparison group.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

主张：参加辩论不会改变态度。哪项更削弱：参加者与可比未参加者态度出现差异，还是两组都喜欢主持人？

Claim: Participating in debate does not change attitudes. Which weakens it more: a later attitude difference between comparable participants and nonparticipants, or both groups liking the moderator?

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

前者；它直接关联参与行为与态度差异，后者不测目标效果。

The former; it connects participation with differing attitudes, whereas moderator preference is irrelevant.

练习出处 / Practice source: 本手册原创，基于 S122 的考点；不是官方原题。 / Created for this guide from the linked skill; not an official question.
原题出处 / Uploaded source: 用户提供截图 S122 · Reading and Writing: Question 12。正式试卷、模块与官方题库ID未核实。 / Test, module, and official item IDs are unverified.
S138独立推导 / Inferred

饮食证据支持动物饲养位置Dietary evidence for animal husbandry locations

知识点：支持推断 · 独立证据与替代解释
Skills: Supporting an inference · Independent evidence and alternative explanations
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

青铜时代牛主要吃人种的黍，羊吃野生植物；研究者推断牛靠近聚落饲养。什么发现最支持？

Bronze Age cattle mainly ate cultivated millet, whereas sheep and goats ate wild plants. Researchers infer cattle were raised nearer settlements. Which finding supports this?

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
A — cattle also consumed cultivated wheat
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

独立推导：A 增添牛也吃人类广泛种植的小麦这一证据，与更接近人类农业活动一致。B 给羊也食黍反而削弱清晰对比；C 营养需求与实际饲养位置无直接联系；D 不能明确支持牛羊空间差别。

Independent derivation: Cattle also eating cultivated wheat adds evidence of proximity to human agriculture, A. Millet in sheep diets blurs the contrast; nutritional needs alone do not locate animals; D does not establish the proposed spatial difference.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

常见错因是用动物吃得多这类合理常识替代与人类种植直接相关的证据。

A common trap is using plausible nutritional facts instead of evidence tied directly to cultivation.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

考古学家推断鹿被圈养在村边。发现鹿吃大量栽培大麦，还是发现鹿体重大，更能支持？

Researchers infer deer were kept near a village. Which supports this more: diets rich in cultivated barley or large body size?

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

栽培大麦；它直接联系到人类种植环境。

Cultivated barley in their diets, because it directly connects them to human farming.

练习出处 / Practice source: 本手册原创，基于 S138 的考点；不是官方原题。 / Created for this guide from the linked skill; not an official question.
原题出处 / Uploaded source: 用户提供截图 S138 · Reading and Writing: Question 9。正式试卷、模块与官方题库ID未核实。 / Test, module, and official item IDs are unverified.
S156独立推导 / Inferred

社交形象如何影响音乐选择How social image influences listening choices

知识点：匹配研究结论与引语 · 动机到行为
Skills: Matching findings with quotations · Connecting motivations to behavior
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

研究发现流媒体用户因担心社交网络如何看待自己的收听而调整行为。哪段引语最能说明？

A study finds that streaming users adjust listening because of how their activity may appear to social contacts. Which quotation illustrates this?

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
A — choosing songs for followers rather than personal preference
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

独立推导：A 明说选择朋友可能喜欢而非自己当时想听的歌，直接体现他人眼光改变行为。B 为算法重复；C 是共享体验；D 是被动消费，均未呈现形象管理。

Independent derivation: A explicitly describes choosing music for followers instead of one's own current preference, directly showing impression management. B concerns recommendations, C shared experience, and D passive consumption.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

常见错因是把任何带“社交”的引语都当支持，未要求行为受到他人评价影响。

A common trap is choosing any social quotation without checking whether perceived judgment changes behavior.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

“我本想发随意照片，但怕同事觉得不专业，改发工作照。”支持什么结论？

I wanted to post a casual photo, but chose a work photo because colleagues might find the first unprofessional. What finding does this illustrate?

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

对他人看法的顾虑影响内容选择，属于形象管理。

Concern about others' perceptions changes content choices, illustrating impression management.

练习出处 / Practice source: 本手册原创，基于 S156 的考点；不是官方原题。 / Created for this guide from the linked skill; not an official question.
原题出处 / Uploaded source: 用户提供截图 S156 · Reading and Writing: Question 11。正式试卷、模块与官方题库ID未核实。 / Test, module, and official item IDs are unverified.
S177独立推导 / Inferred

妥协能力的政策证据Policy evidence of willingness to compromise

知识点：引语支持 · 行为机制 · 避免只支持背景
Skills: Supporting claims with quotations · Behavioral mechanisms · Avoiding evidence that supports only background details
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

作者认为 Reagan 成功不仅因保守意识形态，更因愿意同异见者妥协合作。哪项历史引语最支持？

The author attributes Reagan's success partly to compromise and cooperation across disagreement rather than rigid ideology. Which historical quotation best supports this?

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
D — passing modified priorities without full legislative control
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

独立推导：D 说其政党未完全控制立法机关，却通过多项修改后的优先政策，直接体现合作与让步。A 仅显示支出与口头立场不一致；B、C 讲沟通、个性或话题，不支持妥协机制。

Independent derivation: D describes modified policy priorities passing without full party control of the legislature, evidence of cooperation and concession. A shows a gap between rhetoric and spending but not collaboration; B and C concern communication or personal habits.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

常见错因是把“并非严格保守”的任何迹象当成“与异见者合作”的直接证据。

A common trap is treating any ideological inconsistency as direct evidence of cross-group compromise.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

主张某市长善于妥协。更直接证据是“常说团结”，还是“接受反对派修订后共同通过预算”？

Claim: A mayor is skilled at compromise. Which is stronger evidence: often praising unity, or accepting opposition amendments to pass a joint budget?

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

后者，提供实际让步并合作达成目标的行为。

The latter, because it documents concessions and cooperation that achieve a goal.

练习出处 / Practice source: 本手册原创，基于 S177 的考点；不是官方原题。 / Created for this guide from the linked skill; not an official question.

S178独立推导 / Inferred

不在旧怨群体之中形成信任优势Trust from not belonging to the offending group

知识点：文学引语支持 · 对比与因果
Skills: Support from literary quotations · Contrast and causation
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

Odysseus 认为找回 Philoctetes 的计划依赖 Neoptolemus 未参与当年的遗弃。哪段引语最直接说明？

Odysseus bases the retrieval plan on Neoptolemus not having joined the group that abandoned Philoctetes. Which quotation best illustrates this?

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
C — Neoptolemus can approach safely because he was not in the original band
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

独立推导：C 同时说你的接近安全可信、我的却不能，并解释你不在最初队伍，而责难由我承担，完整对应主张。其他引语只涉隐藏行踪、暂时欺骗或侦察合作。

Independent derivation: C explicitly contrasts safe, trusted access for Neoptolemus with blame borne by Odysseus, explaining that Neoptolemus was not in the original band. The alternatives address secrecy, deception, or reconnaissance instead.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

常见错因是选择同样谈计谋的引文，但没有支持“未参与遗弃”这一具体理由。

A common trap is selecting a quotation about planning that omits the specific reason the plan can work.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

主张：新调解员更易获信任，因为未参与旧争端。什么一句话能直接支持？

Claim: A new mediator is easier to trust because she took no part in the earlier dispute. Supply a supporting statement.

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

“双方愿意与她交谈，因为她从未支持当年的任何一方。”直接连接中立经历与信任。

Both sides will talk to her because she never sided with either party in the old dispute. This links noninvolvement to trust.

S190独立推导 / Inferred

验证回收方法的单变量实验A controlled experiment to evaluate recycling treatment

知识点：对照实验 · 控制变量 · 目标指标
Skills: Controlled experiments · Controlling variables · Target outcome measures
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

碘化铅溶解度低，醋酸铅较高；研究向水加醋酸钠以促成后者。怎样测试是否减少钙钛矿废物中的残留碘化铅？

Lead iodide has low water solubility, while lead acetate is more soluble; sodium acetate is added to water to promote the latter. How should researchers test reduced lead-iodide residue in perovskite waste?

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
A — compare pure water with sodium-acetate water on comparable perovskite waste
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

独立推导：A 用可比钙钛矿样本，一半纯水、一半醋酸钠水，比较残留量，唯一关键差别为处理剂。B、D 换成错误添加物；C 更换电池材料，且硅电池并非同样含铅基线。

Independent derivation: A compares matched perovskite samples under pure-water and sodium-acetate-water treatment, measuring residue. The treatment is the key changed variable. B and D substitute different additives; C confounds treatment with cell material.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

常见错因是拿不同材料做对照，或把想生成的产物误当实验添加剂。

Common traps are comparing different materials and confusing the desired product with the experimental additive.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

要检验清洁剂X是否比水更能除污，应如何设置最小对照？

To test whether cleaner X removes more dirt than water, what minimal comparison is needed?

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

用同样材质、同等污渍样本，在相同条件下分别用水和X处理，再比较残留污渍。

Use matched surfaces with comparable dirt, treat with water or X under otherwise identical conditions, and compare residue.

练习出处 / Practice source: 本手册原创，基于 S190 的考点；不是官方原题。 / Created for this guide from the linked skill; not an official question.
原题出处 / Uploaded source: 用户提供截图 S190 · Scientists are researching ways to recycle photovoltaic (PV) waste—solar cells that have。正式试卷、模块与官方题库ID未核实。 / Test, module, and official item IDs are unverified.
04

图表与数量证据Quantitative evidence

14 题组 / question groups
知识点总则 / Core principles

先读标题、坐标、单位、图例和分母，再明确比较对象与基线。多数是超过50%；区间不等于精确值；组均值不反映个体分布。若‘越小越好’，最优值应找最小值。敲除组标签可能表示缺失而非存在。

Read title, axes, units, legend, and denominator before comparing. Identify the relevant baseline. A majority exceeds 50%; bins are not exact values; means do not describe individual spread. A knockout label may name what is absent, not present.

常见错因 / Common traps

百分比换成人数；换错比较组；跨量表比较；漏看反向指标；将A敲除读成A活跃。

Counts inferred from percentages; wrong comparators; mixed scales; reversed metrics; knockout labels read as active genes.

S003独立推导 / Inferred

植物聚集：与随机基线比较Plant clustering and a random baseline

知识点：比较基线 · 数据支持论点
Skills: Comparison baselines · Data supporting a claim
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

随机分布时约15%的植物应位于植被斑块中；实测五种幼株的斑块比例均约52%—60%。哪项支持聚集有利？

Random placement predicts about 15% of plants in vegetation patches; observed juvenile proportions are about 52–60% for every species.

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
D
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

关键不是与100%或75%比较，而是与15%的随机期望比较。所有物种实测比例远高于基线，与早期聚集优势一致；这提供支持，并不单独证明因果。

The relevant comparator is the 15% random expectation, not 75% or 100%. Every observed proportion is substantially higher, supporting—but not alone proving—the proposed early advantage.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

选择一个真实却与研究假设无关的数字，或把相关证据写成因果证明。

Choosing a true but irrelevant statistic, or overstating support as causal proof.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

随机预计20%幼苗在树荫下，实测65%。这是否支持树荫相关优势？理由是什么？

Random placement predicts 20% of seedlings in shade; 65% are observed there. Does this support a shade-associated advantage?

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

支持：65%显著高于20%的比较基线，但仍需排除其他机制。

Yes: 65% exceeds the relevant 20% baseline, though alternative mechanisms remain possible.

练习出处 / Practice source: 本手册原创，基于 S003 的考点；不是官方原题。 / Created for this guide from the linked skill; not an official question.

S004独立推导 / Inferred

农民图表：多数与分母Farmers: majorities and denominators

知识点：多数阈值 · 百分比与人数
Skills: The majority threshold · Percentages versus counts
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

图表给出各地区、各主要作物农民中女性的百分比；问哪些组女性占多数。

The graph shows the female percentage within each region-and-crop group. Identify groups with a female majority.

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
C
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

majority 必须超过50%。非块根蔬菜组在北部和南部超过50%，对应C。某作物组女性比例较高，不代表该组女性人数更多，因为各组总人数未知。

A majority exceeds 50%. The non-root-vegetable groups in the north and south satisfy this threshold. Percentages do not determine female counts without group totals.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

把最大柱形误当多数；把‘组内女性比例’误读为‘全部女性的作物分布’。

Equating the tallest bar with a majority, or reversing the denominator.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

甲组100人、60%女性；乙组300人、40%女性。哪组女性占多数？哪组女性人数更多？

Group A has 100 people, 60% female; B has 300, 40% female. Which has a female majority, and which has more women?

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

甲组占多数；乙组人数更多，120人对60人。

A has a female majority; B has more women, 120 versus 60.

练习出处 / Practice source: 本手册原创，基于 S004 的考点；不是官方原题。 / Created for this guide from the linked skill; not an official question.
