S019独立推导 / Inferred

羊毛：最柔软对应最小直径Wool: softness and minimum diameter

知识点：反向指标 · 区间端点
Skills: Inverse performance metrics · Interval endpoints
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

内层纤维直径：Merino 17—21微米，Navajo-Churro 10—35，mouflon 15；越细越柔软。

Inner fibers: Merino 17–21 μm, Navajo-Churro 10–35, mouflon 15; thinner means softer.

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
A
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

at its softest要求比较最小值：10 < 15 < 17，因此Navajo-Churro最细内毛可比另两者更软。其10—35的范围很宽，不能推成‘整体更均匀或总是更软’。

At its softest directs attention to minima: 10 < 15 < 17. The broad 10–35 range does not imply uniform softness or superiority at every value.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

把区间最大值当最柔软；把‘有时更软’扩大成‘总是更软’。

Using maxima for softness or turning a best-case comparison into an always-true claim.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

A纤维8—30微米，B纤维12—16微米。谁的最细纤维更软？谁范围更稳定？

Fiber A spans 8–30 μm; B spans 12–16. Which has the softest minimum, and which varies less?

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

A最细时更软；B直径范围更窄。两个结论并不矛盾。

A has the softer minimum; B has the narrower range. These are different comparisons.

练习出处 / Practice source: 本手册原创，基于 S019 的考点；不是官方原题。 / Created for this guide from the linked skill; not an official question.
原题出处 / Uploaded source: 用户提供截图 S019 · Reading and Writing: Question 13。正式试卷、模块与官方题库ID未核实。 / Test, module, and official item IDs are unverified.
S033 · S068独立推导 / Inferred

安慰剂：逐指标比较均值Placebos: compare means within each measure

知识点：组间比较 · 均值与个体
Skills: Between-group comparisons · Means versus individuals
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

知情安慰剂组对照控制组：5.0比3.9，92比46，11.4比5.4；每个指标都是越高越好。

Informed-placebo versus control means are 5.0 vs 3.9, 92 vs 46, and 11.4 vs 5.4; higher is better on each measure.

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
D
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

三项都要在同一指标内横向比较，知情组均更高。不同指标量表不同，5与92之间的距离没有跨指标意义；组均值不能显示各参与者的差异范围。

Compare groups within each measure: all three means are higher. Different scales cannot be compared as a single range, and group means do not reveal individual variability.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

把三个量表数值的跨度，误当受试者之间差异。

Interpreting differences across scales as variation across participants.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

甲组数学均分80、阅读均分6，乙组分别75、5。能说甲组个人得分介于6和80吗？

Group A's math mean is 80 and reading mean 6; B's are 75 and 5. Can A's individual scores be said to span 6–80?

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

不能；它们是不同指标的组均值，只能分别比较80>75、6>5。

No. They are group means on different measures; compare 80>75 and 6>5 separately.

练习出处 / Practice source: 本手册原创，基于 S033 · S068 的考点；不是官方原题。 / Created for this guide from the linked skill; not an official question.

S035 · S048独立推导 / Inferred

蜥蜴：证据匹配逃生情境Lizards: match evidence to escape behavior

知识点：证据相关性 · 分箱限制
Skills: Relevance to the claim · Limits of grouped intervals
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

论点说即使逃避捕食者也可能不使用最大速度；图中多个物种逃生平均速度低于最大值的90%。

The claim concerns submaximal speed even during escape; several species escape at averages below 90% of their maxima.

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
B
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

B直接匹配escaping并给出低于最大值的证据。追猎时低速即使为真也不直接支持此限定。90—100%这一分箱不能单独证明所有物种恰好用100%。

B targets escape and shows submaximal averages. Pursuit data do not directly establish an escape claim; a 90–100% bin cannot isolate exactly 100%.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

只核对数字真实，漏核对行为标签；把区间当精确值。

Checking numbers but not behavior labels, or treating an interval as an exact value.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

要支持‘紧急撤离时也未达最快’，普通散步的低速数据够吗？

To support submaximal speed during emergency evacuation, is slow ordinary walking sufficient evidence?

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

不够；证据需来自紧急撤离情境，并与最大能力比较。

No. Evidence must concern evacuation and compare observed speed with capacity.

S085独立推导 / Inferred

候选人好感：关系在高分段变化Candidate likability: a subgroup exception

知识点：分组趋势 · 交点 · 限定范围
Skills: Subgroup trends · Intersections · Scope limits
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

总体更喜欢可敬特质候选人，但高ignobility分数参与者出现同等或反向偏好。

Admirable candidates are generally preferred, except among participants with high ignobility scores.

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
B
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

B逐分段对应：分数5及以下更偏好可敬者，6及以上同等或更偏好卑劣者。总体平均不能抹去子组例外，也不能把两条曲线都说成同向增加。

B describes the low-score preference and high-score equality or reversal. An overall result does not eliminate subgroup exceptions, and the two series need not share a direction.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

只报告总体趋势，漏掉题目重点except所要求的例外。

Reporting the overall trend while missing the exception targeted by except.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

分数1—4时A评分更高，5时相等，6时B更高。能说所有人都更喜欢A吗？

A rates higher at scores 1–4, ties at 5, and loses at 6. Does everyone prefer A?

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

不能；高分子组构成平局或反转的例外。

No. The high-score subgroup supplies a tie and reversal.

S086独立推导 / Inferred

射电瞬变星：周期不是频率Radio transients: period versus frequency

知识点：指标定义 · 最小值 · 倒数关系
Skills: Metric definitions · Minimum values · Reciprocal relationships
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

题干把连续脉冲间时间定义为period；表中J0614−03的period为0.136秒，最小。

The interval between pulses is defined as period; J0614−03 has the minimum listed period, 0.136 seconds.

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
A
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

按题干定义找period列最小值即可。frequency是每秒次数，与周期互为倒数；频率大反而间隔短。不要把赤经的时分读成脉冲间隔。

Use the period column and select its minimum. Frequency is events per second and is reciprocal to period; right ascension is not the pulse interval.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

单位都涉及时间就混列比较，或认为频率大意味着间隔长。

Mixing columns involving time or assuming a greater frequency means a longer interval.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

甲周期0.2秒，乙0.5秒。谁脉冲更密？各频率多少？

A has period 0.2 s; B 0.5 s. Which pulses more often, and what are their frequencies?

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

甲更密；f=1/T，分别5Hz和2Hz。

A pulses more often; f=1/T gives 5 Hz and 2 Hz.

练习出处 / Practice source: 本手册原创，基于 S086 的考点；不是官方原题。 / Created for this guide from the linked skill; not an official question.
原题出处 / Uploaded source: 用户提供截图 S086 · Reading and Writing: Question 14。正式试卷、模块与官方题库ID未核实。 / Test, module, and official item IDs are unverified.
S099独立推导 / Inferred

果蝇敲除：标签表示缺失基因Fruit-fly knockouts: labels indicate silenced genes

知识点：敲除实验 · 图例 · 特异性
Skills: Knockout experiments · Graph legends · Specificity
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

A型关闭DptA，B型关闭DptB，AB关闭两者；感染A. sicerae后A型存活高，B与AB型接近零。

Type A silences DptA, B silences DptB, and AB silences both. After A. sicerae infection, A survives well while B and AB decline nearly to zero.

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
C
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

A型仍有DptB，B与AB型均缺DptB，表明对该菌的防御与DptB相关；结合另一病菌依赖DptA，支持两基因防御分工，故C的可能特异性最稳妥。并无两基因都开启的对照组可证明协同最强。

A retains DptB; B and AB lack it. Survival therefore supports a DptB role against this pathogen. Combined with the DptA role against another pathogen, C's qualified specialization claim fits. No intact-both group establishes maximal synergy.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

误把A型当作‘只有A活跃’，其实A表示A被关闭；凭空补出双基因完整组。

Reading type A as A active when it means A silenced, or inventing an intact-both group.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

甲组关闭X但保留Y，存活90%；乙组关闭Y，存活5%。这更支持哪个基因参与防御？

Group A silences X but retains Y and has 90% survival; group B silences Y and has 5%. Which gene is implicated?

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

Y；关闭Y伴随防御下降，但还需注意完整实验设计和对照。

Y: silencing it accompanies lost defense, subject to the full design and controls.

练习出处 / Practice source: 本手册原创，基于 S099 的考点；不是官方原题。 / Created for this guide from the linked skill; not an official question.

S106独立推导 / Inferred

系外行星的双条件分类Classifying exoplanets using two criteria

知识点：表格读取 · 双条件分类 · 单位区分
Skills: Reading tables · Classification using two criteria · Distinguishing units
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

热木星要求质量至少0.25木星且周期小于10天；暖木星满足同一质量标准但周期大于10天。表中五颗质量为0.88、0.99、6.33、0.85、2.37，周期为5.003、5.331、3.409、10.180、4.720天。

Hot Jupiters have mass at least 0.25 Jupiters and periods below ten days; warm Jupiters meet the same mass threshold but exceed ten days. Five masses are 0.88, 0.99, 6.33, 0.85, 2.37; periods are 5.003, 5.331, 3.409, 10.180, 4.720 days.

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
D — all meet the mass threshold; only TOI-1478 b exceeds ten days
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

独立推导：所有质量均超过0.25；只有 TOI-1478 b 的10.180天超过10，因此四热一暖，选 D。153 是距离（秒差距），不是周期；半径不参与分类。

Independent derivation: Every mass exceeds 0.25, and only TOI-1478 b has a period above ten days, establishing four hot and one warm Jupiter, D. The value 153 is distance in parsecs; radius is irrelevant.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

常见错因是看错列、只检查一个条件，或把10.180当成小于10。

Common traps include reading the wrong column or checking only one criterion.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

质量0.6木星、周期12天的行星属于哪类？

Classify a planet with mass 0.6 Jupiters and an orbital period of 12 days.

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

暖木星：0.6≥0.25，且12>10。

Warm Jupiter: 0.6 ≥ 0.25 and 12 > 10.

练习出处 / Practice source: 本手册原创，基于 S106 的考点；不是官方原题。 / Created for this guide from the linked skill; not an official question.

S107独立推导 / Inferred

抗生素处理的金属含量比较Metal content across antibiotic treatments

知识点：分组柱状图 · 组间比较
Skills: Grouped bar charts · Between-group comparisons
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

图示无卡那霉素时锌约390、铁约620 ppm；有卡那霉素时锌约300、铁约225 ppm。选支持“处理影响金属吸收”的数据。

Without kanamycin, zinc is about 390 ppm and iron about 620 ppm; with kanamycin, they are about 300 and 225 ppm. Identify evidence that treatment alters metal uptake.

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
D — exposed plants had less iron and zinc
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

独立推导（已核看原图）：处理组两种金属都比对照组低，D 直接呈现处理相关变化。B 虽数值上成立，却未比较差异；A、C 颠倒了关系。

Independent derivation, checked against the image: Both metals are lower with kanamycin, so D directly documents treatment-related differences. B is numerically true but does not establish a difference; A and C reverse relationships.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

常见错因是只验证一个数值陈述为真，却不判断它是否支持研究假说。

A common trap is choosing a true statement that does not support the hypothesis.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

对照组铁50、锌30；处理组铁35、锌20。用一句话支持“处理改变含量”。

Controls have iron 50 and zinc 30; treated plants have iron 35 and zinc 20. State evidence that treatment changes metal content.

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

处理组两种金属含量均较低，分别下降15和10。

Both metals are lower in treated plants, by 15 and 10 respectively.

S121独立推导 / Inferred

反向指标与距今年代轴Inverse proxies and years-before-present axes

知识点：负相关指标 · 时间轴方向 · 局部变化
Skills: Inversely related indicators · Direction of a time axis · Local changes
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

锰钙比与含氧量负相关。Alboran 珊瑚约9000年前下降，Mauritania 约11000年前下降；图中前者10千年至8千年的比值约33升至83，后者约16附近变化很小。

Manganese/calcium inversely tracks oxygenation. Coral declined about 9,000 years ago at Alboran and 11,000 years ago off Mauritania. Around these dates, Alboran's ratio rises from about 33 to 83 between 10 and 8 thousand years ago, while Mauritania's remains near 16.

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
B — oxygen declined at Alboran's coral decline but barely changed at Mauritania's
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

独立推导（已核图）：时间由更久远走向现在，应从10千年读到8千年；锰钙比上升意味着氧下降。Mauritania 同期相应局部变化小，故 B。C 错说 Mauritania 氧低，混淆比值与氧。

Independent derivation, image-checked: Moving forward in time means reading from 10 to 8 thousand years before present. A rising ratio means falling oxygen. Mauritania shows little local change, supporting B. C incorrectly interprets its low ratio as low oxygen.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

常见错因是把反向代理指标当成氧本身，或按横轴从左到右当作时间前进。

Common traps are treating the inverse proxy as oxygen itself and reading left-to-right as forward time.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

某负相关含氧指标从距今6000年的20升至4000年的50，含氧量怎样变？

An inverse oxygen proxy rises from 20 at 6,000 years before present to 50 at 4,000. How did oxygen change?

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

随时间前进，含氧量下降，因为代理指标与氧负相关。

Oxygen decreased over time because the proxy is inversely related to oxygen.

练习出处 / Practice source: 本手册原创，基于 S121 的考点；不是官方原题。 / Created for this guide from the linked skill; not an official question.
原题出处 / Uploaded source: 用户提供截图 S121 · Reading and Writing: Question 9。正式试卷、模块与官方题库ID未核实。 / Test, module, and official item IDs are unverified.
S123独立推导 / Inferred

跨方法比较最差与最佳性能Comparing the worst and best across methods

知识点：图例识别 · 极值交叉比较 · 证据相关性
Skills: Interpreting graph legends · Comparing extremes across groups · Relevance of evidence
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

喷涂 ETL 的最低和最高效率约15.5%、17.2%；旋涂约11.7%、13.6%。选支持喷涂有改进潜力的数据。

Spray-coated ETLs have minimum and maximum efficiencies around 15.5% and 17.2%; spin-coated ETLs around 11.7% and 13.6%. Choose evidence favoring spray coating.

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
B — the worst spray-coated layer outperformed the best spin-coated layer
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

独立推导（已核图）：喷涂最低值15.5%仍高于旋涂最高值13.6%，故 B 提供直接而强的组间比较。A 两者都超过10%不证明优劣；C 数字错；D 只比较喷涂内部。

Independent derivation, image-checked: Even the minimum spray result, about 15.5%, exceeds the maximum spin result, about 13.6%, making B strong direct evidence. A does not distinguish methods, C misreads values, and D compares only within spray coating.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

常见错因是选择真实但不相关的组内波动，或忽略柱形颜色对应的方法。

Common traps are selecting within-method variation or misreading the legend.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

方法甲成绩范围80—90，乙范围60—75。用极值比较说明甲优势。

Method A scores range from 80 to 90; Method B from 60 to 75. State an extreme-value comparison favoring A.

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

甲最低80仍高于乙最高75，所给数据范围完全分离。

A's minimum of 80 exceeds B's maximum of 75; the observed ranges do not overlap.

练习出处 / Practice source: 本手册原创，基于 S123 的考点；不是官方原题。 / Created for this guide from the linked skill; not an official question.

S140独立推导 / Inferred

按表格找最大值Finding a maximum in a table

知识点：数值排序 · 表头单位
Skills: Ordering numerical values · Units in table headings
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

2022年1—4月美国住房开工数依次为1669、1771、1713、1803（千套）。哪个月最多？

US housing starts from January through April 2022 were 1,669, 1,771, 1,713, and 1,803 thousand. Which month had the most?

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
A — April
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

独立推导（已核图纠正OCR）：4月是1803，不是模糊识别的其他数字；1803>1771>1713>1669，最大值对应 April，选 A。单位千套不影响排序。

Independent derivation, image-checked: April's value is 1,803. Since 1,803 > 1,771 > 1,713 > 1,669, April is highest, A. The common thousands unit does not change the ranking.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

常见错因是只比较前几行、把1771误认最大，或将行标签与数值错位。

Common traps are stopping before the final row or mismatching values with month labels.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

三个月销量分别为245、287、269千件，哪个月最多？

Sales for three successive months are 245, 287, and 269 thousand units. Which month is highest?

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

第二个月；287大于245和269。

The second month, because 287 exceeds both 245 and 269.

S155独立推导 / Inferred

潮汐锁定与表格单位Tidal locking and table units

知识点：表格定位 · 定义应用 · 单位识别
Skills: Locating information in tables · Applying definitions · Identifying units
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

TRAPPIST-1 各行星自转周期等于公转周期；表中1e公转6.1天、半径0.92地球半径。完成例子。

TRAPPIST-1 planets have equal rotational and orbital periods; planet 1e has an orbital period of 6.1 days and radius 0.92 Earth radii. Complete the example.

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
B — orbital and rotational periods are both 6.1 days
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

独立推导：先读取1e正确行的6.1天，再应用自转=公转，得到两者都是6.1天，B。0.92是半径，不是时间；1.51、9.21属于其他行星。

Independent derivation: Read 6.1 days from the 1e row, then apply rotation = orbit to obtain 6.1 days for both, B. The value 0.92 is radius, not a period; 1.51 and 9.21 belong to other planets.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

常见错因是取对行但取错列，把半径数字当自转周期。

A common trap is finding the correct row but using the radius column as a period.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

潮汐锁定行星公转8天、半径1.2地球半径，自转多久？

A tidally locked planet orbits in 8 days and has radius 1.2 Earth radii. What is its rotational period?

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

8天。潮汐锁定给出周期相等，半径在此无关。

8 days. Tidal locking supplies equal periods; radius is irrelevant here.

原题出处 / Uploaded source: 用户提供截图 S155 · Reading and Writing: Question 12。正式试卷、模块与官方题库ID未核实。 / Test, module, and official item IDs are unverified.
05

推断与科学解释Inferences and explanations

23 题组 / question groups
知识点总则 / Core principles

只推出证据允许的最小结论。新证据可削弱解释，不一定证明反向因果；指标的解释力取决于是否存在替代来源。比较两个处理组时解释边际差异，不要换成处理组对控制组。旧物年龄不等于发现地点年龄。

Make the smallest supported inference. Weakening a cause does not prove its opposite. A marker's meaning depends on alternative sources. Explain the actual comparison, and do not equate an object's age with the age of its discovery site.

常见错因 / Common traps

always、all、only等绝对化；倒置充分必要关系；加入没有资料的跨物种比较。

Absolute claims; reversed implications; unsupported comparisons or causal mechanisms.

S005独立推导 / Inferred

Zelda：身份不能被单一角色覆盖Zelda's independent literary contributions

知识点：限定推断 · 完整范围
Skills: Restricted inference · The full range of contributions
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

Zelda不仅启发F. Scott的写作，自己也写小说和短篇。把她主要视为灵感来源有何风险？

Zelda both influenced her husband's work and authored her own fiction. What is lost by treating her primarily as an inspiration?

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
B
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

结论须保留她作为独立作家的贡献：单一定位会歪曲或缩减其文学贡献的完整范围。文本未说明两人如何评价彼此，也未证明作品自传性。

That framing misrepresents the full range of her literary contributions. It does not establish the spouses' opinions of each other or the autobiographical nature of their works.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

用熟悉的名人背景代替文中证据，加入婚姻评价。

Importing biographical assumptions or relationship judgments.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

A scientist advised a famous inventor but also patented three devices herself. Why is calling her only an adviser incomplete？

A scientist advised a famous inventor but also patented three devices herself. Why is calling her only an adviser incomplete?

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

忽略她独立的发明贡献。

It omits her independent inventive contributions.

S007独立推导 / Inferred

蜥脚类与二氧化碳解释Sauropods and the carbon-dioxide hypothesis

知识点：削弱解释 · 避免反向因果
Skills: Weakening an explanation · Avoiding unsupported reverse causality
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

若体型巨大源于二氧化碳增加，却在关键体型演化时期找不到相应二氧化碳峰值，应得出什么谨慎结论？

If rising CO₂ explains gigantism, but relevant evolutionary periods lack matching CO₂ spikes, what follows?

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
B
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

证据削弱‘依赖二氧化碳上升’这一解释，支持B。不能由‘没发现上升’推出‘更高二氧化碳一定阻止巨型化’，也不能补出各谱系的差异。

The missing predicted pattern undermines dependence on increased CO₂. It does not prove that higher CO₂ prevents gigantism or establish lineage-specific effects.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

否定一个解释后，自动肯定相反方向的因果关系。

Assuming that weakening a proposed cause proves the opposite causal effect.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

理论预测大型鱼出现时含氧量上升；对应时期未见上升。这能证明高氧抑制生长吗？

A theory predicts oxygen spikes when giant fish evolve; none are found. Does this prove oxygen suppresses growth?

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

不能；它只削弱该理论的这项预测。

No. It weakens that prediction, not establishes an opposite effect.

练习出处 / Practice source: 本手册原创，基于 S007 的考点；不是官方原题。 / Created for this guide from the linked skill; not an official question.

S018 · S143独立推导 / Inferred

独角鲸：解释处理组无差异Narwhals: explaining similar treatments

知识点：比较对象 · 增量效应 · 解释实验结果 · 排除矛盾选项
Skills: Identifying comparators · Incremental effects · Explaining experimental findings · Eliminating contradictory choices
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

船噪与船噪加声脉冲都减少深潜，两个噪声处理之间差异很小。

Ship noise and ship noise plus pulses both reduce deep dives; the two treatments differ little.

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
B
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

需解释两个处理近似，而不是再次证明噪声相对自然条件有害。B说船噪占总声级主导，新增脉冲改变很小，直接解释边际效应。

Explain similarity between treatments, not the already-established difference from natural conditions. Dominant ship noise leaves little incremental effect for added pulses.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

换错比较对象，把‘处理A对处理B’答成‘处理组对自然组’。

Answering a treatment-versus-control question instead of a treatment-versus-treatment question.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

强灯与强灯加小灯的照明几乎相同。一个合理解释是什么？

A bright lamp and that lamp plus a tiny lamp provide nearly identical illumination. Give a plausible explanation.

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

强灯主导总亮度，小灯的新增贡献很小。

The bright lamp dominates total illumination; the added contribution is small.

练习出处 / Practice source: 本手册原创，基于 S018 · S143 的考点；不是官方原题。 / Created for this guide from the linked skill; not an official question.
原题出处 / Uploaded source: 用户提供截图 S018 · S143 · Reading and Writing: Question 15。正式试卷、模块与官方题库ID未核实。 / Test, module, and official item IDs are unverified.
S034 · S064独立推导 / Inferred

微生物：排除替代解释Microbes: ruling out an alternative explanation

知识点：研究设计 · 机制归因
Skills: Research design · Attributing mechanisms
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

移植到较暖环境后养分循环加快，但微生物群落组成不变。为何组成不变重要？

Nutrient cycling accelerates in warmer transplanted soil while microbial community composition remains unchanged.

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
C
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

若组成改变，循环加快可能来自‘换成更活跃的物种’。组成不变排除这一候选解释，更支持温度增加原群落活动这一机制；并非凭此排除所有其他因素。

Composition change could explain acceleration through different organisms. Its stability removes that alternative and supports increased activity in the existing community, without eliminating every possible confounder.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

把控制替代机制的证据，当成直接重复结果的证据。

Treating evidence about an alternative mechanism as merely a restatement of the outcome.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

同一批机器升级软件后产量提高，机器数量与型号不变。‘型号不变’排除了哪种解释？

Output rises after software changes while machine numbers and models stay the same. What explanation is weakened?

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

产量提高来自换用更高效机器的解释。

That the gain came from replacing machines with more efficient models.

练习出处 / Practice source: 本手册原创，基于 S034 · S064 的考点；不是官方原题。 / Created for this guide from the linked skill; not an official question.

S038 · S050独立推导 / Inferred

翼龙：解剖特征限制飞行Pterosaur anatomy and locomotion

知识点：谨慎推断 · 比较范围
Skills: Cautious inference · Scope of comparison
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

长后腿、短翼、长颈及大头冠，使长时间飞行困难、行走较舒适。

Long hind legs, short wings, a long neck, and a large crest make sustained flight difficult and walking more comfortable.

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
D
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

合理更新是比研究者先前想象飞得更短、走得更多。文本比较对象是旧认识，不提供与其他物种翼长或飞程的具体比较，也未改写已知头部大小。

The warranted update is shorter flights and more walking than previously thought. The text does not supply specific interspecies comparisons or a revised head size.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

将对一个物种的推断扩展为没有依据的跨物种比较。

Adding unsupported comparisons with other species.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

新骨骼显示某鸟翅膀较预期短。能直接断定它比所有其他鸟飞得短吗？

A new skeleton has shorter wings than expected. Does that establish shorter flights than every other bird?

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

不能；最多据相关功能证据调整对该物种的原先判断。

No. Relevant functional evidence may revise expectations for that species, not rank all species.

S066独立推导 / Inferred

NH₃：指标的适用条件NH₃: when a biosignature is informative

知识点：条件推断 · 替代来源 · 充分必要
Skills: Conditional inference · Alternative sources · Sufficiency and necessity
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

岩石行星上可探测氨可能需要生命活动；迷你海王星可由高温高压非生物产生氨。

Detectable ammonia on rocky planets may require biology; mini-Neptunes can produce it abiotically through pressure and heat.

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
C
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

相同气体在不同环境中的解释价值不同。岩石行星可视为生命标志，迷你海王星则存在非生物替代来源。没有氨也不能反推没有生命，因文中没有说所有生命必产生可探测氨。

The signal depends on context. An abiotic source weakens its diagnostic value on mini-Neptunes. Absence of ammonia does not prove absence of life: universal production by life was not established.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

把‘发现X支持Y’错误反推为‘没有X就没有Y’。

Reversing a one-way implication or treating a possible indicator as necessary.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

花园里有烟可能说明有人烧火，但雾也能造成类似外观。判断烟雾指标时需检查什么？

Smoke-like haze may suggest a fire, but fog can look similar. What must be checked?

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

检查是否存在非火源解释；也不能说没有烟就绝无火。

Check alternative sources; absence of visible smoke does not exclude every fire.

S084独立推导 / Inferred

章鱼花园：由缺失年龄段推生命周期Octopus Garden: inferring a life-stage pattern

知识点：有限推断 · 观测范围
Skills: Bounded inference · Scope of observations
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

地点有成体、卵和刚孵化个体，却没有juveniles，被认为专用于繁殖。

Adults, eggs, and hatchlings are observed, but no juveniles; the site is used for reproduction.

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
A
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

A推断章鱼到中间发育阶段会离开，能解释幼体缺失。没有跨地点蛋大小比较、觅食资料或温度变化中的种群稳定数据。

Departure at an intermediate stage explains the absence of juveniles. The text offers no comparative egg sizes, feeding evidence, or population stability over temperature changes.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

把温暖地点直接想象成理想觅食地，超出观测证据。

Assuming a warm location is a feeding ground without feeding evidence.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

育幼区见卵和新生鱼，却多年无中期幼鱼。一个受证据约束的解释是什么？

A nursery contains eggs and newborn fish but repeatedly no intermediate juveniles. Give a bounded explanation.

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

鱼可能在进入该阶段前离开；不据此编造食物优势。

They may leave before that stage; no feeding advantage is established.

S093独立推导 / Inferred

旧文物与晚建村落：兼容两事实Older artifacts in a later settlement

知识点：协调证据 · 最少新增假设
Skills: Reconciling evidence · Minimal additional assumptions
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

若文物确属13世纪，而聚落确在14世纪建成，怎样使两项证据相容？

Artifacts date to the thirteenth century, while the settlement was founded in the fourteenth. How can both be true?

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
B
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

文物可早在别处制作，之后经贸易或迁移进入聚落。文物年代不等于定居地建立年代。其他选项猜数量、创立者身份或挖掘破损，均不能直接解释时间差。

Objects can predate their arrival through trade or migration. An artifact's age is not the settlement's foundation date; other choices add facts without resolving the chronology.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

默认发现地点就是制作地点，且文物与定居点必须同龄。

Assuming an object's discovery site is its origin and its age matches the site's age.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

1990年建的房子里发现1850年钱币，是否房子必在1850年前建成？

A coin from 1850 is found in a house built in 1990. Must the house predate 1850?

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

不；旧物可被带入新建筑，年代属于物品而非发现地点。

No. An older object may be brought into a newer building.

S098独立推导 / Inferred

海星：基因活动推身体区域Sea stars: inferring body regions from gene activity

知识点：证据更新 · 活动与含量
Skills: Updating an interpretation · Activity versus abundance
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

全身主要表达头部相关基因，后部基因局限边缘，没有相应躯干活动证据。

Head-associated genes are active across the body; some posterior activity is confined to the edges.

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
D
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

证据更新‘无头身体’的旧想法，支持主要像头部、缺少躯干的身体布局。这里测的是基因活动，不是基因总量；也不是推翻双侧对称祖先。

The finding supports a mainly head-like body lacking a trunk, revising the headless-body view. Gene activity is not gene abundance, and the finding does not reject bilateral ancestry.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

把表达活动当基因数量，或把形态新解释当整个起源理论被推翻。

Confusing expression with abundance or overextending a morphological revision into an ancestry reversal.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

某组织具有与根相似的基因表达。能直接说它含更多根基因，或整株植物起源于根吗？

A tissue shows root-like gene expression. Does this prove more root genes or a root origin for the entire plant?

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

都不能；活动模式只支持功能或区域对应方面的推断。

No. Expression supports a regional or functional inference, not those broader claims.

S104独立推导 / Inferred

人物言行与他人反应Character behavior and others' reactions

知识点：人物推断 · 因果证据链
Skills: Character inference · Causal chains of evidence
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

Ochiltree 太太评论熟人时直率得令人吃惊，因此被回避；她知道并为此得意。熟人可能怎样想？

Mrs. Ochiltree's startlingly frank comments lead acquaintances to avoid her, and she takes pride in this. What likely explains their reaction?

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
D — acquaintances are likely offended by her remarks
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

独立推导：文本明确将尖锐评论与他人回避联系起来，支持熟人可能被冒犯，选 D。她并不希望更多陪伴，因此 B 与她的得意相悖；A、C 无依据。

Independent derivation: The stated link between her comments and avoidance supports the inference that people are offended, D. Her pride undermines B; A and C lack textual support.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

常见错因是把一般社交常识当证据，忽略文中明确的因果关系。

A common trap is substituting general social assumptions for the passage's explicit causal link.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

“Leo 经常公开嘲笑同事的建议，因此同事不再向他征求意见。”可合理推断什么？

Leo often publicly mocks colleagues' suggestions, so they stop asking his advice. What is a reasonable inference?

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

同事可能不喜欢或受到他的评论伤害；不能推断他们都缺乏好建议。

His colleagues likely dislike or feel hurt by his remarks; this does not show that their ideas are poor.

S118独立推导 / Inferred

化石发现扩展地理分布A fossil expands a known geographic range

知识点：新旧信息对比 · 研究意义
Skills: Comparing new and previous information · Research significance
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

此前海蝎某科的化石仅见于劳亚古陆；新种在冈瓦纳古陆发现。问发现意义。

Previously, mixopterid fossils were known only from Laurussia; a new species was found from Gondwana. Why is this significant?

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
D — first evidence of mixopterids outside Laurussia
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

独立推导：新增另一古陆的记录，将已知地理分布扩展到劳亚之外，D 精确对应。超过4亿年并不自动证明最早年代；亲缘关系与演化时间线不是文中发现重点。

Independent derivation: Evidence from another paleocontinent extends the known range beyond Laurussia, matching D. An age over 400 million years does not establish a new earliest date, and neither kinship nor an evolutionary timeline is the stated advance.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

常见错因是选择包含醒目数字的选项，而忽视作者明确指出的地理意义。

A common trap is selecting a striking date instead of the explicitly stated geographic contribution.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

某恐龙此前仅在亚洲有化石，现于南美发现。直接扩展了哪方面认识？

A dinosaur previously known only from Asian fossils is discovered in South America. What knowledge is directly expanded?

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

其已知地理分布；不能仅据此说南美种群更早。

Its known geographic distribution, not necessarily its earliest date.

S119独立推导 / Inferred

文化语境解释隐喻Interpreting metaphor through cultural context

知识点：文化隐喻 · 限定词与证据
Skills: Culturally grounded metaphors · Qualifiers and supporting evidence
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

纳瓦特尔语“鹰、豹”这一双名词隐喻指战士，字面联系可能薄弱，但阿兹特克仪式文化可解释它。问最有支持的判断。

A Nahuatl paired-noun metaphor meaning eagle, jaguar signifies warrior; the link may be obscure literally but intelligible through Aztec ceremonial associations. Which inference is supported?

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
C — the obscurity is resolved through cultural context
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

独立推导：文本明确说这种表达往往依靠文化概念关联才可理解，故 C。两个名词不语义相等；文本未说其形式功能被忽视，也未以使用频率证明可理解性。

Independent derivation: The text explicitly grounds intelligibility in ceremonial cultural associations, supporting C. It does not claim noun equivalence, unnoticed formal structure, or frequency as proof of understanding.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

常见错因是坚持字面释义，或把“常见”误当充分解释其隐喻含义的证据。

A common trap is insisting on literal meanings or treating frequency as proof of metaphorical intelligibility.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

某文化用“炉火”代指家庭，因炉边是家庭聚会中心。理解这一隐喻最需要什么？

A culture uses hearth to mean family because households gather around it. What most helps explain the metaphor?

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

炉火与家庭生活之间的文化关联，而不是认为两词字面相同。

The cultural association between hearths and family life, not literal synonymy.

S124独立推导 / Inferred

反例限制适应性解释A counterexample limits an adaptive explanation

知识点：反例推理 · 必要关系 · 避免过度推断
Skills: Reasoning from counterexamples · Necessary conditions · Avoiding overgeneralization
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

社会性且育幼的动物幼体偏好面孔；独居、不育幼的陆龟幼体也偏好面孔图样。这意味着什么？

Newborn social animals with parental care prefer faces; solitary tortoise hatchlings without parental care also prefer face-like patterns. What follows?

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
B — face preference is not necessarily tied to parental care or sociality
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

独立推导：同样偏好出现在无社会育幼环境的物种中，说明不能认定该偏好必然为社会互动或育幼适应，选 B。不能据此认定社会物种是后天学习；未比较成体，也未测威胁感。

Independent derivation: The preference also occurs without sociality or parental care, so it is not necessarily an adaptation for those conditions, B. The study does not establish learning in social species, adult preferences, or perceived threat.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

常见错因是把“并非必然”扩大为“完全无关”或“另一物种必定后天习得”。

A common trap is expanding not necessarily into no relationship at all or into claims about learning.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

有人说夜行性是拥有特征T的必要条件；一种昼行动物也有T。能得出什么？

Someone claims nocturnality is necessary for trait T; a diurnal species also has T. What follows?

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

夜行性并非T的必要条件；不能说所有昼行动物都有T。

Nocturnality is not necessary for T; this does not mean all diurnal species have T.

练习出处 / Practice source: 本手册原创，基于 S124 的考点；不是官方原题。 / Created for this guide from the linked skill; not an official question.

S126独立推导 / Inferred

从有效机制推导研究方向Deriving research priorities from an effective mechanism

知识点：机制推理 · 结论与研究目标
Skills: Reasoning from mechanisms · Connecting findings to research objectives
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

EBF 激活单一 Or31 受体即可驱蚊，且所有已知传病蚊种都有该受体。新驱蚊剂开发应优先研究什么？

EBF repels mosquitoes by activating Or31, a receptor present in all known disease-carrying mosquito species. What should new repellent research prioritize?

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
A — seek EBF-like molecules that activate Or31
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

独立推导：既然 Or31 是已发现有效且广泛存在的靶点，寻找类似 EBF、可激活 Or31 的分子最直接利用证据，故 A。提取方法、受体位置或激活受体数量均非论证重点。

Independent derivation: Seeking EBF-like molecules that activate the effective, widespread Or31 target directly builds on the finding, A. Extraction methods, receptor locations, and maximizing the number of receptors do not address the stated mechanism.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

常见错因是认为激活越多受体越好，忽略研究的突破正是单一有效靶点。

A common trap is assuming that activating more receptors is better despite the finding about one effective target.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

药物P通过抑制酶E阻止病原体生长，E见于多个菌株。合理后续方向是什么？

Drug P blocks pathogen growth by inhibiting enzyme E, present in several strains. What is a reasonable research direction?

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

筛选其他能有效抑制E的分子，利用已知作用机制。

Screen other molecules that effectively inhibit E, building on the established mechanism.

练习出处 / Practice source: 本手册原创，基于 S126 的考点；不是官方原题。 / Created for this guide from the linked skill; not an official question.
原题出处 / Uploaded source: 用户提供截图 S126 · Reading and Writing: Question 14。正式试卷、模块与官方题库ID未核实。 / Test, module, and official item IDs are unverified.
S129独立推导 / Inferred

传统艺术元素与原创组合Traditional elements in original art

知识点：概括艺术方法 · 证据边界
Skills: Summarizing an artistic approach · Limits of the evidence
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

Gibson 将原住民珠饰、皮革流苏与金属铃锥装饰用于拳击沙袋，挑战性别角色区分。概括其方法。

Gibson decorates punching bags with Native beadwork, fringe, and jingles, challenging gender divisions. Summarize his artistic approach.

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
A — draws on traditional Native art forms for original works
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

独立推导：他将传统原住民艺术形式重组进原创作品，A 有直接依据。文本未比较不同艺术家的影响；他不是设计拳击风连衣裙，也未讨论色彩与图案期待。

Independent derivation: He incorporates traditional Native art forms into original works, supporting A. The passage does not compare artistic influences, describe designing dresses inspired by boxing, or discuss color expectations.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

常见错因是把装饰元素的来源“舞裙”误当成他实际制作的作品。

A common trap is confusing the source of decorative elements with the object he actually creates.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

艺术家把传统剪纸图样刻在现代金属灯上。用一句话概括方法。

An artist engraves traditional paper-cut patterns on modern metal lamps. Summarize the approach.

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

将传统艺术元素融入现代原创物件；不能说她在制作纸灯。

She integrates traditional artistic elements into modern original objects; this does not mean she makes paper lamps.

S141独立推导 / Inferred

熟悉组织结构与择业偏好Organizational familiarity and job preferences

知识点：解释现象 · 因果链补全
Skills: Explaining an observed pattern · Completing a causal chain
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

退伍军人在文职政府岗位占比高；军旅经历使其熟悉类似的组织结构。熟悉感如何解释这一现象？

Veterans are overrepresented in civilian government jobs, and military service familiarizes them with similar organizational structures. How could familiarity explain the pattern?

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
A — civilian government jobs appeal especially to veterans
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

独立推导：熟悉结构可使这些岗位更有吸引力，从而增加退伍军人选择它们的可能，A。C 转向非退伍者，D 声称岗位要求增加，B 过于抽象而未解释具体方向。

Independent derivation: Familiarity could make such jobs more appealing and thus encourage veterans to choose them, A. C concerns nonveterans, D invents changing job requirements, and B fails to supply the specific explanatory direction.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

常见错因是把求职者偏好机制错写成雇主新增资格要求。

A common trap is replacing an applicant-preference explanation with an unsupported employer requirement.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

曾做餐饮的人更常应聘餐厅管理，原因可能是熟悉流程。补全因果链。

People with restaurant experience often apply for restaurant management because they know the workflows. Complete the causal link.

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

流程熟悉使管理岗位更易理解或更有吸引力，因此提高应聘意愿。

Familiar workflows can make the role more understandable or appealing, increasing willingness to apply.

练习出处 / Practice source: 本手册原创，基于 S141 的考点；不是官方原题。 / Created for this guide from the linked skill; not an official question.

S152独立推导 / Inferred

讽刺性人物描写Ironic characterization

知识点：讽刺语气 · 外貌与实绩对比
Skills: Ironic tone · Contrasting appearance with achievement
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

叙述者写 Sir Winston Day 外貌适合做雕像或邮票，却难以知道其半世纪公职有何成就，回忆录也迟迟不说。怎样概括？

Sir Winston Day looks suitable for a bust or stamp, yet his achievements remain unclear after decades of public service and a slow memoir. How is he characterized?

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
A — distinguished appearance but uncertain achievements
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

独立推导：庄重外貌与模糊实绩的反差构成讽刺，A 准确保留“是否配得声望尚不清楚”。B 虚构不光彩行为；C 把 state secrets 的玩笑读成真实保密；D 无能干谦逊的证据。

Independent derivation: The contrast between imposing appearance and unclear accomplishments is ironic, supporting A. B invents dishonorable actions, C literalizes the state-secrets joke, and D assumes competence and modesty without evidence.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

常见错因是把夸张讽刺当事实，或把对成就的质疑扩大成确有丑闻。

Common traps are literalizing irony and converting uncertainty about achievements into proven scandal.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

“他办公室奖杯满架，可没人说得出他完成过什么项目。”主要反差是什么？

His office is full of trophies, yet nobody can name a project he completed. What contrast is emphasized?

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

成功的外在表象与缺乏可确认成就之间的反差；不等于证实奖杯造假。

The contrast between outward signs of success and unverified achievement; it does not prove the trophies are fraudulent.

S157独立推导 / Inferred

模型假设与预测偏差Model assumptions and prediction error

知识点：模型局限 · 未知参数 · 避免无依据定向
Skills: Model limitations · Unknown parameters · Avoiding unsupported claims about error direction
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

Titan 甲烷观测稀疏且不一致；模型将最低大气层甲烷比例设为均一值，而该比例影响降水湿度。应提醒什么？

Sparse, inconsistent Titan observations led a model to prescribe uniform low-level methane, which affects precipitation and humidity. What caveat follows?

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
A — some mismatches in predicted precipitation and humidity are expected
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

独立推导：简化且受约束不足的参数可能造成模拟与真实降水湿度不一致，A。不能判断真实值必然更高；B 说未来才可用单值，但模型已用单值；D 将真实变化误当固定模型内部波动的直接来源。

Independent derivation: An insufficiently constrained simplification can yield mismatches between simulated and actual weather, A. No evidence fixes the error's direction. B overlooks the existing single value; D attributes within-model inconsistency directly to real variation not represented by the uniform assumption.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

常见错因是把“参数不确定”擅自推成“一定低估”或“一定高估”。

A common trap is converting uncertain parameters into a definite direction of error.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

模型将整城降雨设为相同，但真实降雨空间差异未知。能合理提醒什么？

A model assumes uniform rainfall across a city, while actual spatial variation is poorly known. What caveat is justified?

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

局部预测可能偏离实际，但无证据不能说所有地区都低估。

Local predictions may differ from reality, but the information does not show universal underestimation.

练习出处 / Practice source: 本手册原创，基于 S157 的考点；不是官方原题。 / Created for this guide from the linked skill; not an official question.

S169独立推导 / Inferred

工具改进与缝线粗细Tool improvements and seam fineness

知识点：比较方向 · 技术机制
Skills: Direction of comparisons · Technological mechanisms
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

骨锥先打孔再手穿绳；有孔针将两步合一，允许更小孔、更细接缝。早期骨锥制衣与针制衣相比怎样？

Awls required punching holes and manually threading cord; eyed needles combined the steps and allowed smaller holes and finer seams. Compare awl-made and needle-made clothing.

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
B — awl-made clothing likely had coarser seams
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

独立推导：针允许更细接缝，因此骨锥制衣相对更粗，B。A 倒置精细程度；C 倒置速度优势；D 交换地点对应的工具。

Independent derivation: Since needles enabled finer seams, awl-made clothing likely had coarser seams, B. A reverses fineness, C reverses speed, and D swaps the tools associated with the sites.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

常见错因是比较方向反转，或记住地点但交换工具对应。

Common traps are reversing the comparison and swapping site-tool associations.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

新钻头可钻比旧钻头更小的孔。旧钻头形成的孔相对怎样？

A new drill bit can make smaller holes than the old one. How do holes made with the old bit compare?

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

相对更大；只从已给比较推导，不必推测材料强度。

They are relatively larger; the comparison does not establish material strength.

S179独立推导 / Inferred

根据森林规模应用模型偏差Applying a model's bias by forest size

知识点：条件规则应用 · 保持变量对应
Skills: Applying conditional rules · Keeping variables correctly matched
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

模型在大森林高估大型哺乳动物，在小森林高估小型哺乳动物。Tuskegee 是不足10万英亩的小森林；预测有何偏差？

The model overpredicts large mammals in large forests and small mammals in small forests. Tuskegee is a small forest below 100,000 acres. What bias is expected?

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
C — overestimate small-mammal populations
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

独立推导：先用小森林条件定位第二条规则，再保留小型哺乳动物与数量高估，得到 C。不能改成大型动物、短冬效应大小或捕食关系，这些并非相应规则。

Independent derivation: Small forest selects the second rule: small-mammal population estimates are too high, C. The statement does not establish a large-mammal bias, an exaggerated winter effect, or ignored predator-prey relationships there.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

常见错因是将森林规模与动物大小交叉错配，或把预测数量偏差换成效应偏差。

Common traps are mismatching forest and mammal size and substituting effect-size error for population-size error.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

模型在小湖高估鱼苗，在大湖高估成鱼。某小湖应警惕哪种误差？

A model overpredicts juvenile fish in small lakes and adult fish in large lakes. What error should be expected for a small lake?

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

高估鱼苗数量；小湖条件对应鱼苗，不对应成鱼。

Overestimating juvenile fish numbers; the small-lake condition selects juveniles, not adults.

S189独立推导 / Inferred

区分语言起源地与传播中转地Distinguishing language origins from a diffusion hub

知识点：起源与传播区分 · 保留部分地位 · 年代比较
Skills: Distinguishing origins from diffusion · Recognizing a retained partial role · Comparing dates
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

草原假说称印欧语约6000年前起源于黑海—里海草原；新研究给出约8120年，并称一大分支从别处北上传入草原，再扩散欧洲。说明什么？

The steppe hypothesis places Indo-European origins on the Pontic-Caspian Steppe about 6,000 years ago; a new study dates it to about 8,120 years and routes a major branch northward to the steppe before European expansion. What follows?

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
B — incompatible origins, but the steppe remains important in diffusion
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

独立推导：新研究与原起源时间、地点不一致，但仍赋予草原重要传播角色，B。A 错说小分支在草原起源；C 无受欢迎程度比较；D 无证据证明旧方法较不可靠。

Independent derivation: The new origin account conflicts with the steppe hypothesis but preserves a major diffusion role for the steppe, B. A changes a major incoming branch into a minor local origin; C and D add unsupported popularity and reliability judgments.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

常见错因是把传播的重要地区当成最初起源地，或认为新方法必然更可靠。

Common traps are equating a diffusion hub with an origin and assuming novelty proves methodological superiority.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

作物原被认为起源于港城；新证据说起源内陆、后经港城传播。港城地位是否完全消失？

A crop was thought to originate in a port; new evidence places its origin inland but its later spread through the port. Has the port lost all importance?

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

没有。起源假说被挑战，但传播枢纽角色仍可成立。

No. The origin claim is challenged, but the port can remain an important diffusion hub.

S191独立推导 / Inferred

有条件心理机制与谨慎推论A conditional psychological mechanism and a cautious inference

知识点：条件推论 · 避免意识与因果过推
Skills: Conditional inference · Avoiding unsupported claims about awareness and causation
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

红色常与力量及谨慎反应相关；若这种心理关系适用于比赛，曼联红色主场球衣可能怎样影响对手？

Red is associated with dominance and cautious responses. If these findings apply to sports, what might Manchester United's red home uniforms have done to opponents?

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
A — opponents may have been influenced by red uniforms
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

独立推导：A 仅说对手可能受到颜色影响，严格保留 if applicable 与 may 的限定。B 额外要求对手意识到影响；C、D 虚构球迷或自身球衣的效应。材料并未单靠胜率证明颜色导致胜利。

Independent derivation: A cautiously says opponents may have been influenced, preserving the conditional claim. B adds conscious awareness; C and D invent effects involving fans or opponents' own uniforms. Win records alone do not prove a causal color effect.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

常见错因是把可能的无意识影响说成对手明确知道，或把相关胜率当因果证明。

Common traps are adding conscious awareness and converting an association with wins into proven causation.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

若低音会让人感到威胁，广告低音可能影响听众。能否直接说听众知道自己受影响？

If low sounds can feel threatening, low-pitched advertisements might influence listeners. Does that show listeners know they are being influenced?

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

不能。存在影响与意识到影响是两个不同主张。

No. Being influenced and being aware of that influence are separate claims.

S192独立推导 / Inferred

生态位差异缓解同种竞争Niche differentiation offsets conspecific competition

知识点：生态机制 · 负相关方向 · 让步补偿
Skills: Ecological mechanisms · Direction of negative correlations · Conceding a disadvantage and explaining compensation
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

温带稀有树种反而较常同种聚集，可能失去稀有种竞争优势；模型说生态位差异可缓解风险。如何补全？

Rare temperate tree species tend to aggregate with conspecifics, potentially weakening rare-species advantage. Modeling suggests niche differences can offset the risk. Complete the mechanism.

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
D — rare temperate species can exploit resources underused by other species
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

独立推导：D 承认稀有树种局部同种竞争较强，但可使用其他物种少用的资源而繁荣，体现生态位补偿。B 直接称稀有种优势更强；C 倒置丰度与聚集的负相关；A 未说明关键资源分化。

Independent derivation: D retains elevated local conspecific competition while explaining survival through resources underused by other species. B simply asserts greater RSA, C reverses the negative abundance-aggregation relationship, and A misses the key resource-partitioning mechanism.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

常见错因是只看整体物种稀有就认为局部同种个体少，忽略局部聚集。

A common trap is equating regional rarity with few nearby conspecifics despite local clustering.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

稀有植物彼此聚集、同种竞争大，但根深入其他植物够不到的土层。什么机制可能帮助存活？

A rare plant clusters with conspecifics but reaches soil layers inaccessible to other species. What mechanism could help it persist?

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

生态位分化：利用其他物种少用的深层资源，可部分抵消局部竞争压力。

Niche differentiation: access to underused deep resources can partly offset local competitive pressure.

练习出处 / Practice source: 本手册原创，基于 S192 的考点；不是官方原题。 / Created for this guide from the linked skill; not an official question.
原题出处 / Uploaded source: 用户提供截图 S192 · Rare species advantage (RSA) describes the situation in which there are few conspecifics。正式试卷、模块与官方题库ID未核实。 / Test, module, and official item IDs are unverified.
06

双文本比较Cross-text connections

6 题组 / question groups
知识点总则 / Core principles

分别记录两文的前提、结论与理由，再找回应对象。两文可能同意问题存在，却不同意解决方案；‘不完全由X造成’也不同于‘与X无关’。

Separate each text's premise, conclusion, and reasons. Writers may agree on a diagnosis but disagree on a remedy. Not exclusively caused by X does not mean unrelated to X.

常见错因 / Common traps

把部分同意理解为全面同意；把多因素解释改成完全排除某因素。

Treating partial agreement as total agreement, or a multifactor account as complete rejection of one factor.

S015独立推导 / Inferred

公共艺术：作品与环境Public art and its surroundings

知识点：跨文本回应 · 非排他原因
Skills: Responding across texts · Nonexclusive explanations
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

第二篇强调公众反应也受作品与公共环境融合程度影响。

Text 2 stresses that reception also depends on how art is integrated into a public setting.

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
D
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

因此它会反对‘争议反应完全由作品自身属性造成’的看法。D的not exclusively保留作品属性的作用，同时加入环境因素；A说unrelated过度否定。

D rejects an exclusively intrinsic explanation while allowing artistic attributes to matter. Unrelated in A goes too far: context matters, but intrinsic qualities need not be irrelevant.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

把‘不完全由X造成’误读成‘与X完全无关’。

Converting not exclusively caused by X into completely unrelated to X.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

甲说差评来自菜品；乙说服务也影响评分。乙是否认为菜品完全不重要？

A attributes low ratings to food; B says service also matters. Does B find food irrelevant?

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

不是。乙反对单一原因，而非否认该原因的一切作用。

No. B rejects exclusivity, not all contribution from food.

练习出处 / Practice source: 本手册原创，基于 S015 的考点；不是官方原题。 / Created for this guide from the linked skill; not an official question.
原题出处 / Uploaded source: 用户提供截图 S015 · Reading and Writing: Question 7。正式试卷、模块与官方题库ID未核实。 / Test, module, and official item IDs are unverified.
S045独立推导 / Inferred

胡桃夹子：接受前提，反对放弃The Nutcracker: agree on age, disagree on abandonment

知识点：跨文本 · 前提与结论
Skills: Cross-text connections · Premise versus conclusion
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

两文都认为传统版本过时；第二文主张保留并创新，而不是彻底放弃。

Both texts see the traditional show as outdated; Text 2 advocates updating it rather than abandoning it.

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
D
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

D准确呈现回应：提供改编版本。第二文接受‘过时’的诊断，却不同意由此推出‘抛弃’；其收益讨论也没有证明大多数舞团改编一定增收。

D captures revision rather than rejection. Agreement with the diagnosis does not require agreement with the proposed remedy; revenue claims about most companies are unsupported.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

把对同一前提的认同，当成对同一政策结论的认同。

Confusing agreement on a premise with agreement on the conclusion.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

甲：图书馆陈旧，应拆掉。乙：确实陈旧，但应翻新。两人同意和不同意什么？

A: the library is outdated, so demolish it. B: it is outdated, but renovate it. Where do they agree and differ?

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

同意现状诊断，不同意解决方案。

They agree on the diagnosis but disagree on the remedy.

练习出处 / Practice source: 本手册原创，基于 S045 的考点；不是官方原题。 / Created for this guide from the linked skill; not an official question.

S117独立推导 / Inferred

挑战浮游植物竞争的前提Challenging an assumption about phytoplankton competition

知识点：跨文本回应 · 识别论证前提
Skills: Cross-text responses · Identifying argumentative premises
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

文本1认为共享资源应使一种浮游植物胜出；文本2说微小尺度和水的相对黏稠使直接接触竞争很少。文本2会怎样回应？

Text 1 expects one phytoplankton species to outcompete others sharing resources. Text 2 argues that microscopic scale and relatively dense water limit interaction. How would its researchers respond?

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
A — the conventional account misconceives direct competition
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

独立推导：文本2否定的不是多样性，而是“普遍发生直接竞争”的前提，故 A。营养补充、与大型生物竞争均未谈及；水密度限制而非增加互动。

Independent derivation: Text 2 questions the premise of frequent direct competition, so A fits. It does not invoke nutrient replenishment or competition with larger organisms, and density limits rather than increases interaction.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

常见错因是把研究解释补写成常识性营养原因，而未定位两文实际分歧。

A common trap is supplying a plausible nutrient explanation instead of identifying the actual disagreement.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

甲：共享食物的动物必然激烈竞争。乙：这些动物进食时间不同，几乎不碰面。乙质疑什么前提？

A: Animals sharing food must compete intensely. B: They feed at different times and rarely meet. Which premise does B challenge?

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

共享资源必然意味着频繁直接竞争的前提。

The assumption that sharing resources necessarily entails frequent direct competition.

练习出处 / Practice source: 本手册原创，基于 S117 的考点；不是官方原题。 / Created for this guide from the linked skill; not an official question.

S153独立推导 / Inferred

地层年代不等于化学变化年代Rock deposition dates versus later chemical alteration

知识点：跨文本分歧 · 时间归因 · 证据与解释
Skills: Cross-text disagreements · Attributing evidence to a time period · Evidence versus interpretation
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

Anbar 将25亿年前地层中钼增多解释为同时期氧气增加；Slotznick 发现火山碎屑与微裂隙，认为氧化可在沉积很久后发生。两方争论什么？

Anbar treats elevated molybdenum in 2.5-billion-year-old shale as evidence of oxygen rising then; Slotznick's microscopy suggests much later oxidation through microfractures. What do they dispute?

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
B — whether molybdenum indicates an oxygen rise at 2.5 Ga
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

独立推导：双方不必否认钼增多或氧化风化，而是分歧于该化学信号能否对应沉积当时的大气增氧，故 B。A 转向25亿年前之前；C 是共同可能机制；D 错说增多本身不存在。

Independent derivation: The disagreement concerns whether the chemical signal dates atmospheric oxygenation to deposition, B, not whether molybdenum increased or oxidation occurred. A concerns earlier time, C can be shared, and D denies the observation itself.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

常见错因是把“解释的时间不同”误当“是否观察到同一物质变化”的分歧。

A common trap is confusing disagreement about timing with disagreement about the observation.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

古陶器有铁锈：甲说制造时形成，乙说埋藏后渗水形成。争议核心是什么？

An old vessel has rust: A says it formed during manufacture; B says groundwater caused it after burial. What is the central dispute?

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

铁锈形成的时间及是否能代表制造时环境，而不是铁锈是否存在。

When the rust formed and whether it reflects the original environment, not whether rust exists.

练习出处 / Practice source: 本手册原创，基于 S153 的考点；不是官方原题。 / Created for this guide from the linked skill; not an official question.

S166独立推导 / Inferred

诗歌价值不等于专业发表Valuable poetry beyond professional publication

知识点：跨文本回应 · 评价标准区分
Skills: Cross-text responses · Distinguishing evaluation criteria
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

Lorde 认为诗歌成本低，可在工作间隙创作；文本2强调主要诗刊作者往往需要专业训练和财务支持。Lorde 会如何回应？

Lorde argues that poetry can be created cheaply between work shifts; Text 2 emphasizes training and financial security among major-journal poets. How would Lorde respond?

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
B — worthwhile poetry can be made outside the circumstances in Text 2
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

独立推导：Lorde 的重点是创作有价值诗歌的可及性，而非进入专业刊物，故 B：那些专业条件之外仍可创作有价值诗歌。其他选项添加经济回报、工作目的或靠诗谋生等无依据结论。

Independent derivation: Lorde's claim concerns the accessibility of worthwhile creation, not entry into professional journals, so B fits. The alternatives add unsupported claims about earnings, reasons for employment, or making a living from poetry.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

常见错因是把艺术价值、专业训练和知名刊物发表混成同一个标准。

A common trap is conflating artistic value, professional training, and prestigious publication.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

甲说任何人都能用手机拍有价值的电影；乙说电影节导演多受专业训练。甲可怎样回应而不否认乙事实？

A says anyone can make worthwhile films on a phone; B notes that festival directors are usually professionally trained. How can A respond without denying B?

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

有价值的电影也能在电影节与专业训练体系之外产生。

Worthwhile films can also be produced outside the professional-training and festival system.

练习出处 / Practice source: 本手册原创，基于 S166 的考点；不是官方原题。 / Created for this guide from the linked skill; not an official question.

S187独立推导 / Inferred

找出两文共享判断Finding common ground across two texts

知识点：跨文本共同点 · 总体立场与局部共识
Skills: Cross-text common ground · Overall positions versus agreement on specific points
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

文本1批评写作工坊偏好传统结构，并以 Mathis 为例；文本2用 Torres 的实验作品反驳风格单一，但仍称 Mathis 结构传统。两文同意什么？

Text 1 uses Mathis to criticize conventional workshop fiction; Text 2 uses Torres's experimental work to defend diversity while still contrasting it with Mathis. Where do the texts agree?

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
C — The Twelve Tribes of Hattie uses conventional narrative structure
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

独立推导：两文对工坊总体评价不同，却都把 Mathis 的 The Twelve Tribes of Hattie 视作传统叙事，C。没有证据比较多数毕业生，也未说她影响 Torres 或促成工坊改革。

Independent derivation: The authors disagree about the workshop overall but both characterize Mathis's novel as conventionally structured, C. Neither establishes comparisons with most graduates, influence on Torres, or institutional reform.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

常见错因是因两文总体对立就忽略局部共识，或把“不同于”当“受其启发”。

Common traps are missing local agreement within overall disagreement and treating contrast as influence.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

甲说学校风格单一，乙说学校风格多元；两人都说学生P画写实画。他们的共同判断是什么？

A calls a school stylistically uniform; B calls it diverse. Both describe student P as a realist painter. What is their common ground?

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

P的画属于写实风格；这不等于同意学校整体是否多元。

P paints realistically; this does not settle their disagreement about the whole school.

练习出处 / Practice source: 本手册原创，基于 S187 的考点；不是官方原题。 / Created for this guide from the linked skill; not an official question.
原题出处 / Uploaded source: 用户提供截图 S187 · Text 1。正式试卷、模块与官方题库ID未核实。 / Test, module, and official item IDs are unverified.
07

语法与标点Grammar and boundaries

33 题组 / question groups
知识点总则 / Core principles

先删去插入语、关系从句和介词修饰，保留主语与限定谓语。两个独立分句可用句号、分号或合适的逗号＋并列连词连接，不能只用逗号。冒号前通常须为完整句；列表和同位语不自动成为完整句。分词开头要修饰正确主语。

Strip modifiers to reveal subjects and finite predicates. Independent clauses need a valid boundary, not a comma alone. A colon normally follows a complete clause; lists and appositives are not automatically clauses. Introductory modifiers must attach to the right subject.

常见错因 / Common traps

凭停顿选标点；就近配合动词；把to be当限定谓语；把长短语当完整句；悬垂修饰。

Punctuation by pauses; nearest-noun agreement; infinitives used as finite predicates; fragments mistaken for clauses; dangling modifiers.

S006独立推导 / Inferred

Mission 66：分号连接完整句Mission 66: a semicolon between clauses

知识点：独立分句 · 逗号拼接
Skills: Independent clauses · Comma splices
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

前句说Mission 66是对国家公园基础设施的投资；后接 it prioritized physical improvements。

One clause describes the infrastructure investment; the next begins it prioritized physical improvements.

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
C — parks;
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

两侧都有主语和限定动词：initiative represented / it prioritized。分号可连接相关的独立分句。只用逗号是comma splice；无标点是run-on；本题 and 选项缺少连接两个独立分句所需的逗号。

Both sides are independent clauses: initiative represented and it prioritized. A semicolon joins them. A comma alone splices clauses; no boundary fuses them; the offered and lacks the required comma here.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

只凭朗读停顿选逗号，没有先找两组主谓。

Choosing punctuation by a pause instead of clause structure.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

The program expanded ___ it hired more staff. 填分号还是逗号？

The program expanded ___ it hired more staff. Choose a semicolon or a comma.

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

分号：The program expanded; it hired more staff. 两边都是完整句。

A semicolon: both sides can stand as sentences.

练习出处 / Practice source: 本手册原创，基于 S006 的考点；不是官方原题。 / Created for this guide from the linked skill; not an official question.

S008独立推导 / Inferred

岛屿模型：从句缺谓语An island model needs a finite verb

知识点：限定动词 · 从句主干 · 插入定义
Skills: Finite verbs · Embedded clause structure · Inserted definitions
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

A model predicts that the rate of speciation—定义—on an isolated island ___ triple the rate elsewhere。

A model predicts that the rate of speciation—followed by a definition and location modifiers—___ triple another rate.

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
D — will be
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

删除破折号解释及地点修饰，骨架是 predicts that the rate ___ triple the rate。that从句需要自己的限定谓语，will be符合；being、to be、to have been均不能独立作该从句谓语。

Strip away the definition and modifiers: predicts that the rate ___ triple the rate. The embedded clause needs a finite predicate; will be works, whereas the participle and infinitives do not.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

把主句predicts或插入定义里的form误认为that从句已经有谓语。

Mistaking the main-clause verb or a verb inside the definition for the embedded predicate.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

The report predicts that demand near the new station ___ high. 填 being / will be。

The report predicts that demand near the new station ___ high. Choose being or will be.

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

will be。that demand will be high是完整从句。

Will be supplies the finite predicate of that demand will be high.

S020独立推导 / Inferred

青蛙分布：当前事实A frog's current range

知识点：时态 · 时间线索
Skills: Verb tense · Temporal cues
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

Currently, the frog's range ___ parts of two Australian states。

Currently, the frog's range ___ parts of two Australian states.

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
A — includes
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

Currently指目前的客观分布，使用一般现在时includes。主语range为单数。过去、过去完成及将来完成都没有对应时间依据。

Currently anchors a present fact. Singular range takes includes; the past and perfect forms lack a suitable temporal basis.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

把生物物种的历史背景误当此句时态依据。

Letting the species' history override the sentence's present-time marker.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

Currently, the reserve ___ three lakes. 填contains / had contained。

Currently, the reserve ___ three lakes. Choose contains or had contained.

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

contains：描述当前状态。

Contains states the current situation.

S037独立推导 / Inferred

frameworks：长主语的中心词Frameworks: agreement with the head noun

知识点：主谓一致 · 嵌套修饰
Skills: Subject–verb agreement · Nested modifiers
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

the various cultural and ideological frameworks a person has internalized ___ each emotional reaction。

The various cultural and ideological frameworks a person has internalized ___ each emotional reaction.

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
C — underlie
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

主语中心词是复数frameworks，a person has internalized是修饰它的从句，reaction是宾语。应使用复数动词underlie，不随最近的单数名词变化。

Plural frameworks is the head subject. A person has internalized modifies it; reaction is the object. Agreement therefore requires underlie.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

就近配合person或reaction，忽略主语中心词。

Agreeing with a nearby singular noun rather than the head subject.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

The paintings that the curator selected ___ upstairs. 填is / are。

The paintings that the curator selected ___ upstairs. Choose is or are.

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

are，主语是复数paintings，curator在修饰从句内。

Are agrees with paintings; curator belongs to the relative clause.

S051独立推导 / Inferred

微雕：同位语的逗号Microsculptures: an appositive comma

知识点：同位语 · 非完整句
Skills: Appositives · Fragments
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

Wigan以microsculptures闻名，后接creations so small that...来解释这些微雕。

Creations so small that... explains the preceding microsculptures.

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
B — microsculptures, creations
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

creations...是对microsculptures的同位说明，使用逗号连接。Creations so small...本身没有主句谓语，不能在前面用句号制造独立句；and也不应把同一对象写成并列两类。

The creations phrase renames microsculptures, so a comma introduces the appositive. It lacks a main predicate and cannot stand as the offered new sentence.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

看到that从句内的are就误以为整个名词短语可独立成句。

Mistaking a verb inside a relative clause for a main-clause predicate.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

She collects miniatures ___ objects small enough to fit in a matchbox. 填逗号还是句号？

She collects miniatures ___ objects small enough to fit in a matchbox. Choose a comma or period.

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

逗号；后面的objects短语重述miniatures，而非独立句。

A comma introduces the appositive; the objects phrase is not a sentence.

S052独立推导 / Inferred

Vermeer：objects决定动词形式Vermeer: objects controls agreement

知识点：主谓一致 · 插入修饰
Skills: Subject–verb agreement · Interrupting modifiers
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

Such quotidian objects, depicted...by Vermeer, a painter..., ___ the daily minutiae。

Such quotidian objects, followed by descriptive phrases, ___ daily minutiae.

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
D — reveal
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

中心主语是复数objects，删除中间分词短语和同位语后为objects reveal。Vermeer与painter不是主语；reveals、has revealed、was revealing均不与复数主语一致。

The plural head objects requires reveal. Vermeer and painter occur in modifiers, not as subjects; the singular alternatives disagree.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

让离空格最近的单数painter控制主句动词。

Letting the nearby singular painter control agreement.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

These tools, designed by an engineer, ___ energy. 填saves / save。

These tools, designed by an engineer, ___ energy. Choose saves or save.

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

save，与复数tools一致。

Save agrees with plural tools.

S053独立推导 / Inferred

针孔相机：because从句的归属Pinhole camera: attaching a because-clause

知识点：句界 · 从属连词
Skills: Sentence boundaries · Subordinating conjunctions
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

A diagram reveals how this works. Because [原因], all light ... can ... [结果]。本题只考句法。

A diagram reveals how this works. Because [cause], all light ... can ... [result]. The question tests syntax.

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
B — works. Because
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

B先结束完整句，再以Because引出原因状语从句并配上后面的主句。A会让前面完整句与后面的主句之间只剩逗号；D的colon后it's because也未解决后续结构。原文光学表述不是本解析要验证的科学结论。

B ends the first sentence, then pairs a Because-clause with its following main clause. The alternatives fail to delimit the later independent clause correctly. This analysis concerns syntax, not verification of the passage's optics.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

认为Because一律不能放句首，忽视它可以和主句构成完整句。

Assuming Because cannot begin a sentence even when a main clause follows.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

The diagram shows how it works ___ the valve closes, pressure rises. 填'. Because'还是', because'？

The diagram shows how it works ___ the valve closes, pressure rises. Choose '. Because' or ', because'.

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

'. Because'：The diagram shows how it works. Because the valve closes, pressure rises.

'. Because' gives two complete sentences, the latter with a subordinate clause.

S067独立推导 / Inferred

直接问句：语序与问号Direct questions: inversion and punctuation

知识点：疑问倒装 · 问号
Skills: Question inversion · Question marks
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

What are atmospheric rivers, and how ___？

Complete the coordinated direct question: What are atmospheric rivers, and how ___?

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
C — do they affect our weather?
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

直接问句需how + 助动词do + 主语they + 原形affect，并以问号结束。they do affect是陈述语序，适用于嵌入式表达时又通常不保留do强调结构。

A direct question uses how + do + they + affect and ends with a question mark. The alternatives use declarative order or the wrong final punctuation.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

只改标点而不检查疑问句语序，或反过来。

Checking the question mark without checking inversion.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

How ___ the system work___ 填do/does及句末标点。

How ___ the system work___ Supply do/does and the final punctuation.

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

How does the system work? 单数system配does，直接问句加问号。

How does the system work? Singular system takes does; use a question mark.

织毯：避免悬垂修饰Tapestries: avoiding a dangling modifier

知识点：修饰语归属 · 分词短语
Skills: Modifier attachment · Participial phrases
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

Woven from recycled yarn and hand tufted... 后面主语应是谁？

After Woven from recycled yarn and hand tufted..., what must be the main subject?

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
A
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

被编织的是tapestries而不是艺术家。A让织毯成为主语，正确承接过去分词修饰；其余选项把艺术家或其他结构放在应被修饰的位置。

The woven objects are the tapestries, not the artist. A places the intended referent as the main subject; the alternatives misattach the introductory modifier.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

按人名是否完整选择，忽略谁真正承受woven动作。

Focusing on the artist's name rather than what can be woven.

S071独立推导 / Inferred

玻璃与蜡纸：分号边界Glass and wax paper: a semicolon boundary

知识点：独立分句 · 关系从句不改变边界
Skills: Independent clauses · Relative clauses and sentence boundaries
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

Clear glass, which..., allows...; wax paper, which..., allows...。

Clear glass, which..., allows...; wax paper, which..., allows....

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
C — through;
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

两个主句分别是glass allows与wax paper allows。which插入从句不改变两侧完整性；分号可以连接。逗号单独不够，裸and选项也没有所需的句间逗号。

The main clauses are glass allows and wax paper allows. Relative clauses do not remove their independence; a semicolon supplies the correct boundary.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

被长which从句干扰，未识别第二个主句从wax paper开始。

Losing track of the second main clause amid relative clauses.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

Copper conducts electricity ___ rubber resists it. 填分号还是无标点？

Copper conducts electricity ___ rubber resists it. Choose a semicolon or no punctuation.

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

分号；两边为相关的完整句。

A semicolon joins the related independent clauses.

S073独立推导 / Inferred

拉丁舞：however插入语加列举冒号Latin dances: an aside and an introducing colon

知识点：冒号列举 · 插入副词
Skills: Colons introducing lists · Parenthetical adverbs
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

Only five...are included in competitions, however: rumba, samba...。

Only five...are included in competitions, however: rumba, samba....

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
A — competitions, however:
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

冒号前的主干是完整句，用冒号引出五种舞蹈的名单。however作为此句的插入副词，前面用逗号；其后冒号承担边界。不能用分号直接连接缺乏谓语的名单。

The main clause is complete and the colon introduces its list. However is a sentence-level aside, not a new clause; a semicolon cannot join the following bare list as a sentence.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

死记however前一定分号，而不判断它在本句的位置和后文结构。

Applying a fixed semicolon-before-however rule without examining its role.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

Only two colors are available, however___ red and blue. 填冒号还是分号？

Only two colors are available, however___ red and blue. Choose a colon or semicolon.

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

冒号：red and blue是名单，不是独立分句。

A colon introduces the list; red and blue is not a clause.

S087独立推导 / Inferred

室内设计：介词开头不独立成句Interior design: an introductory prepositional phrase

知识点：主句识别 · 介词短语
Skills: Identifying main clauses · Prepositional phrases
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

With a blend of traditional...and modern...之后才出现主语transitional design与谓语splits。

With a blend of traditional and modern elements precedes the subject transitional design and verb splits.

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
D — windows, transitional
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

with引出的长短语没有独立谓语，需用逗号接主句。分号与句号都错误把开头短语当完整句；单破折号也不符合此处结构。

The introductory with-phrase is not a clause. A comma connects it to the main clause; a period or semicolon wrongly treats the fragment as independent.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

开头太长就误认为已经是完整句。

Mistaking a long phrase for a complete clause.

With a careful plan ___ the team succeeded. 填逗号还是分号？

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

逗号；with短语不是独立分句。

A comma: the with-phrase is not an independent clause.

S092独立推导 / Inferred

recombinant电视：分号与句首状语Recombinant television: a semicolon and time adverbial

Skills: Sentence boundaries · Commas after introductory adverbials
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

前句说明旧叙事包装为新内容；后句in that era, even shows...attracted...。

One clause describes repackaging formulas; another begins in that era, even shows...attracted....

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
B — content; in that era,
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

第一句use referenced...与第二句shows attracted...各自完整。分号分隔两句；in that era是第二句开头时间状语，其后逗号清楚界定。

The clauses have separate predicates, referenced and attracted. The semicolon separates them, and the comma marks the second clause's introductory time phrase.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

把in that era接回content，遗漏后续even shows引出的新主句。

Attaching the time phrase to content and missing the new main clause.

分号，两边是完整句；in that decade属于后句。

S105独立推导 / Inferred

插入语不改变单数主语Intervening phrases and singular agreement

知识点：主谓一致 · 介词短语不改变主语
Skills: Subject-verb agreement · Prepositional phrases do not change the subject
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

主语是数学家 Perelman，中间插入 sometimes in conjunction with Hamilton and Yau，填被动谓语。

Supply the passive verb for Perelman, with the intervening phrase sometimes in conjunction with Hamilton and Yau.

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
D — is
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

独立推导：主语核心为单数 Perelman；in conjunction with 后的人名不是并列主语。故用 is credited。其余三个选项均为复数形式。

Independent derivation: The head subject is the singular Perelman. Names following in conjunction with do not create a compound subject, so is credited is correct; the other options are plural.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

常见错因是让离谓语较近的复数人名决定动词形式。

A common trap is letting nearby plural names control agreement instead of the subject.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

填空：The director, together with two assistants, ____ responsible.（is/are）

Complete: The director, together with two assistants, ____ responsible. (is/are)

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

is。主语核心 director 为单数；together with 不等于 and。

Is. Director is singular; together with does not function like and.

一般过程中的 until 时态Tense with until in a general process

Skills: Simple present tense · Adverbial clauses of time
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

原子在同步加速器中不断加速，直到达到目标能量，再被导向碰撞。填 until 从句的动词。

Atoms accelerate in a synchrotron until they attain the desired energy and are directed into collisions. Complete the until clause.

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
B — reach
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

独立推导：整段描述常规科学过程，主句 travel、后文 are diverted 均为一般现在时，因此用 reach。will reach 不适合此时间从句；had reached 无过去完成参照。

Independent derivation: The passage describes a general process using travel and are diverted, so reach maintains the simple-present pattern. Will reach is inappropriate here, and had reached lacks a past reference point.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

常见错因是认为“达到”发生在加速之后，就一定要用 will；时间先后不等于未来时。

A common trap is assuming that a later step automatically requires will.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

falls。一般过程的 until 从句使用一般现在时。

Falls. The until clause uses the simple present in this general process.

S110独立推导 / Inferred

比较对象必须一致Comparing like with like

知识点：逻辑比较 · 代词指代 · 冒号解释
Skills: Logical comparisons · Pronoun reference · Colons introducing explanations
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

句首 Compared to that of alumina glass 中的 that 指原子排列；选择后接主句。

In Compared to that of alumina glass, that refers to atomic arrangement. Select the appropriate following subject.

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
D — silica glass's atomic arrangement is more dispersed
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

独立推导：必须比较两种玻璃的原子排列，故主语需为 silica glass's atomic arrangement，选 D。A、B 比较排列与玻璃，C 比较排列与劣势，均不平行。冒号后解释分散排列造成的缺点。

Independent derivation: The comparison requires atomic arrangement versus atomic arrangement, making D correct. A and B compare an arrangement with glass; C compares it with a disadvantage. The colon then introduces the explanation.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

常见错因是只检查语法通顺，不核对 compared to 两端究竟比较什么。

A common trap is checking surface fluency without identifying both objects of comparison.

改正：Compared with that of copper, aluminum is lower in density.（that 指密度）

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

Compared with that of copper, aluminum's density is lower. 比较双方均为密度。

Compared with that of copper, aluminum's density is lower. Both compared items are densities.

S111独立推导 / Inferred

主句已有谓语时的分词修饰Participial modification when a main verb already exists

知识点：句子主干 · 现在分词后置修饰
Skills: Identifying the main clause · Present-participial postmodifiers
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

This hypothesis ____ that trees survived throughout the Holocene cannot stand...，填空修饰 hypothesis。

Complete This hypothesis ____ that certain trees survived throughout the Holocene cannot stand....

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
A — suggesting
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

独立推导：主句是 This hypothesis cannot stand。空格部分修饰 hypothesis，应为 suggesting，约等于 that suggests。suggests、has suggested 或作谓语的 suggested 会与 cannot stand 形成无连接的双谓语。

Independent derivation: The main clause is This hypothesis cannot stand. Suggesting introduces a modifier equivalent to that suggests. The finite alternatives would create an unconnected second predicate.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

常见错因是看到单数 hypothesis 就选 suggests，未发现后面的 cannot stand 才是主句谓语。

A common trap is selecting suggests for singular agreement without noticing the existing main predicate cannot stand.

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

describing。was rejected 是主句谓语；describing serious damage 修饰 report。

Describing. Was rejected is the main predicate; describing serious damage modifies report.

复数主语与长插入语Plural subjects across long modifiers

Skills: Subject-verb agreement · Identifying the head of the subject
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

The trailblazing accomplishments of Goldin, winner of the 2023 Nobel Prize..., ____ to the value of diverse scholars。

Complete The trailblazing accomplishments of Goldin, winner of the 2023 Nobel Prize..., ____ to the value of diverse scholars.

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
D — attest
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

独立推导：主语核心 accomplishments 为复数，of Goldin 和获奖说明均为修饰，因此谓语用 attest。attests、has attested、is attesting 都配单数主语。

Independent derivation: The head subject accomplishments is plural; of Goldin and the prize description are modifiers. Attest is the plural-compatible form; the other options are singular.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

常见错因是把最近的人名 Goldin 当主语，而忽略句子真正说的是“成就证明”。

A common trap is agreeing with the nearby singular name rather than the plural accomplishments.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

are。主语为复数 discoveries，而非 scientist。

Are. Discoveries, not scientist, controls agreement.
