S130独立推导 / Inferred

冒号连接完整解释A colon introducing a full explanation

知识点：独立分句边界 · 冒号解释
Skills: Independent-clause boundaries · Colons introducing explanations
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

光强影响光合作用反应速率；后文解释光强增大时反应物转化也加快。选择 photosynthesis 后的标点。

Light intensity affects the reaction rate of photosynthesis; the following clause explains that increasing light accelerates conversion of reactants. Choose the punctuation.

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
C — photosynthesis:
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

独立推导：冒号前为完整句，后面 as light intensity increases, so does... 也是完整解释，故 C。逗号造成逗号拼接；无标点造成粘连；and 前缺少用于连接两个完整分句的逗号。

Independent derivation: The complete statement before the blank is followed by a full explanatory clause, so a colon works. A comma alone creates a splice; no punctuation creates a run-on; and lacks the needed boundary comma.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

常见错因是把后面的 as 从句视作全部结构，未看到其后 so does... 构成完整主句。

A common trap is noticing as but overlooking the following main clause so does....

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

填标点：The test confirmed one result____ higher temperatures increased reaction speed.

Supply punctuation: The test confirmed one result____ higher temperatures increased reaction speed.

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

冒号。前句完整，后句说明所确认的结果。

A colon. The complete clause is followed by an explanation of the result.

S132独立推导 / Inferred

必要时间从句前不乱加标点Avoiding unnecessary punctuation before a time clause

知识点：从属连词 · 句子完整性
Skills: Subordinating conjunctions · Sentence completeness
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

每当地球自转计时比原子钟慢整整0.9秒，就加入闰秒；选择 added 和 whenever 的连接方式。

A leap second is added whenever rotation-based time lags atomic time by a full 0.9 seconds. Choose the boundary before whenever.

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
D — added whenever
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

独立推导：whenever 引导说明何时添加的时间从句，紧随主句无需标点。分号或句号会把 whenever 从句拆成残句；此处逗号也无必要。

Independent derivation: Whenever introduces the following integrated time clause, so no punctuation is needed. A semicolon or period would leave a dependent-clause fragment; a comma is unnecessary here.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

常见错因是看到长句便想断开，未判断断开后的 whenever 从句能否独立。

A common trap is splitting a long sentence without checking whether the resulting whenever clause can stand alone.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

填选项：The alarm sounds____ smoke is detected.（; whenever / whenever）

Choose: The alarm sounds____ smoke is detected. (; whenever / whenever)

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

whenever。它引导依附主句的时间条件从句，不能用分号分隔。

Whenever. It introduces a dependent time clause that cannot be separated by a semicolon.

S142独立推导 / Inferred

情态动词统辖并列动词A modal governing coordinated verbs

知识点：并列结构 · 情态动词后原形
Skills: Parallel structure · Base-form verbs after modals
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

The lock would increase the salinity of the lakes and ____ a natural barrier。

Complete The lock would increase the salinity of the lakes and ____ a natural barrier.

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
B — create
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

独立推导：would 同时支配 increase 和 create，形成 would increase... and create...，两者都用动词原形。creates、creating、created 破坏平行。

Independent derivation: Would governs both coordinated verbs: would increase ... and create .... Both must be base forms; creates, creating, and created disrupt the parallel structure.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

常见错因是因 lock 为单数就选 creates，忘记情态动词后的原形规则。

A common trap is choosing creates for a singular subject while overlooking the governing modal.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

填空：The new rule will reduce waste and ____ energy.（saves/save）

Complete: The new rule will reduce waste and ____ energy. (saves/save)

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

save。will 统辖 reduce 和 save 两个原形动词。

Save. Will governs both reduce and save in the base form.

S145独立推导 / Inferred

破折号插入语必须配对Paired dashes around an interruption

知识点：成对标点 · 插入语边界
Skills: Paired punctuation · Boundaries of parenthetical interruptions
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

Walston's findings indicate that—assuming solar panel installers' ____ practice has the potential...，补全插入部分的结尾。

Complete Walston's findings indicate that—assuming solar panel installers' ____ practice has the potential....

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
B — cooperation—this
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

独立推导：assuming... cooperation 是插入语，已用破折号开始，必须用破折号结束，再接 this practice，故 B。去掉插入语，主干为 findings indicate that this practice has...，结构完整。

Independent derivation: The parenthetical assuming ... cooperation opens with a dash and must close with a dash before this practice, B. Removing it leaves findings indicate that this practice has ..., a complete structure.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

常见错因是用逗号关闭破折号开启的插入语，或遗漏结束标点。

A common trap is closing a dash-delimited interruption with a comma or no punctuation.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

填标点：The plan—if approved____ will begin in June.

Supply punctuation: The plan—if approved____ will begin in June.

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

破折号：The plan—if approved—will begin in June. 插入语两侧匹配。

A dash: The plan—if approved—will begin in June. The interruption is enclosed by matching dashes.

S158独立推导 / Inferred

过去分词开头的修饰对象The subject of an introductory past-participial modifier

知识点：悬垂修饰 · 分号连接完整句
Skills: Dangling modifiers · Semicolons joining complete clauses
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

Established in 1936 by Richard Wright, ____ it would become...，选择正确主语和分句连接。

After Established in 1936 by Richard Wright, choose a clause before it would become....

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
A — the South Side Writers Group provided a valuable forum...;
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

独立推导：被创建的是 South Side Writers Group，必须紧接为主语，A。其他选项把作家、写作活动或城市当成被创建对象。A 中分号两侧均完整，it 指该团体。

Independent derivation: The entity established in 1936 was the South Side Writers Group, which must be the following subject, A. Other subjects create dangling modifiers. The semicolon joins complete clauses and it refers to the group.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

常见错因是认为句中出现正确团体名就够，而不要求它成为开头修饰语所修饰的主语。

A common trap is accepting the correct noun anywhere in the sentence rather than as the modified subject.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

填主语：Founded in 1990, ____ serves local students.（the school / local teachers）

Choose: Founded in 1990, ____ serves local students. (the school / local teachers)

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

the school。被创建的是学校，不是老师。

The school. The school, not the teachers, was founded.

S159独立推导 / Inferred

名词加分词构成独立主格An absolute phrase with noun plus participle

知识点：独立主格 · 分词与限定谓语
Skills: Absolute phrases · Participles versus finite predicates
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

古埃及人睡硬质高枕，后接 their design ____ airflow... over comfort，选择动词形式。

Ancient Egyptians slept on elevated headrests, followed by their design ____ airflow and protection over comfort. Choose the verb form.

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
D — prioritizing
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

独立推导：主句已完整，逗号后 their design prioritizing... 是独立主格，说明设计特点，故 D。had prioritized、was prioritizing、prioritized 都会形成未连接的独立分句。

Independent derivation: The complete main clause is followed by the absolute phrase their design prioritizing ..., so D supplies a participle. The finite alternatives create a second independent clause joined only by a comma.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

常见错因是把过去叙事当作选过去式的唯一依据，忽略结构是否允许第二个谓语。

A common trap is choosing past tense solely for historical context without checking clause structure.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

studying。their guide studying the map 是独立主格；studied 会造成逗号拼接。

Studying. The noun-plus-participle absolute avoids a comma splice.

S161独立推导 / Inferred

标题而非听众承接开头修饰Titles, not listeners, receive the opening modifiers

Skills: Dangling modifiers · Semantic subjects
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

开头写 Laden with emojis... and driven by Sawano's mood，后面应以哪个对象为主语？

After Laden with emojis and seemingly meaningless words, and driven by Sawano's mood, what should be the subject?

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
B — the titles allow the listener to approach each piece free from expectations
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

独立推导：充满表情和词语、由作曲者情绪驱动的是 titles，故 B。听众、乐曲本身或听众的欣赏方式都不是这些文字特征的承载对象，其他选项修饰悬垂或错位。

Independent derivation: The titles contain emojis and words and are driven by the composer's mood, so B supplies the correct subject. Listeners, pieces, and approaches are not the intended recipients of those modifiers.

逗号后独立主格说明成因An absolute phrase explaining formation

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
C — forming
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

独立推导：the gems typically forming... 为名词加分词的独立主格，补充成因，C。can form、will form、form 都是限定谓语，会制造只有逗号连接的两个独立分句。

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

常见错因是因科学事实应使用一般现在时而选 form，却没有先判断空格是否需要谓语。

moving。逗号后的独立主格提供补充说明。

Moving. The absolute phrase supplies supplementary information without a comma splice.

S180独立推导 / Inferred

动名词短语作单数主语A gerund phrase as a singular subject

知识点：动名词主语 · 主谓一致 · 介词短语干扰
Skills: Gerund phrases as subjects · Subject-verb agreement · Distracting prepositional phrases
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

Depicting people and events, whether in paintings, poems, or other art forms, ____ humanity to perceive...。

Complete Depicting people and events, whether in paintings, poems, or other art forms, ____ humanity to perceive....

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
A — enables
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

独立推导：主语是整个动作 Depicting people and events，按单数处理，须用限定谓语 enables。people、events、art forms 都不是主语核心；enabling 不能单独作主句谓语。

Independent derivation: The entire gerund phrase Depicting people and events is a singular subject, requiring enables. Its plural objects and modifiers do not control agreement; enabling would leave no finite main verb.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

常见错因是受 people and events 的复数影响，忽略“描绘这一行为”才是主语。

A common trap is agreeing with people and events instead of the activity expressed by the gerund phrase.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

expands。整个 reading 短语作为一种活动，按单数处理。

Expands. The entire reading phrase denotes a single activity and takes singular agreement.

S193独立推导 / Inferred

同一主语的三个并列谓语Three coordinated predicates with one subject

知识点：并列谓语 · 系列逗号 · 分号误用
Skills: Coordinated predicates · Commas in a series · Misuse of semicolons
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

Westbrook drew on shared ____ and conveyed her individual perspective，中间需加入 improvised with available materials。

Complete Westbrook drew on shared ____ and conveyed her individual perspective, incorporating improvised with available materials.

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
A — patterns, improvised with available materials,
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

独立推导：三项谓语平行：drew on..., improvised..., and conveyed...，以系列逗号分隔，A。分号不能切开共用主语但无独立主语的谓语片段；冒号也不适合此直接并列。

Independent derivation: The three coordinated predicates are drew on ..., improvised ..., and conveyed ..., so series commas give A. Semicolons would separate predicate fragments lacking their own subject; a colon does not express the intended parallel series.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

常见错因是看到三个长动作就使用分号，而未检查每部分是否独立分句。

A common trap is using semicolons for lengthy actions without checking clause independence.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

填标点：She gathered fabric____ cut rectangles____ and stitched a quilt.

Supply punctuation: She gathered fabric____ cut rectangles____ and stitched a quilt.

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

两处均用逗号，组成三个共主语的并列谓语。

Use commas at both blanks for a three-part predicate series.

S194独立推导 / Inferred

插入定语从句之后仍需主谓结构Completing the main clause after an intervening relative clause

知识点：句子主干 · 及物动词宾语 · 插入从句
Skills: Identifying the main clause · Objects of transitive verbs · Intervening clauses
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

An attack by cotton leafworms, which are common predators, ____ to produce foul-tasting chemicals。

Complete An attack by cotton leafworms, which are common predators, ____ to produce foul-tasting chemicals.

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
B — will trigger the plant
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

独立推导：删去 which 从句，主干应为 An attack will trigger the plant to produce...，故 B。A、C 让名词 plant 不当地重复插入主谓之间；D 仅分词，主句无谓语。

Independent derivation: Removing the relative clause reveals An attack will trigger the plant to produce ..., B. A and C insert an incompatible extra subject, while D leaves the main clause without a finite verb.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

常见错因是把定语从句中的 are 当成整句主谓已经齐全，或因 plant 语义突出而误当主句主语。

Common traps are treating the relative clause's are as the main verb and mistaking plant for the main subject.

causes。主句 A signal causes... 需要限定谓语。

Causes. The main clause A signal causes ... needs a finite predicate.

原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

Independent derivation: The supplementary structure is with + noun phrase + present participle, so all contributing is correct. The finite alternatives cannot complete this with construction.

长破折号插入语后的复数谓语Plural agreement after a long dash-delimited phrase

原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

Commercial applications of synthetic dye technology—beginning with mauve, discovered in 1856—____ responsible for a manufacturing revolution。

Complete Commercial applications of synthetic dye technology—beginning with mauve, discovered in 1856—____ responsible for a manufacturing revolution.

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
A — were
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

独立推导：主语核心 applications 为复数，且叙述十九世纪历史，故 were。technology、mauve 均为修饰内容中的单数词，不能支配谓语；其余选项为单数。

Independent derivation: Applications is the plural head, and the historical context calls for were. Technology and mauve occur in modifiers and do not control agreement; all other choices are singular.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

常见错因是让长插入语里的单数 dye 或 technology 诱导 was，而忘记复数 applications。

were。主语 inventions 为复数，过去历史用 were。

Were. The plural subject inventions takes the past plural form.

分号后以同位说明开头的新分句A new clause beginning with an appositive after a semicolon

Skills: Independent-clause boundaries · Clause-initial appositives · Semicolons
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

前句介绍 Lüneburg 是汉萨同盟成员，定语从句止于13—17世纪；后句以 a loose confederation of cities... 开头，接 the League has been described...。

One clause describes Lüneburg's Hanseatic membership through a relative clause ending with the thirteenth to seventeenth centuries; the next begins a loose confederation of cities ... and continues the League has been described....

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
A — centuries;
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

独立推导：第一句主干 city was a member 完整；第二句前置同位短语说明 the League，主干 League has been described 也完整，故分号连接，A。逗号会拼接；and 后紧接同位名词短语而无匹配隔离，给定形式不合结构。

Independent derivation: City was a member and the League has been described form two complete clauses; the second opens with an appositive describing the League. A semicolon therefore works, A. A comma splices clauses, and the supplied and constructions fail to delimit the opening appositive properly.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

常见错因是误把 a loose confederation 当作前句的继续，未定位后面新主语 the League。

A common trap is attaching a loose confederation to the earlier clause instead of recognizing its relation to the new subject the League.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

填标点：The town joined the alliance in 1600____ a union of trading ports, the alliance expanded rapidly.

Supply punctuation: The town joined the alliance in 1600____ a union of trading ports, the alliance expanded rapidly.

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

分号。后句由同位短语开头，但其主干 the alliance expanded rapidly 完整。

A semicolon. The second clause begins with an appositive but has the complete main clause the alliance expanded rapidly.

S198独立推导 / Inferred

冒号后包含让步从句的完整解释A colon before a full explanation containing a concessive clause

知识点：冒号解释 · though 从属结构 · 逗号拼接
Skills: Colons introducing explanations · Subordinate clauses introduced by though · Comma splices
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

先说葡萄牙 Porto Côvo 海滩沙色特别；后说虽然大多数沙来自灰褐石英、长石，该地氧化铁等却使其呈橙色。选择 shade 后标点。

The passage states that Porto Côvo sand has a distinct color, then explains that although most sand derives color from quartz and feldspar, iron oxide and other material give this beach an orange tint. Choose punctuation after shade.

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
C — shade: though most
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

独立推导：冒号前 The sand... is a strikingly different shade 完整；后面 though most sand gets... 是让步从句，deposits... lend... 为其主句，整体解释特殊沙色，C。仅逗号会连接两个完整句；though. 的断法把后文两个主句错误拼接。

Independent derivation: A complete statement precedes the colon. After it, though most sand gets ... is subordinate to deposits ... lend ..., together explaining the special color, C. A comma alone splices clauses; placing though before a period leaves the following main clauses improperly joined.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

常见错因是看到 though 就认为全句只能是从句，忽略后面还有自己的主句。

A common trap is seeing though and overlooking the independent main clause later in the explanation.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

填标点：The lake has an unusual color____ although most lakes look blue, this one appears pink because of microbes.

Supply punctuation: The lake has an unusual color____ although most lakes look blue, this one appears pink because of microbes.

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

冒号。后面“虽然多数湖蓝，但此湖因微生物呈粉色”是完整解释。

A colon. The following concessive clause plus its main clause forms a complete explanation.

原题出处 / Uploaded source: 用户提供截图 S198 · On most of the world' s beaches, sand is a predictable cream or beige color. The sand at。正式试卷、模块与官方题库ID未核实。 / Test, module, and official item IDs are unverified.
08

逻辑过渡Transitions

18 题组 / question groups
知识点总则 / Core principles

先用自己的话写清A句与B句的关系，再选连接词：因果、补充、举例、细化、让步、纠正、时间推进或强化。先划定逻辑层级，句内when in fact的转折不一定决定句间空格。

State the relation before choosing a transition: result, addition, example, specification, concession, correction, time, or reinforcement. Locate the logical boundary; a contrast inside a later clause may not govern the blank.

常见错因 / Common traps

见such as就选举例；见科学因果内容就选Therefore；见后文纠正就不分层级选However。

Local example cues mistaken for sentence relations; causal subject matter mistaken for an inferential transition; contrast applied at the wrong level.

S021独立推导 / Inferred

语言接触：原因与结果Language contact: cause and result

知识点：因果过渡
Skills: Cause-and-effect transitions
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

阿拉伯语政权长期统治该地区，许多阿拉伯语词因此进入西班牙语。

Arabic-speaking rule in a region led to many Arabic words entering Spanish.

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
D — Consequently
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

前句提供历史原因，后句给语言结果，应选Consequently。后句内部such as才引出词语例子，不能把句间关系误当举例。

Historical rule is the cause; vocabulary borrowing is the result. Such as introduces an example inside the latter sentence, not the relationship between sentences.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

被局部的such as吸引，给整个句间关系选For example。

Letting a local example phrase determine the whole sentence transition.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

The bridge closed. ___, traffic used the tunnel. 填Consequently / Specifically。

The bridge closed. ___, traffic used the tunnel. Choose Consequently or Specifically.

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

Consequently：交通改道是封桥的结果。

Consequently: rerouting is a result of closure.

练习出处 / Practice source: 本手册原创，基于 S021 的考点；不是官方原题。 / Created for this guide from the linked skill; not an official question.

S022 · S147独立推导 / Inferred

藤壶：现象不等于解释Barnacles: observation versus explanation

知识点：论证指代 · 反驳解释 · 指代对象 · 现象与解释区分
Skills: References within an argument · Challenging an explanation · Identifying referents · Distinguishing phenomena from explanations
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

藤壶移动曾归因于水流被动推动；研究发现它们逆水流移动。

Barnacle movement was attributed to passive water flow; observed movement goes against the flow.

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
B — Undermining this explanation
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

新发现没有否定‘会移动’这个现象，而是削弱‘被水流推动’的解释。因此this explanation指代因果假说，不能用Contrary to this phenomenon。

The finding confirms movement but undermines the passive-flow explanation. The target is a causal account, not the existence of the phenomenon.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

把对解释的反证，错说成对现象本身的否认。

Treating evidence against an explanation as denial of the observation.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

灯熄灭被解释为停电，但其他电器正常。被削弱的是现象还是解释？

A lamp goes out, supposedly because of a power cut, but other appliances work. What is weakened?

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

停电解释被削弱；灯熄灭这一观察仍成立。

The power-cut explanation is weakened; the lamp's failure remains observed.

练习出处 / Practice source: 本手册原创，基于 S022 · S147 的考点；不是官方原题。 / Created for this guide from the linked skill; not an official question.

S039独立推导 / Inferred

龟壳：结果连接与句内转折Turtle shells: an outer result, an inner contrast

知识点：因果过渡 · 逻辑层级
Skills: Cause-and-effect transitions · Levels of logical relations
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

龟壳看似位于身体外部，因此常被误认为外骨骼；实际上属于内骨骼。

A shell looks external, so it is mistaken for an exoskeleton, although it belongs to the internal skeleton.

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
D — Hence
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

空格连接‘外观’与‘由外观导致的误解’，应为Hence。真正的事实纠正在后半句when in fact内部发生，不能把它的转折关系搬到空格。

The blank connects appearance to the resulting misconception: Hence. The correction is introduced later by when in fact; that local contrast does not govern the blank.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

一看到后面有纠正事实，就机械选However。

Selecting However solely because a later clause corrects a misconception.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

The stone looks like gold. ___, visitors often misidentify it, although it is pyrite. 填Hence / However。

The stone looks like gold. ___, visitors often misidentify it, although it is pyrite. Choose Hence or However.

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

Hence：金色外观导致误认；although负责句内转折。

Hence links appearance to misidentification; although handles the internal correction.

S054独立推导 / Inferred

美术馆：过去与今天An art museum: past versus present

知识点：时间过渡
Skills: Temporal transitions
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

2009年扩建前只能展出约5%，后句说现在可展出约30%。

Before an expansion the museum displayed about 5%; the next sentence gives its current approximately 30% capacity.

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
D — Today
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

Today建立过去与现在的时间推进。不是对5%举例，也不是同一时间的简单补充；Nevertheless会突出不符合预期的转折，此处改善正符合扩建结果。

Today marks the temporal shift. The second figure is not an example of the first, and improvement after expansion is not an unexpected concession.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

见数字不同就选转折，不看时间锚点。

Selecting contrast merely because numbers differ, without checking time markers.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

In 1990 the town had one clinic. ___, it has six. 填Today / For example。

In 1990 the town had one clinic. ___, it has six. Choose Today or For example.

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

Today，明确从过去推进至现在。

Today explicitly advances from past to present.

S055独立推导 / Inferred

quipus：添加另一项用途Quipus: adding another use

知识点：并列补充 · 非因果
Skills: Adding parallel information · Noncausal relationships
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

绳结记事记录数量信息，也可能记录故事与神话。

Knotted devices recorded numerical information and may also have recorded narratives.

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
C — In addition
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

两句是不同用途的补充。故事不是数量信息的一个例子，也不是由记录数量必然产生的结果，故In addition。

The second use adds information. Stories are not examples of numerical data and do not follow causally from recording numbers.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

把‘另一个功能’误当‘前一功能的例子’。

Treating another function as an example of the first.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

The tool measures distance. ___, it records temperature. 填In addition / As a result。

The tool measures distance. ___, it records temperature. Choose In addition or As a result.

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

In addition：功能并列，没有因果说明。

In addition: these are parallel capabilities, not a stated causal chain.

S057独立推导 / Inferred

Darwin与Wallace：由前提推出判断Darwin and Wallace: an inference marker

知识点：推论 · 插入位置
Skills: Inference · Position of an aside
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

既然当时自然科学思想已使自然选择成为顺理成章的下一步，两人独立提出就可能不是巧合。

Given the scientific ideas of the time, natural selection was an obvious next step; independent discovery may therefore not be coincidental.

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
B — then
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

then在此是‘那么/因此’的推论，不是时间上的‘然后’。however会错误制造对立；moreover仅补充，未如then准确标出前提和结论。

Then marks an inference: given that context, the coincidence is less surprising. It is not a chronological then; however would introduce the wrong relation.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

只会把then理解为随后，忽视推论义。

Recognizing only the chronological meaning of then.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

The roads are flooded. It may be wise, ___, to postpone travel. 填then / however。

The roads are flooded. It may be wise, ___, to postpone travel. Choose then or however.

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

then：由路况推出延迟出行。

Then marks the recommendation inferred from the road conditions.

S072独立推导 / Inferred

地核：连续因果链Earth's core: a causal chain

知识点：因果链 · 顺承结果
Skills: Causal chains · Successive consequences
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

金属流动产生电流，电流接着形成防护屏障。

Moving metals generate currents; the currents then create a protective barrier.

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
A — in turn
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

in turn把前一步结果作为下一步原因。likewise用于平行相似事物，nevertheless用于让步转折，都未表现这条递进因果链。

In turn marks a result becoming the next cause. Likewise would compare parallel phenomena; nevertheless would signal a concession.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

把‘接着产生下一结果’当成两个相似事实的并列。

Treating a causal chain as a similarity comparison.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

Rain fills the reservoir, which, ___, supplies the town. 填in turn / nevertheless。

Rain fills the reservoir, which, ___, supplies the town. Choose in turn or nevertheless.

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

in turn：降雨→水库→供水的链式作用。

In turn links rainfall, storage, and supply in a chain.

S074独立推导 / Inferred

Henry James：用强证据强化前述Henry James: strengthening a prior claim

知识点：递进强化 · 例证与改述区分
Skills: Reinforcement · Evidence versus paraphrase
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

两个版本差异极大，甚至有评论家视为两部不同小说。

The editions differ so much that some critics treat them as separate novels.

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
B — in fact
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

in fact用更强的具体判断证实‘差异极大’。不是对立；也不完全是同义改述，因为新增评论家的判断和强度。

In fact reinforces the unusually large difference with stronger evidence. There is no opposition, and the critics' judgment adds more than a simple paraphrase.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

只看后句更具体就机械选in other words，未判断是否新增证据。

Choosing in other words whenever the next sentence is more specific.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

The renovation was extensive; ___, only one original wall remains. 填in fact / by contrast。

The renovation was extensive; ___, only one original wall remains. Choose in fact or by contrast.

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

in fact：仅剩一面墙强化大规模改造这一说法。

In fact: the remaining wall reinforces the extent of renovation.

S088独立推导 / Inferred

Local Bubble：从结论到机制细节The Local Bubble: specifying a mechanism

知识点：概括与具体 · 解释细化
Skills: General claims and specifics · Specifying an explanation
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

先说超新星与新恒星形成有关，再说明膨胀气泡如何困住气尘云并促成坍缩。

A general link to star formation is followed by how expanding gas traps clouds that later collapse.

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
D — Specifically
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

后句把前句结论具体化，给出how，因此Specifically。它不是反例或让步，也不是单纯基于前句推出一个新独立结果。

The second sentence specifies how the general claim works. Specifically fits detail, rather than contradiction, concession, or a separate inferred consequence.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

见物理因果内容就选Hence，而未看两句话的修辞关系。

Choosing Hence because the topic contains causation, without checking the sentences' rhetorical relation.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

The machine saves energy. ___, it switches off when idle. 填Specifically / However。

The machine saves energy. ___, it switches off when idle. Choose Specifically or However.

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

Specifically：给出节能的具体机制。

Specifically introduces the particular energy-saving mechanism.

S089独立推导 / Inferred

四维画作：限制后的让步转折A four-dimensional painting: a qualified concession

知识点：让步转折 · 保留价值
Skills: Concessive transitions · Acknowledging remaining merit
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

画不能真正让人看见第四维，但多视角仍可提供有趣的想象。

The painting cannot literally display a fourth dimension, but multiple viewpoints still suggest it imaginatively.

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
B — That said
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

That said承认限制后转向仍然存在的艺术价值。后句不是对‘不能表示第四维’的简单同义改写，也不是它的例子。

That said moves from an acknowledged limitation to a remaining merit. The second sentence is neither a paraphrase nor an example of literal impossibility.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

把先承认限制再肯定价值的结构当作纯添加。

Missing the concessive shift from limitation to merit.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

The map is not exact. ___, it is useful for planning. 填That said / In other words。

The map is not exact. ___, it is useful for planning. Choose That said or In other words.

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

That said：虽不精确，仍有用途。

That said preserves usefulness despite the limitation.

S100独立推导 / Inferred

街画：纠正视觉误认Street art: correcting an illusion

知识点：纠正关系 · 表象与事实
Skills: Correcting a misconception · Appearance versus fact
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

人们以为路面开了深洞，实际只是透视法制造的深度幻觉。

Viewers assume a deep hole has opened; the artist has actually created only an illusion.

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
B — On the contrary
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

第二句否定前句的具体判断，On the contrary引出相反事实。与龟壳那题不同，这里的纠正就在空格之后，而非之后的句内从句。

The next sentence directly corrects the assumed hole, so On the contrary fits. Unlike the turtle-shell item, the correction occurs at the blank itself.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

看到幻觉主题就套用某一道题的过渡词，不重新划分逻辑边界。

Reusing a transition from a similar topic without locating the actual correction.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

It looks like a doorway. ___, it is a flat painted wall. 填On the contrary / As a result。

It looks like a doorway. ___, it is a flat painted wall. Choose On the contrary or As a result.

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

On the contrary：后句直接纠正前句的判断。

On the contrary introduces the direct correction.

练习出处 / Practice source: 本手册原创，基于 S100 的考点；不是官方原题。 / Created for this guide from the linked skill; not an official question.

S112独立推导 / Inferred

研究结果与理论反驳的因果From findings to theoretical implications

知识点：结论关系 · 转折与因果区分
Skills: Conclusion relationships · Distinguishing contrast from causation
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

Ostrom 证明共同资源可由使用者持续管理；下一句称其工作反驳“公地悲剧”。选连接语。

Ostrom showed that users can sustainably manage shared resources. The next sentence describes her work as a repudiation of the tragedy of the commons. Choose the transition.

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
D — As such,
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

独立推导：可持续管理的实证结果使其工作构成反驳，后句是前句的推论，As such 最合适。By contrast 和 That said 表示转折，For example 引入例子，均不符。

Independent derivation: The empirical finding makes the work a rebuttal, so the second sentence follows as a conclusion: As such. Contrast, concession, and exemplification do not capture this relationship.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

常见错因是看到两种相反观点，就自动选转折；真正连接的是“发现”与“发现的意义”。

A common trap is choosing contrast merely because opposing theories appear; the connected sentences express a finding and its implication.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

填连接语：试验证明过滤器可去除污染物。____，它有望改善供水质量。

Choose a transition: Tests show that the filter removes contaminants. ____, it may improve water quality.

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

因此／As such。后句是研究结果的推论。

As such / Therefore. The second sentence is an implication of the test result.

S131独立推导 / Inferred

广播输与电视胜的转折Contrasting radio and television judgments

知识点：让步转折 · 不同受众的结论
Skills: Concession and contrast · Conclusions from different audiences
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

广播听众调查显示 Kennedy 输了辩论，但电视观众更支持他，辩论最终被视为他的胜利。选连接语。

A radio-listener poll suggested Kennedy lost the debate, yet television viewers favored him and it was ultimately seen as his victory. Choose the transition.

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
D — Nevertheless,
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

独立推导：第二句结论与前句看似不利的调查结果相反，需要 Nevertheless。Therefore 是因果，Likewise 是相似，In other words 是改述，都不合。

Independent derivation: The second sentence reverses the implication of the unfavorable poll, requiring Nevertheless. Therefore signals causation, Likewise similarity, and In other words restatement.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

常见错因是将不同媒介下的相反评价当成同一事实的解释。

A common trap is treating opposing judgments from different audiences as a restatement.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

填连接语：方案初测成绩很低。____，修订后它最终获奖。

Choose a transition: The proposal scored poorly in its first review. ____, after revisions it won the award.

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

Nevertheless／然而。最终结果与最初迹象相反。

Nevertheless. The final outcome contrasts with the initial indication.

S133独立推导 / Inferred

从概括到具体观点From a general statement to a specific argument

知识点：细化说明 · 论点层级
Skills: Elaboration with specifics · Levels of generality in an argument
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

先说 Sauer 挑战自然环境塑造文化的主流观点，再说明他认为文化主动与环境互动、参与自身发展。选过渡语。

The passage first says Sauer challenged prevailing views, then states his specific argument that cultures actively shape their development through environmental interaction. Choose the transition.

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
D — Specifically,
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

独立推导：后句具体说明他如何挑战旧观念，属概括到细节，故 Specifically。没有顺序清单可用 Finally，也不是新观点由前句推导出的 Therefore。

Independent derivation: The second sentence specifies the content of his challenge, so Specifically fits. There is no ordered list for Finally, nor a causal inference requiring Therefore.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

常见错因是把所有后续说明都当因果，而未区分“说清是什么”和“所以导致什么”。

A common trap is confusing elaboration—what the claim means—with causation—what follows from it.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

填连接语：她提出了一项节能方案。____，她建议为全部窗户加装隔热层。

Choose a transition: She proposed an energy-saving plan. ____, she recommended insulating every window.

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

Specifically。第二句给出方案具体内容。

Specifically. The second sentence supplies the plan's concrete content.

S148独立推导 / Inferred

语言接触的结果A consequence of language contact

知识点：历史因果 · 结果连接
Skills: Historical causation · Transitions indicating consequences
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

阿拉伯语穆斯林政权长期统治伊比利亚半岛部分地区，许多阿拉伯词进入西班牙语。选连接语。

Arabic-speaking states ruled much of the Iberian Peninsula for centuries; many Arabic words entered Spanish. Choose the transition.

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
D — Consequently,
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

独立推导：政治统治带来语言接触，借词是其结果，Consequently 最合适。For example、Specifically 不能把词汇借入说成统治事件的例子；Instead 表替代。

Independent derivation: Extended rule produced language contact, with borrowing as a consequence, so Consequently fits. Borrowed words are not an example or specification of the act of political rule, and Instead would imply replacement.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

常见错因是看到 such as 后的具体词例，就误把整个句际关系判断为举例。

A common trap is letting the local such as example determine the relationship between the two whole sentences.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

填连接语：两地区长期贸易往来。____，双方语言都吸收了对方的商业词汇。

Choose a transition: The regions traded for centuries. ____, both languages borrowed commercial terms from each other.

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

Consequently。词汇借入是长期接触的结果。

Consequently. Lexical borrowing is presented as a result of sustained contact.

S160独立推导 / Inferred

实际更常见的迁移模式The migration pattern that was more common

知识点：频率对比 · 分号后解释
Skills: Comparing frequencies · Explanations after semicolons
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

常见叙述称沙尘暴地区居民远迁加州；研究却发现更多人只在本地区迁移。选择转承表达。

Popular accounts emphasize Dust Bowl migration to California, but census research finds that relocation more commonly stayed within the region. Choose the transition.

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
B — more often,
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

独立推导：more often 明确对比远迁叙述与更频繁的区域内迁移，B。for this reason 错作因果，additionally 未表达频率纠偏，nevertheless 会重复反转而不说明更常见情况。

Independent derivation: More often specifies the more frequent alternative to the familiar long-distance story, B. For this reason invents causation, additionally misses the correction, and nevertheless does not express the frequency comparison.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

常见错因是看到 however 就再选转折词，而未理解空后正在给出更常发生的事实。

A common trap is repeating a contrast marker without identifying the frequency relationship.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

大众以为毕业生多去首都；数据发现他们更常留在本省。填空：“____，毕业生选择省内岗位。”

People assume graduates move to the capital; data show staying in the province is more common. Complete: ____, graduates choose jobs within their province.

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

More often。用频率词纠正对典型去向的印象。

More often. The phrase corrects the perceived typical destination using relative frequency.

S167独立推导 / Inferred

具体诗句确认前述概括A poetic example confirms a preceding generalization

知识点：确认与例证 · 隐喻关联
Skills: Confirmation and exemplification · Metaphorical connections
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

先说 Browning 的雕塑经历可能使其用触觉描绘抽象概念；后引他把思想写成可用手抹平的皱褶。选过渡语。

Browning's sculpting may have inspired tactile portrayals of abstractions; a quoted poem then describes smoothing a crumpled mind by hand. Choose the transition.

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
C — Indeed,
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

独立推导：后文为“抽象概念触觉化”的具体确认，Indeed 最准确。Even so 表转折；Likewise 暗示另一平行事例；Additionally 只加信息，不突出证实关系。

Independent derivation: The quotation directly substantiates the preceding claim about tactile abstraction, so Indeed fits best. Even so contrasts, Likewise suggests a parallel case, and Additionally merely adds information without signaling confirmation.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

常见错因是把所有新例子都用加法连接，忽略其强化证明作用。

A common trap is treating a substantiating example as mere additional information.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

填连接语：他的小说经常以食物写记忆。____，最新一章用柠檬味唤起童年。

Choose a transition: His fiction often evokes memory through food. ____, the latest chapter uses lemon flavor to recall childhood.

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

Indeed。具体例子确认前句概括。

Indeed. The specific example confirms the general observation.

S174独立推导 / Inferred

权威性与历史价值并非同一标准Authority and historical significance are distinct criteria

知识点：让步转折 · 证据与评价维度
Skills: Concession and contrast · Evidence and dimensions of evaluation
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

《Catherine》连载版常被视为不如书版权威，但引文强调十九世纪读者最初阅读并回应的是连载文本。选择承接句。

The serial Catherine is often considered less authoritative than the book, yet a quotation emphasizes that nineteenth-century readers first encountered and responded to serialized texts. Choose the connecting statement.

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
B — Still, the serial version matters for historical context;
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

独立推导：引文支持连载版的历史阅读情境价值，而非更忠于作者意图。B 用 Still 承认权威性较低但指出历史重要性，随后引文正好解释。A、C、D 与证据重心不符。

Independent derivation: The quotation establishes historical readership value, not greater fidelity to authorial intentions. B concedes lower authority while preserving importance for historical context, exactly what the quotation supports.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

常见错因是把历史重要性自动等同作者定稿权威，混淆不同评价维度。

A common trap is equating historical importance with textual authority or authorial intention.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

初稿不如定稿准确，但能显示作者如何修改。用让步句概括其价值。

A draft is less accurate than the final version but reveals the author's revisions. Summarize its value with a concession.

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

初稿虽不如定稿准确，仍对理解创作过程很重要。

Although less accurate than the final version, the draft remains important for understanding the writing process.

09

笔记综合Rhetorical synthesis

37 题组 / question groups
知识点总则 / Core principles

把任务动词和限定词圈出：介绍谁、向什么受众、突出相似还是差异、比较数量还是定义、写目的还是结果、是否必须引用。只使用完成任务所需笔记，真实性和相关性都要过关。

Identify the goal and qualifiers: whom to introduce, for which audience, similarity or contrast, counts or definitions, purpose or findings, quotation or paraphrase. A correct response must be both factual and goal-matched.

常见错因 / Common traps

真实但不切题；只完成and的一半；把背景当成就；有引号却不能支持论点；数字配错变量。

True but off-goal details; half a compound goal; background replacing achievement; irrelevant quotations; percentages attached to the wrong variable.

S009独立推导 / Inferred

海雀研究：总结要包含发现Auks: a summary includes the finding

知识点：研究总结 · 目的与结果
Skills: Summarizing a study · Purpose versus findings
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

海雀在更强风下更难成功着陆；任务是总结研究，而不只是介绍研究对象。

Auks' landing success decreases as wind intensifies. The task asks for a study summary.

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
D
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

D同时提供研究背景与核心发现：风越强，成功率越低。只有收集数据的方法、研究目的或单个成功率，都不如总体关系完整。

D combines study context with the central result: stronger wind, lower landing success. Method, purpose, or one isolated success rate is less complete.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

把‘研究了什么’当作‘研究发现了什么’。

Confusing the research question with the result.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

笔记：团队测试30枚种子；光照组发芽更多。用一句话总结研究。

Notes: a team tested 30 seeds; more seeds germinated in light. Write a one-sentence summary.

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

团队发现光照条件下种子发芽更多。

The study found greater seed germination under light.

练习出处 / Practice source: 本手册原创，基于 S009 的考点；不是官方原题。 / Created for this guide from the linked skill; not an official question.

S010独立推导 / Inferred

翼龙颌骨：研究加结论Pterosaur jawbones: study plus conclusion

知识点：双重任务 · 证据链
Skills: Two-part goals · Evidence chains
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

任务要求介绍研究及发现；较少生长线让研究者判断颌骨属于幼体。

The response must present both a jawbone study and its finding: few growth lines indicate juveniles.

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
D
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

D包含研究、观察和结论：与成体相比生长线少，因此属于幼体。A仅描述初始不确定性；B/C主要给研究对象或方法，遗漏最终发现。

D links the study, fewer growth lines, and the juvenile conclusion. The alternatives mainly give initial uncertainty, subject, or method rather than the finding.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

只完成‘介绍研究’这一半要求。

Satisfying only the study-description half of a two-part goal.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

笔记：分析树轮；样本只有5圈；判断为幼树。写出研究与发现。

Notes: rings analyzed; five rings found; specimen judged young. State study and finding.

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

研究者通过树轮分析，发现样本只有5圈，判断其为幼树。

Ring analysis found only five rings, indicating a young tree.

S011独立推导 / Inferred

Selvon：为陌生读者介绍作者作品Introducing Selvon and his novel

知识点：受众意识 · 作者与作品
Skills: Audience awareness · Author and work
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

向不了解Samuel Selvon的读者介绍其1956年小说The Lonely Londoners。

Introduce Samuel Selvon and The Lonely Londoners to readers unfamiliar with them.

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
A
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

介绍需把作者身份、作品名称与基本内容连接起来。A指出特立尼达作者、小说及战后加勒比移民内容，比仅说作品间人物复用更适合新读者。

An introduction connects author identity, title, and basic content. A does this; recurring characters across later works are secondary for a new audience.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

优先选择冷僻细节，而非读者建立理解所需的基础信息。

Favoring specialist details over essential introductory information.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

笔记：Mina是肯尼亚作家；River Home是移民小说。为新读者写介绍。

Notes: Mina is a Kenyan writer; River Home is a migration novel. Introduce both.

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

肯尼亚作家Mina的小说River Home探讨移民生活。

River Home, a novel by Kenyan writer Mina, explores migrant life.

S012独立推导 / Inferred

Paradise：作者与书的双重介绍Introducing Paradise and its author

知识点：受众意识 · 信息整合
Skills: Audience awareness · Integrating information
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

向不了解Gurnah及Paradise的读者介绍两者。

Introduce both Gurnah and Paradise to an unfamiliar audience.

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
C
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

C同时说明这是关于东非殖民时期的历史小说，并交代作者获2021年诺贝尔文学奖。作者生平不能替代小说内容；只有小说内容也未充分介绍作者。

C identifies the historical novel's colonial East African setting and its Nobel-winning author. Biography alone omits the book's substance; content alone underintroduces the author.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

把题目里的and看漏，只介绍作者或只介绍作品。

Missing the conjunction and and introducing only one target.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

要求同时介绍某画家和作品，应只写作品尺寸吗？写一个更合适的句式。

To introduce a painter and a work, is size alone enough? Supply a better sentence pattern.

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

不够。‘画家X的作品Y以Z为主题。’同时建立作者和作品身份。

No. 'Y, a work by painter X, explores Z' introduces both.

S023独立推导 / Inferred

虎鲸：论点和支持证据Orcas: a claim with support

知识点：论点加证据 · 数字修饰对象
Skills: A claim with support · What a number measures
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

笔记提出觅食时间减少99%可能使虎鲸刺激不足，增加与船只互动。

Notes link a 99% reduction in hunting time to understimulation and more vessel interactions.

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
A
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

A以较少觅食支持刺激不足这一行为解释。99%修饰觅食时间减少，不能改成金枪鱼数量增幅；声学阻吓装置仍待测试，不能声称已证实有效。

A supports the behavioral claim with reduced hunting time. The 99% concerns time, not tuna-population growth; an untested deterrent cannot be called proven effective.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

摘对数字却配错对象，或把待验证措施写成成功结论。

Attaching the right number to the wrong variable, or calling an untested measure effective.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

笔记：等待时间减少40%；满意度可能提高。能写‘满意度提高40%’吗？

Notes: waiting time fell 40%; satisfaction may improve. Can you write 'satisfaction rose 40%'?

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

不能；40%属于等待时间，且满意度仅为可能结果。

No. The percentage belongs to waiting time, and satisfaction is only a proposed effect.

S024独立推导 / Inferred

Forever Free：具体历史背景Forever Free: historical context

知识点：历史背景 · 作品与事件
Skills: Historical context · Works and historical events
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

任务是解释1867年雕塑与1863年《解放宣言》的关系。

Explain the 1867 sculpture's relationship to the 1863 Emancipation Proclamation.

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
C
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

C把作品与四年前的历史事件相连，直接完成historical context。作品造型、艺术家生卒年或‘自由的庆典’评价，不能替代具体事件关系。

C connects the work to the earlier historical event. Appearance, lifespan, or praise does not supply that specific context.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

把任何含年代或历史感的表述都当成历史背景。

Assuming any date or historically sounding description establishes context.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

某纪念碑建于1950年，纪念1945年停战。用一句话交代历史背景。

A monument built in 1950 commemorates a 1945 armistice. State its historical context.

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

该1950年纪念碑纪念五年前的停战。

The 1950 monument commemorates the armistice five years earlier.

数字档案：优势必须对应数字化A digital archive's specific advantage

Skills: Goal-based selection · Actions and their advantages
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

数字档案扩大查阅范围，同时不必把原件移离原属国家。

Digitization expands access without removing original documents from their countries.

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
B
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

B明确连接数字形式与可访问性、原件留存这两项优势。文献数量、内容或负责人虽然真实，却不解释being digital的好处。

B connects digital format to access and retention of originals. Counts, contents, or leadership may be true without explaining the digital advantage.

Online copies enable remote access without moving the originals.

S027独立推导 / Inferred

Tharpe：引用与支持缺一不可Tharpe: a quotation that supports the claim

知识点：直接引用 · 相关证据
Skills: Direct quotation · Relevant supporting evidence
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

要求使用引语支持Tharpe对摇滚乐发展的贡献。

Use a quotation to support Tharpe's contribution to rock and roll.

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
C
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

C引用她被称为该流派‘founding mother’，直接支持影响力。B虽表达影响却只是转述；D虽有引号，却只是歌声感染力，不直接证明摇滚贡献。

C quotes founding-mother language directly supporting genre influence. B paraphrases without quoting; D quotes emotional singing rather than rock's development.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

只检查有引号，或只检查观点相关，没有同时满足两项。

Checking only quotation form or only relevance, not both.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

要用引语证明某工程师的桥梁贡献，‘她非常友善’够吗？给出合适的原创引语。

To quote evidence of an engineer's bridge contribution, is 'She was kind' enough? Supply a suitable invented quote.

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

不够。原创示例：‘她设计的支架让桥梁承重翻倍。’直接对应工程贡献。

No. Invented example: 'Her supports doubled the bridge's load capacity.' It addresses the contribution.

S056独立推导 / Inferred

Janaki Ammal：突出个人成就Janaki Ammal: emphasizing an achievement

知识点：目标动词 · 成就与背景
Skills: Goal verbs · Achievements versus background
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

Ammal杂交进口甘蔗与本地草，成功培育适应印度气候的品种。

Ammal crossed imported cane with native grasses and produced climate-suited hybrids.

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
A
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

A同时凸显人物、关键方法和成功结果。研究所目标与聘用背景不是她的成就本身；被动句若省去人物，也弱化题目要强调的贡献。

A foregrounds the person, method, and success. Institutional motives or recruitment are background; omitting her agency weakens an achievement-focused response.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

用研究动机替代已经完成的成果。

Replacing an achieved result with the reason the project began.

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

Lin成功培育了耐盐水稻，而不只说研究所聘请了她。

State that Lin successfully bred salt-tolerant rice, not merely that she was hired.

S059独立推导 / Inferred

Catlett：共同主题而非不同造型Catlett: shared themes rather than differences

知识点：比较相同点 · 概括层级
Skills: Comparing similarities · Levels of generalization
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

两件雕塑人物面部细节不同，但姿势都象征支持性关系。

The sculptures differ in facial detail but both portray supportive relationships through their figures' poses.

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
B
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

题目要similarity，B把两件作品都纳入同一共同特征。只写一件、并列年代或强调面部差异，都未完成相同点任务。

B identifies one shared feature applying to both works. A single work, two dates, or a contrast in features does not satisfy a similarity goal.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

看到两件都出现就选，忽略两者之间到底是相同还是不同。

Selecting a sentence mentioning both works without checking whether it states similarity.

甲画与乙画色调不同，但都描绘互助。要求写相同点应选哪一事实？

Two paintings have different palettes but both depict mutual help. Which fact answers a similarity task?

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

都描绘互助；不同色调属于差异。

Their shared depiction of mutual help; palette differences answer a contrast task.

S060独立推导 / Inferred

帆船复制品：概括整体材料Dhow replicas: a qualified generalization

知识点：总体概括 · 限定词
Skills: Qualified generalizations · Qualifiers
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

复制帆船大多用传统材料，也使用少量现代材料。

Replica dhows use mostly traditional materials with some modern materials.

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
C
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

C保留most与some的总体比例关系。单说椰棕绳只是一种材料的实例；all traditional会与现代材料的存在冲突。

C captures the overall mostly/some pattern. Coconut-fiber rope is one example, not a generalization, and all traditional would erase the stated exceptions.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

用一个代表例子代替整体结论，或把mostly改成all。

Substituting one example for a generalization or replacing mostly with all.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

10种材料中8种传统、2种现代。用一句不绝对化的话概括。

Eight of ten materials are traditional; two are modern. Generalize without overstatement.

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

材料以传统为主，但也包含现代材料。

The materials are mostly traditional but include some modern ones.

Code Talkers：跨对象概括相似Code Talkers: a shared function

原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

S076独立推导 / Inferred

陨石：对比数量而非定义Meteorites: contrasting counts, not definitions

知识点：数量对比 · 任务词
Skills: Contrasting counts · Goal wording
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

meteorite falls约1200例，finds超过60000例；要求对比数量。

Meteorite falls number about 1,200; finds exceed 60,000. Contrast their numbers.

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
D
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

D同时给出两类数量并形成对照。定义两种类别虽相关，却没完成number这一限定；只给一个数字也不充分。

D presents both counts in contrast. Defining categories does not compare numbers, and one count alone is incomplete.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

只读contrast而漏掉the number of，答成概念定义差异。

Reading contrast but missing the qualifier the number of.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

A有20件，B有90件。若要求对比数量，只给A的定义够吗？

A has 20 items; B has 90. Is defining A sufficient for a numerical contrast?

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

不够；应写B有90件，而A只有20件。

No. State both: B has 90, whereas A has only 20.

植物标本：突出指定采集地A botanical sample: focus on a named site

原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

S078独立推导 / Inferred

信息分类：由标准推类别Classifying information by a stated rule

知识点：条件匹配 · 规则应用
Skills: Matching a rule to a case · Applying a rule
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

damage归Confidential，serious damage归Secret；常规外交通信是前者而非后者。

Damage maps to Confidential; serious damage maps to Secret. Routine correspondence fits damage but not serious damage.

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
C
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

C将案例与正确阈值连接，得出Confidential。只复述有分类制度，或说通信可能影响安全，都未给出要求的具体类别。

C applies the rule to the case and names Confidential. General statements about classification or harm omit the requested category.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

看到更强烈的敏感含义就选更高级类别，未遵守给定阈值。

Choosing a stronger category based on intuition instead of the stated threshold.

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

轻度。按给定标准归类，不自行提高等级。

Mild: apply the given threshold.

S080独立推导 / Inferred

Raymond's Run：只回答地点Raymond's Run: answering a setting goal

知识点：精确任务 · 地点与视角
Skills: The precise rhetorical goal · Setting versus point of view
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

问题只问故事发生在哪里，笔记给出Harlem。

The requested information is the story's location; the notes identify Harlem.

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
A
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

A直接写故事发生在Harlem。作者、出版书名和第一人称视角都属于不同信息维度，不能替代setting。

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

故事发生在Lagos。

The story takes place in Lagos.

S090独立推导 / Inferred

共享单车：同时比较两类缺点Bike sharing: comparing disadvantages

知识点：比较目标 · 双侧缺点
Skills: The requested comparison · Drawbacks of both alternatives
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

有桩模式成本和空间需求高，无桩模式易混乱；任务是比较缺点。

Docked systems require more resources; dockless systems can be disorganized. Compare disadvantages.

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
C
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

C同时表达有桩的资源负担与无桩的管理难题。A主要用无桩缺点对照有桩优点；B还把灵活性关系写反。应检查两侧内容是否都对应目标维度。

C covers resource intensity and organizational challenges. A mainly contrasts a disadvantage with an advantage; B reverses the flexibility facts.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

看见对比连接词就选，没检查比较维度是否是disadvantages。

Choosing any contrast without checking that both sides address disadvantages.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

甲车贵、乙车续航短。要求比较缺点，能写‘甲车贵，乙车轻便’吗？

Car A is costly; B has short range. For disadvantages, is 'A is costly, B is lightweight' sufficient?

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

不；应写甲成本高而乙续航短，覆盖两侧不足。

No. Compare A's cost with B's short range to cover both drawbacks.

S094独立推导 / Inferred

席位分配：反驳必须方向正确Seat allocation: refuting in the right direction

知识点：反驳 · 变量方向 · 人口与土地
Skills: Refutation · Direction of relationships · Population versus land area
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

分配公式考虑人口与土地，使地广人稀地区相对人口获得较多席位；反驳城市小区受益之说。

A population-and-land formula overrepresents sparsely populated large rural districts; refute the claim that small urban districts benefit.

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
D
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

D明确指出实际受益者和公式机制。A与B尽管可能包含反驳语气，却颠倒受益方向；C只定义malapportionment，不回应具体主张。

D identifies the actual beneficiaries and mechanism. Refuting language alone is insufficient if the distribution is reversed; a definition alone does not rebut the claim.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

被‘It's untrue’等反驳句式吸引，未核查后续事实方向。

Selecting refutation wording without checking whether the supporting facts are correct.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

补贴按土地面积计算，地广人少村落获益。反驳‘人口密集村落更获益’应写什么？

A subsidy based on land area favors large, sparse villages. How would you refute a claim favoring dense villages?

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

指出面积权重使地广人少村落获得相对更多补贴。

Explain that the land-area weighting gives relatively more to large, sparse villages.

S096独立推导 / Inferred

California：误解如何造成命名California: how a misconception shaped a name

知识点：因果综合 · 错误认知
Skills: Synthesizing cause and effect · Misconceptions
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

探险者把半岛误认作岛，因而借用小说中岛屿的名字California。

Explorers mistook a peninsula for an island and borrowed a fictional island's name.

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
D
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

D同时给出误解、真实地理和命名结果。只说小说有这个名字或地点如今在哪，无法解释misconception的作用；说他们当时知道是半岛还会消除误解。

D links the mistaken belief, actual geography, and naming result. The fictional name alone does not explain the misconception; saying they already recognized a peninsula removes it.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

知道结论地名就选，漏掉题目特别要求的误解机制。

Providing the name while omitting the requested role of mistaken belief.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

游客误把盐湖当海，称其‘小海’。要突出误解应保留哪些信息？

Visitors mistake a salt lake for a sea and name it Little Sea. What must a misconception-focused account include?

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

真实是湖、误认成海、因此得名三者的关系。

The actual lake, the mistaken sea belief, and the resulting name.

S108独立推导 / Inferred

土星环假说的事件顺序Sequencing a hypothesis about Saturn's rings

知识点：笔记整合 · 事件顺序 · 因果链
Skills: Synthesizing notes · Sequencing events · Causal chains
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

根据笔记重述假说：约1.6亿年前 Chrysalis 靠近土星，被潮汐力撕碎，碎片形成土星环。

Recount the proposed sequence: roughly 160 million years ago Chrysalis approached Saturn, broke apart under tidal forces, and supplied debris for the rings.

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
C — approach, tidal breakup, then ring formation
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

独立推导：C 依次覆盖靠近、破碎、成环，并保留正确因果。A 把环碎片说成破碎原因，倒置因果；D 叙述研究流程而非天体事件；B 仅给一般规律。

Independent derivation: C preserves all three events and their causal order. A reverses causation, D describes the research process rather than celestial events, and B provides only a general rule.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

常见错因是把“研究者如何研究”与“研究者认为发生了什么”混淆。

A common trap is confusing the sequence of research activities with the sequence proposed by the research.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

笔记：陨石撞击→尘埃遮光→气温下降。用一句话按顺序概述。

Notes: asteroid impact → dust blocks sunlight → temperatures fall. Summarize the sequence.

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

陨石撞击后扬起尘埃，遮挡阳光，随后气温下降。

The impact raised dust that blocked sunlight, after which temperatures fell.

S113独立推导 / Inferred

同时呈现研究与结论Presenting a study and its conclusion

知识点：写作目标双要求 · 谨慎表述结论
Skills: Meeting a two-part writing goal · Expressing conclusions cautiously
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

2013年分析泉护村约5300年前的猫骨，谷物摄入证据提示可能驯化。目标是介绍研究及结论。

A 2013 analysis of 5,300-year-old cat bones from Quanhucun found grain consumption suggesting possible domestication. Present the study and its conclusion.

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
B — analysis suggests domestication 5,300 years ago
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

独立推导：B 同时说明分析对象、地点、时间和“可能已驯化”的结论，且未把提示说成证明。A 仅方法；C 仅研究问题；D 仅发现骨片。

Independent derivation: B includes the analysis and its tentative domestication conclusion without overstating certainty. A gives only method, C the research topic, and D the discovery.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

常见错因是选择事实正确但未完成“研究＋结论”两个目标的句子。

A common trap is choosing a true statement that fulfills only one part of the writing goal.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

笔记：2024年测量古陶罐残留物，发现乳脂，提示装过奶。写一句研究加结论。

Notes: A 2024 analysis found dairy fat in ancient jars, suggesting milk storage. Write a study-plus-conclusion sentence.

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

2024年的陶罐残留物分析提示，这些罐子可能曾用于储存奶。

A 2024 analysis of jar residues suggests that the vessels may once have stored milk.

两本书的共同点A shared feature of two books

知识点：相似性整合 · 共同属性抽象
Skills: Synthesizing similarities · Generalizing shared features
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

Tan 的两部短篇集分别发生在普通郊区和普通城市，但都包含超现实事件；强调共同点。

Tan's two collections place surreal events in ordinary suburban and urban settings. Emphasize their similarity.

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
D — both depict surreal events in otherwise ordinary places
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

独立推导：D 将不同地点上位概括为“otherwise ordinary places”，准确提炼共同点。A、C 强调差异，B 仅列不同年份，不满足目标。

Independent derivation: D generalizes the settings as otherwise ordinary places and identifies their shared surreal events. A and C emphasize differences; B merely lists publication dates.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

常见错因是看到两本书都被提及就认为符合题意，而不看句子的强调方向。

A common trap is assuming that mentioning both books automatically emphasizes similarity.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

甲书讲普通学校中的魔法，乙书讲普通办公室中的魔法。写出共同点。

Book A depicts magic in ordinary schools; Book B depicts magic in ordinary offices. State their shared feature.

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

两书都将魔法事件置于日常环境中。

Both books place magical events in everyday settings.

Skills: Selecting relevant notes · Identifying a work and its publication details
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

Use notes on Vicuña's career to introduce her 1983 poetry collection.

C — Precario/Precarious, published in 1983 by Tanam Press

S128独立推导 / Inferred

媒体如何扩大人物影响How media expanded a historical figure's legacy

知识点：因果整合 · 前后对比
Skills: Synthesizing causal relationships · Before-and-after comparisons
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

黄飞鸿生前以医师和武术家在当地闻名；逾百部影视等作品使其成为国际民间英雄。强调媒体影响。

Wong Fei-hung was locally known as a physician and martial artist; depictions in over a hundred media works made him an international folk hero. Emphasize media's effect.

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
B — local recognition became international fame through media
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

独立推导：B 对比生前地方知名与后来的国际声誉，并明确媒体促成变化。A 错将地方知名归因于媒体；C 因果不明确；D 只谈表现内容。

Independent derivation: B contrasts local recognition with later international fame and explicitly attributes the change to media. A misattributes his original local reputation; C leaves the causal effect unclear; D describes portrayals only.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

常见错因是交换先后阶段，或只提到影视作品而未说明对声誉的作用。

Common traps are reversing stages or mentioning media without explaining its effect on reputation.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

网络视频使这位原本只在当地知名的歌手获得全球关注。

Online videos transformed the locally known singer into a globally recognized performer.

S134独立推导 / Inferred

发现的重要性与影响链A discovery's significance and downstream effects

知识点：强调意义 · 因果影响链
Skills: Emphasizing significance · Chains of causal effects
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

Ochoa 1955年发现 PNPase，虽最初假说有误，但该发现有助破解遗传密码，进而改善对遗传变异与健康关系的认识。强调意义。

Ochoa discovered PNPase in 1955. Although his initial hypothesis was wrong, the discovery helped decipher the genetic code and improve understanding of genetic variation and health. Emphasize significance.

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
A — PNPase helped decode the genetic code and understand health
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

独立推导：A 从发现连接到破解密码，再到健康认识，完整呈现重要影响。B、C 仅介绍事实；D 虽提重要性，却把重心转向错误假说。

Independent derivation: A traces the discovery's contribution to decoding the genetic code and understanding health. B and C provide background facts; D shifts emphasis toward the mistaken hypothesis.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

常见错因是误把有趣的错误细节当作题目要求的科学价值。

A common trap is emphasizing an interesting error instead of the requested scientific importance.

发现X使疫苗开发成为可能，进而降低死亡率。写一句强调X的意义。

Discovery X enabled vaccine development, which reduced mortality. Write a sentence emphasizing X's significance.

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

X为疫苗开发奠定基础，并因此帮助降低死亡率。

X laid the foundation for vaccine development and thereby helped reduce mortality.

S135独立推导 / Inferred

解释外观改变而非只描述差异Explaining an appearance change rather than merely noting it

知识点：变化原因 · 时间对比与机制
Skills: Causes of change · Temporal comparisons and mechanisms
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

古代多种文化称昴星团有七星，如今看起来六颗；两颗恒星移近到看似一颗。要求说明改变原因。

Ancient cultures described seven Pleiades stars; today six are apparent because two moved close enough to look like one. Explain why the appearance changed.

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
C — two stars moved together and now appear as one
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

独立推导：C 明确连接古代七星、后来两星靠近、如今视觉合并，既是变化又有原因。B 只说前后不同；D 描述当前原因但不如 C 明确呈现“移动导致改变”的时间机制。

Independent derivation: C connects the earlier seven-star appearance with the subsequent movement and visual merging. B states only the difference; D describes the present arrangement but does not explicitly explain the movement producing the change.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

常见错因是把“现在为什么看成六颗”与“外观为何发生改变”的完整时序解释混同。

A common trap is missing the temporal mechanism when asked why an appearance changed.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

过去两座山峰看似分开，如今视角变化使其重叠。写一句解释外观改变。

Two peaks once appeared separate; a changed viewing position now makes them overlap. Explain the change.

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

观察位置改变后，两座山峰在视线中重叠，因此原本分开的轮廓看似合为一体。

After the viewing position changed, the peaks aligned in the line of sight, making their formerly separate outlines appear merged.

S137独立推导 / Inferred

苹果品种减少及其原因Declining apple diversity and its cause

知识点：双重写作目标 · 因果与具体对象
Skills: Meeting a two-part writing goal · Causation tied to a specific subject
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

美国曾种逾14000种苹果；工业农业偏向商业适宜品种，数千种消失。要求强调品种下降并说明原因。

The US once grew over 14,000 apple varieties; industrial agriculture favored commercially suitable kinds and thousands disappeared. Emphasize the decline and explain its cause.

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
C — industrial farming favored a few varieties, eliminating thousands
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

独立推导：C 同时锁定苹果、品种丧失与工业农业选择，完成两个目标。B 有下降无原因；D 有农业变化但未明确苹果；A 重心是找回项目。15种占销售90%不等于如今只存在15种。

Independent derivation: C explicitly links lost apple varieties to industrial agriculture's selection, meeting both goals. B lacks the cause, D lacks apple-specific decline, and A focuses on recovery. Fifteen varieties dominating sales does not mean only fifteen exist.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

常见错因是漏掉一个目标，或把市场主导数量误当存世总数。

Common traps are satisfying only one goal and equating market dominance with total surviving diversity.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

某地原种50种稻，机械化后偏种5种，其他逐渐消失。写一句下降加原因。

A region once grew 50 rice varieties; mechanization favored five and others disappeared. State the decline and its cause.

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

机械化偏好少数适宜品种，导致当地稻种多样性下降。

Mechanization favored a few suitable varieties, causing a decline in local rice diversity.

S146独立推导 / Inferred

比较两种心智模块论的范围Comparing the scope of two modularity theories

知识点：比较范围 · 部分与全部
Skills: Comparing scope · Some versus all
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

Fodor 认为低级认知系统有模块性，高级系统没有；Carruthers 的 MMH 将所有认知系统都纳入。要求比较。

Fodor treats low-level but not high-level cognitive systems as modular; Carruthers's MMH includes all cognitive systems. Compare the theories.

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
A — Fodor's some-systems account is narrower than MMH
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

独立推导：A 用 some but not all 与更广泛的 MMH 对照，准确呈现范围差异。C 错说低级系统存在分歧；D 错说 Carruthers 否认共同的模块基本观念；B 更偏时间顺序。

Independent derivation: A accurately contrasts some but not all systems with MMH's broader scope. C invents disagreement about low-level systems; D misstates their shared premise; B emphasizes chronology rather than the explicit comparison.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

常见错因是把扩大理论适用范围误读成否定原理论全部内容。

A common trap is mistaking an expansion of scope for total rejection of the earlier theory.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

理论甲只适用于视觉，乙适用于所有感觉。用一句话比较。

Theory A applies only to vision; Theory B applies to all senses. Compare their scope.

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

乙范围更广；甲只覆盖乙所包括的一个感觉领域。

B has broader scope; A covers only one sensory domain included in B.

S149独立推导 / Inferred

区分研究方法与发现Distinguishing methods from findings

知识点：方法筛选 · 研究程序
Skills: Selecting methodological information · Research procedures
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

研究者用射频标签记录138只雌林鸭访问巢箱的次数；笔记还列有统计结果。要求介绍方法。

Researchers used radio-frequency tags to record nest-box visits by 138 female wood ducks; notes also list statistical findings. Present the methods.

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
C — tag 138 female ducks and record nest-box visits
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

独立推导：C 说明样本、工具与记录对象，是操作程序。A、B 重点是发现；D 编造检查巢址痕迹的方法，与笔记标签追踪不同。

Independent derivation: C states the sample, tracking tool, and measured behavior, making it methodological. A and B emphasize results; D invents a sign-inspection procedure instead of the recorded tagging approach.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

常见错因是被百分比吸引，选成结果总结而非研究如何进行。

A common trap is selecting numerical findings when the goal is how the study was conducted.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

笔记：给20只蝙蝠装GPS；记录路线；发现飞行均距3公里。写一句只介绍方法。

Notes: Fit 20 bats with GPS; record routes; find a mean flight distance of 3 km. Write a methods sentence.

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

研究者给20只蝙蝠装上GPS，并记录其飞行路线。

Researchers fitted 20 bats with GPS devices and recorded their flight routes.

S162独立推导 / Inferred

两种诗歌韵律的准确对比Accurately contrasting two poetic meters

知识点：特征映射 · 比较对象一致
Skills: Matching features to their subjects · Comparing like with like
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

Widsith 使用头韵格，靠重复声音；Meghadūta 使用音量格，靠长短音节。要求强调韵律差异。

Widsith uses alliterative meter based on repeated sounds; Meghadūta uses quantitative meter based on long and short syllables. Emphasize their metrical difference.

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
D — Meghadūta uses syllable lengths; Widsith uses repeated sounds
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

独立推导：D 将两种韵律特征正确对应到两首诗。A 只比语言；B 把韵律总定义当成音量格定义；C 把长短音节错误对应 Widsith。

Independent derivation: D maps each metrical feature to the correct poem. A contrasts languages, B substitutes the generic definition of meter for quantitative meter, and C wrongly assigns syllable length to Widsith.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

常见错因是记住两个术语却交换定义，或比较了题目未要求的语言。

Common traps are swapping definitions and comparing language instead of meter.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

诗甲靠重复开头辅音组织节奏，诗乙靠音节长短。用一句话对比。

Poem A organizes rhythm through repeated initial consonants; Poem B through syllable lengths. Compare them.

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

甲以声音重复构成头韵格，乙以长短音节构成音量格。

A uses alliterative patterns of repeated sounds, whereas B uses quantitative patterns of syllable length.

S163独立推导 / Inferred

把政治立场变化与文件写作相连Connecting changing political beliefs to a declaration

知识点：历史时序 · 变化背景 · 准确归因
Skills: Historical chronology · Context for changes · Accurate attribution
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

Valle 长期反独立，1820年起义后改变态度，随后撰写1821年中美洲独立宣言。要求将宣言放入其观念变化背景。

Valle long opposed independence, changed his mind after an 1820 revolt, and wrote the 1821 Central American declaration. Situate the declaration within his changing beliefs.

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
B — Valle changed after the 1820 revolt and wrote the 1821 declaration
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

独立推导：B 同时包含旧立场、1820年转变及1821年写作，满足背景目标。A 无宣言联系；C 错将转变定在1814年；D 加入无法证实的反事实推断。

Independent derivation: B includes the earlier stance, the 1820 change, and the 1821 declaration. A omits the declaration, C misdates the change to 1814, and D adds an unsupported counterfactual.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

常见错因是将相关历史事件的年份混淆，或把“之后改变”夸大为“没有它绝不可能”。

Common traps are confusing dates and strengthening a documented sequence into an unproved counterfactual necessity.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

某人原反对扩建，2020年调查后转而支持，2021年起草提案。怎样概括最准确？

A person opposed expansion, supported it after a 2020 survey, and drafted a proposal in 2021. Summarize accurately.

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

原本反对扩建的他在2020年调查后改变态度，并于2021年起草提案。

Previously opposed to expansion, he changed his view after the 2020 survey and drafted the proposal in 2021.

S165独立推导 / Inferred

叠压顺序支持岩画风格分类Rock-art classification using superposition and form

知识点：主张与证据 · 叠压年代 · 特征差异
Skills: Claims and evidence · Dating through superposition · Differences in features
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

LNF 是简单侧身动物轮廓，叠于 Gwion 人形画之上、Wanjina 之下；要求提出并支持风格判断。

LNF depicts simple, mainly profile animal outlines and overlies Gwion human figures while underlying Wanjina. Make and support a stylistic claim.

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
C — LNF merits a distinct style because animal forms depart from the preceding period
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

独立推导：叠压使 LNF 晚于 Gwion；从其人形转向动物形象是类别差异，可支持 LNF 独立风格，C。A 错放在 Gwion 之前；B 仅复述层位；D 说细节增多，与简单解剖特征不符。

Independent derivation: Superposition places LNF after Gwion, and its animal subjects contrast categorically with Gwion's human figures, supporting a distinct style, C. A misorders LNF, B merely reports layering, and D contradicts the simple anatomical detail.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

常见错因是颠倒上下层年代，或将简单风格误读为越来越精细。

Common traps are reversing superposition chronology and reading simplified forms as increased detail.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

壁画乙画动物，覆盖画人的甲，又被丙覆盖。乙与甲的风格分类可用什么证据支持？

Animal mural B overlies human mural A and is overlain by C. What supports distinguishing B from A?

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

乙晚于甲且主体从人转为动物；年代层位和题材差异共同支持区分。

B is later than A and changes the subject from humans to animals; chronology and subject matter support the distinction.

S170独立推导 / Inferred

针对地点的笔记筛选Selecting notes for a location question

知识点：任务关键词 · 物种与地点映射
Skills: Task keywords · Matching species to locations
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

Catagonus wagneri 于1974年在阿根廷发现活体；另一树种于1978年在塞舌尔发现。要求说明前者发现地点。

A living Catagonus wagneri was identified in Argentina in 1974; another tree species was found in the Seychelles in 1978. Specify the former's location.

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
B — a living Catagonus wagneri was identified in Argentina
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

独立推导：B 同时包含指定物种和 Argentina，直接回答哪里。A 是另一物种；C 仅分类；D 仅年份，无法满足地点目标。

Independent derivation: B identifies the requested species and Argentina, directly answering where. A concerns the other species, C provides classification, and D gives only the date.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

常见错因是选择同一物种但只有年份的句子，忽视问题问哪里而非何时。

A common trap is selecting the right species but a date-only statement when the task asks where.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

Notes: Fish X was found in Chile in 1995; Fish Y in Peru in 2000. State where X was found.

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

鱼X在智利发现。地点 Chile 必须出现，年份可省略。

Fish X was found in Chile. The location is required; the year is optional.

S172独立推导 / Inferred

区分两种现在主义解答Distinguishing two presentist solutions

知识点：理论对比 · 共享前提与分歧
Skills: Comparing theories · Shared premises and points of disagreement
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

Bigelow 用世界具有“曾有恐龙”这类过去时属性解释过去陈述为真；Bourne 用时间之外代表过去的抽象时间。要求区分。

Bigelow grounds true past statements in past-tensed properties; Bourne uses abstract ersatz time representing the past. Distinguish their approaches.

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
C — ersatz time differs from past-tensed properties
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

独立推导：C 将过去时属性与抽象时间这一具体机制对照。双方都试图提供真值根据，也都是现在主义者；A、D 虚构基本立场分歧，B 把不同机制误说成在同一机制上加条件。

Independent derivation: C contrasts the actual mechanisms: past-tensed properties versus abstract ersatz time. Both provide truth makers and share presentism; A and D invent foundational disagreements, while B incorrectly combines their mechanisms.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

常见错因是看到理论不同就认为基本立场对立，忽视共同前提下的不同解决方式。

A common trap is assuming differing theories reject each other's shared premise rather than offering different solutions.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

甲乙都认为记忆可存储；甲说化学方式，乙说电信号方式。准确分歧是什么？

A and B both think memories can be stored; A proposes chemical storage, B electrical signals. What is the precise disagreement?

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

存储机制不同，而非是否能存储记忆。

They disagree about the storage mechanism, not whether memory can be stored.

S173独立推导 / Inferred

理论解释与研究发现的连接Connecting an explanatory argument with an empirical finding

知识点：研究与解释对接 · 避免无关相关性
Skills: Connecting findings with explanations · Avoiding irrelevant correlations
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

研究发现切分节奏更受喜爱；Hein 说意料外重音带来乐趣。要求连接一项发现与其论点。

A study finds syncopated rhythms more enjoyable; Hein argues that unexpected accents create fun. Connect a finding to his argument.

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
B — Hein offers a reason for greater enjoyment of syncopation
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

独立推导：B 将“非切分容易无聊”的解释与“更喜欢切分节奏”的组间发现对应。A 的不相关指标不能证明无聊；C 只列测量项目；D 的幸福与享受相关不如 B 直接对接节奏差异的机制。

Independent derivation: B uses Hein's boredom argument to explain the observed preference for syncopation. A relies on irrelevant noncorrelations, C lists measured variables rather than a result, and D's happiness-enjoyment correlation is less directly tied to the proposed rhythmic mechanism.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

常见错因是只看共有关键词“幸福、乐趣”，而没有连接具体研究结果与解释逻辑。

A common trap is matching words such as happiness and fun without matching the finding's explanatory logic.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

研究发现意外结局更受欢迎；评论家说可预测结局令人无聊。怎样连接？

A study finds unexpected endings more popular; a critic says predictable endings are boring. Connect the two.

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

评论家的观点为研究中人们更喜欢意外结局提供了可能解释。

The critic's argument offers a possible explanation for the observed preference for unexpected endings.

S182独立推导 / Inferred

相同遗物不代表相同功能Shared artifacts do not imply identical site functions

知识点：反驳主张 · 比较证据 · 谨慎推断
Skills: Challenging a claim · Comparative evidence · Cautious inference
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

Ortiz 有贝壳、海洋动物残骸和多种石器，推测居住用；另一遗址主要是贝壳堆，推测采集用。要求挑战两地功能相同。

Ortiz contains shells, marine remains, and varied stone artifacts, suggesting habitation; another site mainly has shell midden, suggesting resource collection. Challenge identical functions.

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
B — Ortiz appears to have been more than a collecting site
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

独立推导：B 承认共有贝壳，但指出 Ortiz 不只是采集地点，利用差异挑战相同功能。A 反而支持相同；C 编造未来反证；D 加入只单一功能的过度断言。

Independent derivation: B acknowledges shared shells while using Ortiz's broader evidence to challenge identical function. A supports similarity, C invents future refutation, and D overclaims an exclusively single function.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

常见错因是将一个共同特征当成整体功能相同，或把推测居住升级成只有居住。

Common traps are inferring identical functions from one shared feature and turning probable habitation into exclusive use.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

甲房间有锅、床和衣柜；乙只有锅。能因都有锅就说用途完全一样吗？

Room A has cookware, a bed, and a wardrobe; Room B has only cookware. Does shared cookware establish identical use?

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

不能。甲的其他物品提示更广泛的居住功能，可挑战用途完全相同。

No. A's additional objects suggest broader residential use, challenging identical functions.

S183独立推导 / Inferred

用具体作品例示体裁Illustrating a genre with a specific work

知识点：定义与实例 · 目标句必要成分
Skills: Definitions and examples · Required elements of a goal-focused sentence
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

书信体小说以虚构文书组成；Absolutely Normal Chaos 主要是学生暑假日记。要求给书信体小说例子。

Epistolary novels consist of fictional documents; Absolutely Normal Chaos mainly contains a student's summer journal entries. Provide an example of the genre.

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
A — Absolutely Normal Chaos is epistolary because it consists of journal entries
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

独立推导：A 给出作品名、体裁和日记结构这一符合定义的依据。B 未明确指出作为体裁实例；C 只有定义没有作品；D 只有出版事实。

Independent derivation: A names the work, identifies the genre, and supplies the journal structure that qualifies it. B omits the explicit genre connection, C gives a definition without an example, and D gives publication information only.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

常见错因是把定义当例子，或只列作品名却未说明它为何属于该类。

A common trap is substituting a definition for an example or naming a work without connecting it to the category.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

寓言以故事寓意传达教训；原创故事《狐狸与灯》用动物故事教人节约。写一句例示寓言。

A fable conveys a lesson through a story; the invented Fox and Lamp teaches thrift through animals. Write an example sentence.

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

《狐狸与灯》是寓言，因为它通过动物故事传达节约的教训。

Fox and Lamp is a fable because its animal story conveys a lesson about thrift.

10

数学建模与计算Mathematical reasoning

1 题组 / question groups
知识点总则 / Core principles

先定义变量及单位，再把题目要问的量代入计算。指数模型A·b^t中A是t=0的值，b是每期倍率：b=1+r代表增长，b=1−r代表衰减。区分百分率、固定增量、初始值；其他数学技能见对应题卡中的具体步骤。

Define variables and units before calculating. In A·b^t, A is the value at t=0 and b is the per-period multiplier. Distinguish growth rates from constant increments and initial values; each math card supplies the specific rule it needs.

常见错因 / Common traps

系数与增长率混淆；百分数未除以100；指数增长当线性增加；答案没带含义或单位。

Coefficients confused with rates; percentages not converted; exponential changes treated as constant increments; missing interpretation or units.

S041独立推导 / Inferred

指数模型：初始值与增长率An exponential model's initial value

知识点：指数初值 · 增长因子 · 百分率
Skills: Initial values in exponential models · Growth factors · Percentage rates
原题例题 · 精简转述 / Uploaded example · paraphrased

P(t)=1800(1.02)^t，t为研究开始后的年数，解释P(0)=1800。

P(t)=1800(1.02)^t, with t years since the study began. Interpret P(0)=1800.

查看答案与知识点讲解 / Answer & explanation
B — 1,800 at the start
决定性证据与规则 / Evidence & rule

代入t=0，(1.02)^0=1，故初始估计数量为1800。1.02=1+0.02，表示每年增长2%，而不是每年增加1.02或1800；第一年增加36，之后增加量随基数变化。

At t=0, the exponential factor equals 1, giving an initial estimate of 1,800. A factor of 1.02 means 2% annual growth, not a constant numerical increase; the first increase is 36.

可能错因 · 非个人诊断 / Possible trap · not a diagnosis

把前面的系数当每年增量，或把1.02当102%的增长率。

Treating the coefficient as an annual increment or the factor as a 102% growth rate.

原创迁移例题 / Original transfer practice

N(t)=500(0.96)^t。求初始值及每期变化率。

N(t)=500(0.96)^t. Find the initial value and percentage change per period.

练习答案与解释 / Practice solution

N(0)=500；0.96=1−0.04，每期减少4%。

N(0)=500; 0.96=1−0.04 means a 4% decrease per period.
